    <div class="modal-field"><label>Password</label><input type="password" id="signup-pass" placeholder="Min 8 characters" autocomplete="new-password" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter')doSignup()"></div>
    <button class="modal-btn" onclick="doSignup()">Create Account →</button>
    <div class="modal-switch">Already have an account? <a onclick="openLoginModal()">Sign in</a></div>
  </div>

<script>
// ═══════════════════════════════════════
// HELPERS

const q = s => { try { return document.querySelector(s); } catch(e) { return null; } };
const fz = b => b<1024?b+'B':b<1048576?(b/1024).toFixed(1)+'KB':(b/1048576).toFixed(2)+'MB';
const f2 = (n,c='') => c+(Math.round(n*100)/100).toLocaleString('en',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2});
const readBuf = f => new Promise((res,rej) => { const r=new FileReader(); r.onload=e=>res(e.target.result); r.onerror=rej; r.readAsArrayBuffer(f); });
const canBlob = (c,m,q2) => new Promise(r=>c.toBlob(r,m,q2));
const blobU8 = async b => new Uint8Array(await b.arrayBuffer());
const esc = s => s.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');

function setStatus(id,type,msg){
  const el=document.getElementById('st-'+id); if(!el)return;
  el.className='status show '+type;
  document.getElementById('stxt-'+id).textContent=msg;
}
function clearStatus(id){ const el=document.getElementById('st-'+id); if(el)el.className='status'; }

function makeDL(data,name,mime){
  const blob=new Blob([data],{type:mime});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url; a.download=name; a.className='dl-btn';
  a.innerHTML='<span style="font-size:16px">⬇</span> '+name;
  return a;
}
function addDL(aId,data,name,mime){
  const el=document.getElementById(aId);
  if(!el) return;
  const blob=new Blob([data],{type:mime});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url; a.download=name; a.className='dl-btn';
  a.innerHTML='<span style="font-size:16px">⬇</span> '+name;
  el.appendChild(a);
  // Save to downloads history if user is logged in
  if(typeof currentUser !== 'undefined' && currentUser){
    saveDownloadRecord(name,mime,blob.size,url);
  }
}

function saveDownloadRecord(name,mime,size,url){
  try {
    const key='ts_downloads_'+((currentUser&&currentUser.email)||'guest');
    const icon = mime.includes('pdf')? '📄' : mime.includes('image')? '🖼' : mime.includes('zip')? '📦' : '📁';
    // Convert blob URL to base64 so the file persists across sessions after sign-in
    fetch(url).then(r=>r.blob()).then(blob=>{
      const reader=new FileReader();
      reader.onloadend=()=>{
        const b64=reader.result; // full data URI: data:mime;base64,...
        const entry={name,mime,size,b64,icon,ts:Date.now(),date:new Date().toLocaleString()};
        const latest=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');
        latest.unshift(entry);
        try{ localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(latest.slice(0,40))); }catch(quota){
          // Storage full — store metadata only without binary data
          latest[0].b64=null;
          try{ localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(latest.slice(0,40))); }catch(e2){}
        }
        if(document.getElementById('p-my-downloads').classList.contains('active')) renderDownloadsList();
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
    }).catch(()=>{
      // Fallback if fetch fails — store metadata without data
      const stored=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');
      stored.unshift({name,mime,size,b64:null,icon,ts:Date.now(),date:new Date().toLocaleString()});
      try{ localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(stored.slice(0,40))); }catch(e){}
      if(document.getElementById('p-my-downloads').classList.contains('active')) renderDownloadsList();
    });
  } catch(e) {}
}

function renderDownloadsList(){
  const dlList = document.getElementById('downloads-list-el');
  if(!dlList) return;
  if(!currentUser){ dlList.innerHTML=''; return; }
  const key='ts_downloads_'+(currentUser.email||'guest');
  const items=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');
  if(!items.length){
    dlList.innerHTML='<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-state-icon">📂</div><div class="empty-state-text">No downloads yet</div><div class="empty-state-sub">Use any tool to process a file — it will appear here.</div></div>';
    return;
  }
  dlList.innerHTML=items.map(function(it,i){
    const dlLink = it.b64
      ? `<a href="${it.b64}" download="${esc(it.name)}" class="dl-btn" style="padding:8px 16px;font-size:12px;flex-shrink:0">⬇ Download</a>`
      : `<span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink5);font-family:var(--font-mono)" title="File data not stored — re-process to download again">⚠ Re-process to download</span>`;
    return '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--border-subtle);border-radius:var(--r10);padding:12px 16px;transition:all 0.15s"><div style="font-size:22px;flex-shrink:0">'+it.icon+'</div><div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><div style="font-weight:600;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink);overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">'+esc(it.name)+'</div><div style="font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:9px;color:var(--ink4);margin-top:2px">'+fz(it.size)+' &nbsp;·&nbsp; '+it.date+'</div></div>'+dlLink+'<button onclick="removeDownloadRecord('+i+')" style="background:rgba(248,113,113,0.1);border:1px solid rgba(248,113,113,0.2);color:var(--red);border-radius:var(--r6);width:28px;height:28px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;flex-shrink:0">✕</button></div>';
  }).join('');
}

function removeDownloadRecord(idx){
  if(!currentUser) return;
  const key='ts_downloads_'+(currentUser.email||'guest');
  const items=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');
  items.splice(idx,1);
  localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(items));
  renderDownloadsList();
}

function clearAllDownloads(){
  if(!currentUser) return;
  if(!confirm('Clear all downloads from history?')) return;
  localStorage.removeItem('ts_downloads_'+(currentUser.email||'guest'));
  renderDownloadsList();
}

function goTo(id, navEl) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.sb-item').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('active'));
  const p = document.getElementById('p-'+id); if(p) p.classList.add('active');
  if(navEl) navEl.classList.add('active');
  // Track recent tools
  if(typeof addRecent === 'function') addRecent(id);
  // Update topbar breadcrumb
  const nm = (typeof TOOL_NAMES !== 'undefined' && TOOL_NAMES[id]) || id.replace(/-/g,' ').replace(/\w/g,c=>c.toUpperCase());
  const ttl = document.getElementById('tb-title'); if(ttl) ttl.textContent = nm;
  const tpath = document.getElementById('tb-path'); if(tpath) { const cat = id.startsWith('pdf')?'PDF Tools':id.startsWith('img-social')?'Social Media':id.startsWith('img')?'Image Tools':id.startsWith('calc')?'Calculators':id.startsWith('unit')?'Unit Converters':id.startsWith('txt')?'Text Tools':'Overview'; tpath.innerHTML = `toolsuite / <span>${cat.toLowerCase()}</span>`; }
  document.getElementById('tb-title').textContent = titleMap[id]||id;
  document.getElementById('tb-path').textContent = 'toolsuite / '+id.replace(/-/g,' ');
  window.scrollTo(0,0);
  // Lazy init
  if(id.startsWith('unit-')) buildUnitConverter(id.replace('unit-',''));
  if(id==='calc-salary') calcSalary();
  if(id==='calc-emi') calcEMI();
  if(id==='calc-gpa') { initGPARows(); calcGPA(); }
  if(id==='calc-gpa-convert') calcGPAConvert();
  if(id==='calc-bmi') calcBMI();
  if(id==='calc-age') { document.getElementById('age-ref').value=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10); if(!document.getElementById('age-dob').value) document.getElementById('age-dob').value='1995-01-15'; calcAge(); }
  if(id==='calc-compound') calcCI();
  if(id==='calc-tax') calcTax();
  if(id==='calc-tip') calcTip();
  if(id==='account') updateAuthUI();
  if(id==='my-downloads') { updateAuthUI(); renderDownloadsList(); }
}

function filterSidebar(q2) {
  q2=q2.toLowerCase();
  document.querySelectorAll('.sb-item').forEach(el=>{
    const kw=(el.textContent+' '+(el.dataset.kw||'')).toLowerCase();
    el.style.display=kw.includes(q2)?'':'none';
  });
}

// Drag and drop setup
document.querySelectorAll('.dz').forEach(dz=>{
  dz.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{e.preventDefault();dz.classList.add('over');});
  dz.addEventListener('dragleave',()=>dz.classList.remove('over'));
  dz.addEventListener('drop',e=>{
    e.preventDefault();dz.classList.remove('over');
    const inp=dz.querySelector('input[type=file]');if(!inp)return;
    const files=e.dataTransfer.files;
    const m=inp.id.match(/^fi-(.+)$/);if(!m)return;
    const key=m[1];
    if(inp.id==='fi-edit'){loadPDFEditor(files);return;}
    if(inp.id==='fi-rotate'){loadRotatePreview(files);return;}
    if(inp.id==='fi-reorder'){loadReorderPreview(files);return;}
    if(inp.id==='fi-delp'){loadDeletePreview(files);return;}
    if(inp.id==='fi-w2p'){loadWordToPDF(files);return;}
    if(inp.id==='fi-split'){loadSplitPreview(files);return;}
    if(inp.id==='fi-merge'){pdfAdd('merge',files);loadMergePreview();return;}
    if(inp.id==='fi-wmk'){loadWatermarkPreview(files);return;}
    const genericPreviewKeys=['compress','pgn','prot','unlk','repair','p2i','p2w','p2t'];
    const keyMatch=inp.id.match(/^fi-(.+)$/);
    if(keyMatch && genericPreviewKeys.includes(keyMatch[1])){loadGenericPreview(keyMatch[1],files);return;}
    if(inp.multiple) pdfAdd(key,files)||imgAdd(key,files);
    else { pdfSingle(key,files); imgSingle(key,files); }
  });
});

// PDF STATE

const PF = {}; // pdfFiles by key

function pdfAdd(key,files) {
  if(!PF[key]) PF[key]=[];
  Array.from(files).forEach(f=>{ if(f.type==='application/pdf'&&!PF[key].find(x=>x.name===f.name)) PF[key].push(f); });
  renderPDFList(key);
  // Show add-more indicator
  if(key==='merge') {
    const more=document.getElementById('dz-merge-more');
    const cnt=document.getElementById('dz-merge-count');
    if(more && PF[key].length>0) { more.style.display='flex'; if(cnt) cnt.textContent=PF[key].length+' file'+(PF[key].length!==1?'s':'')+' added'; }
    loadMergePreview();
  }
  return true;
}
function pdfSingle(key,files) {
  if(!files||!files.length) return;
  PF[key]=[files[0]];
  renderPDFList(key);
}
function renderPDFList(key){
  const c=document.getElementById('fl-'+key); if(!c)return;
  c.innerHTML='';
  (PF[key]||[]).forEach((f,i)=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='fchip';
    d.innerHTML=`<span class="fchip-icon">📄</span><span class="fchip-name">${f.name}</span><span class="fchip-size">${fz(f.size)}</span><button class="fchip-rm" onclick="pdfRm('${key}',${i})">×</button>`;
    c.appendChild(d);
  });
  const btn=document.getElementById('btn-'+key);
  if(btn) btn.disabled=!(PF[key]&&PF[key].length>0);
  if(key==='merge'&&btn) btn.disabled=!(PF[key]&&PF[key].length>=2);
  clearStatus(key);
}
function pdfRm(key,i){ PF[key].splice(i,1); renderPDFList(key); }
function pdfClear(key){ PF[key]=[]; renderPDFList(key); const d=document.getElementById('dl-'+key);if(d)d.innerHTML=''; }

// ─── SHARED PAGE PREVIEW HELPER ───
// Tracks deleted pages per grid: gridId -> Set of original page indices (0-based)
const _gridDeletedPages = {};

function _getGridPageOrder(gridId) {
  const grid = document.getElementById(gridId);
  if(!grid) return [];
  return Array.from(grid.querySelectorAll('.pdf-preview-page:not(.page-deleted)'))
    .map(el => parseInt(el.dataset.idx));
}

function _getGridDeletedSet(gridId) {
  return _gridDeletedPages[gridId] || new Set();
}

async function renderPagePreviewGrid(gridId, pdfJSDoc, options={}) {
  const grid = document.getElementById(gridId);
  if(!grid) return;
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  _gridDeletedPages[gridId] = new Set();
  const scale = options.scale || 0.4;
  const totalPages = pdfJSDoc.numPages;

  // Instructions bar
  const instrBar = document.createElement('div');
  instrBar.className = 'pdf-preview-instructions';
  instrBar.innerHTML = `<span>⠿ drag to reorder</span><span>🗑 hover & click ✕ to delete</span><span id="${gridId}-del-count" style="color:var(--red);display:none"></span>`;
  grid.parentElement.insertBefore(instrBar, grid);

  for(let i=1; i<=totalPages; i++){
    const page = await pdfJSDoc.getPage(i);
    const vp = page.getViewport({scale});
    const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
    wrapper.className = 'pdf-preview-page';
    wrapper.dataset.page = i;
    wrapper.dataset.idx = i-1;
    wrapper.draggable = true;
    wrapper.title = 'Drag to reorder';

    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = vp.width; canvas.height = vp.height;
    await page.render({canvasContext:canvas.getContext('2d'), viewport:vp}).promise;

    // Delete button overlay
    const actions = document.createElement('div');
    actions.className = 'pdf-preview-page-actions';
    const delBtn = document.createElement('button');
    delBtn.className = 'pdf-page-action-btn pdf-page-action-del';
    delBtn.title = 'Delete page'; delBtn.textContent = '✕';
    delBtn.onclick = (e) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      const isDeleted = wrapper.classList.contains('page-deleted');
      if(isDeleted){
        wrapper.classList.remove('page-deleted');
        delBtn.textContent = '✕'; delBtn.title = 'Delete page';
        delBtn.className = 'pdf-page-action-btn pdf-page-action-del';
        _gridDeletedPages[gridId].delete(i-1);
      } else {
        wrapper.classList.add('page-deleted');
        delBtn.textContent = '↩'; delBtn.title = 'Restore page';
        delBtn.className = 'pdf-page-action-btn pdf-page-action-del';
        delBtn.style.background = 'rgba(163,255,79,0.7)'; delBtn.style.color = '#0d0b1a';
        _gridDeletedPages[gridId].add(i-1);
      }
      // Update deleted count display
      const countEl = document.getElementById(gridId+'-del-count');
      if(countEl){
        const n = _gridDeletedPages[gridId].size;
        countEl.style.display = n>0 ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
        countEl.textContent = n+' page'+(n!==1?'s':'')+' marked for deletion';
      }
    };
    actions.appendChild(delBtn);

    const label = document.createElement('div');
    label.className = 'pdf-preview-page-label';
    const pageLabel = options.pageLabel ? options.pageLabel(i) : 'p.'+i;
    label.innerHTML = `<span>${pageLabel}</span><span style="opacity:0.5;font-size:7.5px">⠿</span>`;

    wrapper.appendChild(canvas);
    wrapper.appendChild(actions);
    wrapper.appendChild(label);

    // Drag-to-reorder events
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragstart', e => {
      e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', wrapper.dataset.idx);
      wrapper.style.opacity = '0.45';
    });
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragend', () => wrapper.style.opacity = '1');
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragover', e => { e.preventDefault(); wrapper.classList.add('drag-over-indicator'); });
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragleave', () => wrapper.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator'));
    wrapper.addEventListener('drop', e => {
      e.preventDefault(); wrapper.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator');
      const fromIdx = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');
      const fromEl = grid.querySelector(`[data-idx="${fromIdx}"]`);
      if(!fromEl || fromEl === wrapper) return;
      const allEls = Array.from(grid.children);
      const fPos = allEls.indexOf(fromEl), tPos = allEls.indexOf(wrapper);
      if(fPos < tPos) grid.insertBefore(fromEl, wrapper.nextSibling);
      else grid.insertBefore(fromEl, wrapper);
    });

    grid.appendChild(wrapper);
  }
}

// Helper: apply grid page order + deletions to a PDFLib doc copy
async function applyGridTransformsToPDF(gridId, srcDoc, outDoc) {
  const grid = document.getElementById(gridId);
  if(!grid) return;
  const deletedSet = _gridDeletedPages[gridId] || new Set();
  const orderedEls = Array.from(grid.querySelectorAll('.pdf-preview-page'));
  const keepIndices = orderedEls
    .map(el => parseInt(el.dataset.idx))
    .filter(idx => !deletedSet.has(parseInt(idx)));
  if(keepIndices.length === 0) throw new Error('All pages deleted — nothing to save.');
  const pages = await outDoc.copyPages(srcDoc, keepIndices);
  pages.forEach(p => outDoc.addPage(p));
  return { deletedCount: deletedSet.size, total: orderedEls.length, kept: keepIndices.length };
}

async function getPDFJSFromFile(file){
  pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
  const ab = await readBuf(file);
  return pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:new Uint8Array(ab),verbosity:0,cMapUrl:'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/cmaps/',cMapPacked:true}).promise;
}

async function loadGenericPreview(key, files){
  if(!files||!files[0]) return;
  pdfSingle(key, files);
  const dzEl = document.getElementById('dz-'+key);
  const dzMore = document.getElementById('dz-'+key+'-more');
  const previewArea = document.getElementById(key+'-preview-area');
  const info = document.getElementById('dz-'+key+'-info');
  const btn = document.getElementById('btn-'+key);
  if(dzEl) dzEl.style.display = 'none';
  if(dzMore) dzMore.style.display = 'flex';
  if(info) info.textContent = files[0].name+' (loading…)';
  if(previewArea) previewArea.style.display = 'block';
  if(btn) btn.disabled = false;
  try {
    const pdf = await getPDFJSFromFile(files[0]);
    if(info) info.textContent = files[0].name+' · '+pdf.numPages+' page'+(pdf.numPages!==1?'s':'');
    await renderPagePreviewGrid(key+'-page-grid', pdf);
  } catch(e) {
    if(info) info.textContent = files[0].name+' (preview unavailable)';
  }
}

async function loadSplitPreview(files){
  if(!files||!files[0]) return;
  pdfSingle('split', files);
  document.getElementById('dz-split').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('dz-split-more').style.display = 'flex';
  document.getElementById('split-preview-area').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('btn-split').disabled = false;
  try {
    const pdf = await getPDFJSFromFile(files[0]);
    document.getElementById('dz-split-info').textContent = files[0].name+' · '+pdf.numPages+' page'+(pdf.numPages!==1?'s':'');
    await renderPagePreviewGrid('split-page-grid', pdf);
  } catch(e) {
    document.getElementById('dz-split-info').textContent = files[0].name+' (preview unavailable)';
  }
}

async function loadMergePreview(){
  const files = PF['merge'];
  if(!files || files.length === 0) return;
  const previewArea = document.getElementById('merge-preview-area');
  const grid = document.getElementById('merge-page-grid');
  if(!previewArea || !grid) return;
  previewArea.style.display = 'block';
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  _gridDeletedPages['merge-page-grid'] = new Set();

  // Instructions bar (only add once)
  let instrBar = previewArea.querySelector('.pdf-preview-instructions');
  if(!instrBar){
    instrBar = document.createElement('div');
    instrBar.className = 'pdf-preview-instructions';
    instrBar.innerHTML = `<span>⠿ drag to reorder</span><span>🗑 hover & click ✕ to delete</span><span id="merge-page-grid-del-count" style="color:var(--red);display:none"></span>`;
    previewArea.insertBefore(instrBar, grid);
  }

  try {
    let globalIdx = 0;
    for(let fi=0; fi<files.length; fi++){
      const pdf = await getPDFJSFromFile(files[fi]);
      const fileColor = ['var(--red)','var(--teal)','var(--amber)','var(--green)'][fi % 4];
      for(let i=1; i<=pdf.numPages; i++){
        const capturedGlobalIdx = globalIdx;
        const page = await pdf.getPage(i);
        const vp = page.getViewport({scale:0.4});
        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
        wrapper.className = 'pdf-preview-page';
        wrapper.dataset.idx = capturedGlobalIdx;
        wrapper.dataset.fileIdx = fi;
        wrapper.dataset.pageInFile = i;
        wrapper.draggable = true;
        wrapper.style.borderTop = '3px solid '+fileColor;

        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width = vp.width; canvas.height = vp.height;
        await page.render({canvasContext:canvas.getContext('2d'), viewport:vp}).promise;

        // Delete button overlay
        const actions = document.createElement('div');
        actions.className = 'pdf-preview-page-actions';
        const delBtn = document.createElement('button');
        delBtn.className = 'pdf-page-action-btn pdf-page-action-del';
        delBtn.title = 'Delete page'; delBtn.textContent = '✕';
        const myGIdx = capturedGlobalIdx;
        delBtn.onclick = (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          const isDeleted = wrapper.classList.contains('page-deleted');
          if(isDeleted){
            wrapper.classList.remove('page-deleted');
            delBtn.textContent = '✕'; delBtn.style.background = ''; delBtn.style.color = '';
            _gridDeletedPages['merge-page-grid'].delete(myGIdx);
          } else {
            wrapper.classList.add('page-deleted');
            delBtn.textContent = '↩'; delBtn.style.background = 'rgba(163,255,79,0.7)'; delBtn.style.color = '#0d0b1a';
            _gridDeletedPages['merge-page-grid'].add(myGIdx);
          }
          const countEl = document.getElementById('merge-page-grid-del-count');
          if(countEl){
            const n = _gridDeletedPages['merge-page-grid'].size;
            countEl.style.display = n>0?'inline-flex':'none';
            countEl.textContent = n+' page'+(n!==1?'s':'')+' marked for deletion';
          }
        };
        actions.appendChild(delBtn);

        const label = document.createElement('div');
        label.className = 'pdf-preview-page-label';
        label.innerHTML = `<span>p.${i}</span><span style="color:${fileColor};font-size:8px">F${fi+1}</span>`;

        wrapper.appendChild(canvas);
        wrapper.appendChild(actions);
        wrapper.appendChild(label);

        // Drag-to-reorder
        wrapper.addEventListener('dragstart', e => { e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', capturedGlobalIdx); wrapper.style.opacity='0.45'; });
        wrapper.addEventListener('dragend', () => wrapper.style.opacity='1');
        wrapper.addEventListener('dragover', e => { e.preventDefault(); wrapper.classList.add('drag-over-indicator'); });
        wrapper.addEventListener('dragleave', () => wrapper.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator'));
        wrapper.addEventListener('drop', e => {
          e.preventDefault(); wrapper.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator');
          const fromIdx = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');
          const fromEl = grid.querySelector(`[data-idx="${fromIdx}"]`);
          if(!fromEl || fromEl===wrapper) return;
          const allEls = Array.from(grid.children);
          const fPos = allEls.indexOf(fromEl), tPos = allEls.indexOf(wrapper);
          if(fPos < tPos) grid.insertBefore(fromEl, wrapper.nextSibling);
          else grid.insertBefore(fromEl, wrapper);
        });

        grid.appendChild(wrapper);
        globalIdx++;
      }
    }
  } catch(e) { /* silently fail preview */ }
}

// ─── MERGE ───
async function doMerge(){
  const files=PF['merge']; if(!files||files.length<2)return;
  setStatus('merge','loading',`Merging ${files.length} PDFs…`);
  document.getElementById('dl-merge').innerHTML='';
  try{
    const {PDFDocument}=PDFLib;

    // Check if grid has been rendered with reorder/delete state
    const grid = document.getElementById('merge-page-grid');
    const deletedSet = _gridDeletedPages['merge-page-grid'] || new Set();
    const gridEls = grid ? Array.from(grid.querySelectorAll('.pdf-preview-page')) : [];

    if(gridEls.length > 0){
      // Build a flat array of all pages from all files
      const allPages = [];
      for(let fi=0; fi<files.length; fi++){
        const doc = await PDFDocument.load(await readBuf(files[fi]));
        const count = doc.getPageCount();
        for(let pi=0; pi<count; pi++) allPages.push({doc, pi});
      }
      const out = await PDFDocument.create();
      // Follow the visual order in the grid, skipping deleted
      for(const el of gridEls){
        const gIdx = parseInt(el.dataset.idx);
        if(deletedSet.has(gIdx)) continue;
        if(gIdx < allPages.length){
          const {doc, pi} = allPages[gIdx];
          const [pg] = await out.copyPages(doc, [pi]);
          out.addPage(pg);
        }
      }
      if(out.getPageCount()===0) throw new Error('All pages deleted — nothing to merge.');
      const bytes = await out.save();
      const delMsg = deletedSet.size > 0 ? ` (${deletedSet.size} pages removed)` : '';
      setStatus('merge','ok',`✓ Merged ${files.length} PDFs${delMsg} · ${fz(bytes.length)}`);
      addDL('dl-merge',bytes,'merged.pdf','application/pdf');
    } else {
      // Fallback: standard merge without grid state
      const out=await PDFDocument.create();
      for(const f of files){ const b=await readBuf(f); const d=await PDFDocument.load(b); const pg=await out.copyPages(d,d.getPageIndices()); pg.forEach(p=>out.addPage(p)); }
      const bytes=await out.save();
      setStatus('merge','ok',`✓ Merged ${files.length} PDFs · ${fz(bytes.length)}`);
      addDL('dl-merge',bytes,'merged.pdf','application/pdf');
    }
  }catch(e){setStatus('merge','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

// ─── SPLIT ───
let splitMode='each';
function setSplitMode(m,btn){
  splitMode=m;
  document.querySelectorAll('#p-pdf-split .tab').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));btn.classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('split-range-cfg').style.display=m==='range'?'block':'none';
  document.getElementById('split-specific-cfg').style.display=m==='specific'?'block':'none';
}
async function doSplit(){
  const files=PF['split']; if(!files||!files[0])return;
  setStatus('split','loading','Splitting…');
  document.getElementById('dl-split').innerHTML='';
  try{
    const {PDFDocument}=PDFLib;
    const src=await PDFDocument.load(await readBuf(files[0]));
    const total=src.getPageCount();
    let groups=[];
    if(splitMode==='each') groups=Array.from({length:total},(_,i)=>[i]);
    else if(splitMode==='range'){
      const f=Math.max(0,parseInt(document.getElementById('sp-from').value)-1);
      const t=Math.min(total-1,parseInt(document.getElementById('sp-to').value)-1);
      groups=[[...Array.from({length:t-f+1},(_,i)=>f+i)]];
    } else {
      const raw=document.getElementById('sp-pages').value;
      const pg=[];
      raw.split(',').forEach(s=>{s=s.trim();if(s.includes('-')){const[a,b]=s.split('-').map(Number);for(let i=a;i<=b&&i<=total;i++)pg.push(i-1);}else{const n=parseInt(s)-1;if(n>=0&&n<total)pg.push(n);}});
      groups=[pg];
    }
    const dl=document.getElementById('dl-split');
    for(let i=0;i<groups.length;i++){
      const doc=await PDFDocument.create();
      const pg=await doc.copyPages(src,groups[i]);
      pg.forEach(p=>doc.addPage(p));
      const b=await doc.save();
      const nm=splitMode==='each'?`page-${groups[i][0]+1}.pdf`:`split-${i+1}.pdf`;
      dl.appendChild(makeDL(b,nm,'application/pdf'));
    }
    setStatus('split','ok',`✓ Created ${splitMode==='each'?total:groups.length} file(s)`);
  }catch(e){setStatus('split','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

// ─── COMPRESS ───
let _compressPDFOrigSize = 0;

// initCompressSizeUI — called alongside loadGenericPreview, stores original size only
function initCompressSizeUI(files) {
  if(!files||!files[0]) return;
  _compressPDFOrigSize = files[0].size;
  // Hide result stats until compression is actually run
  document.getElementById('compress-size-preview').style.display='none';
}

function updatePDFCompressLabel() {
  const lvl = parseInt(document.getElementById('compress-level').value);
  const labels = ['','Low','Medium','High (smaller file)'];
  const descs = ['','Preserves most image quality. Good for text-heavy PDFs.','Balances file size reduction with image quality.','Maximum compression. Smaller file, reduced image quality.'];
  document.getElementById('compress-level-label').textContent = labels[lvl];
  document.getElementById('compress-level-desc').textContent = descs[lvl];
  // Hide any previous result stats when level changes (stale after slider move)
  document.getElementById('compress-size-preview').style.display='none';
}

async function doCompress(){
  const files=PF['compress']; if(!files||!files[0])return;
  setStatus('compress','loading','Compressing — rendering pages…');
  document.getElementById('dl-compress').innerHTML='';
  const level = parseInt((document.getElementById('compress-level')||{value:'2'}).value) || 2;

  // Scale & quality per level
  // level 1 (Low):    render at 1.2x scale, jpeg quality 0.82
  // level 2 (Medium): render at 1.0x scale, jpeg quality 0.60
  // level 3 (High):   render at 0.75x scale, jpeg quality 0.38
  const renderScale = [0, 1.2, 1.0, 0.75][level];
  const jpegQuality = [0, 0.82, 0.60, 0.38][level];

  try{
    const {PDFDocument, rgb} = PDFLib;
    const orig = await readBuf(files[0]);
    const origByteLength = orig.byteLength || files[0].size; // capture before possible ArrayBuffer detach

    // Load with pdfjs to render each page to canvas
    pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
    const pdfjs = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({data: new Uint8Array(orig), verbosity:0}).promise;

    // Determine which pages to keep (respecting delete/reorder from grid)
    const grid = document.getElementById('compress-page-grid');
    const deletedSet = _gridDeletedPages['compress-page-grid'] || new Set();
    const orderedEls = Array.from(grid ? grid.querySelectorAll('.pdf-preview-page') : []);
    let keepIndices; // 0-based page indices from original
    if(orderedEls.length > 0){
      keepIndices = orderedEls.map(el=>parseInt(el.dataset.idx)).filter(idx=>!deletedSet.has(idx));
    } else {
      keepIndices = Array.from({length: pdfjs.numPages}, (_,i)=>i);
    }
    if(keepIndices.length === 0) throw new Error('All pages deleted — nothing to save.');

    const outDoc = await PDFDocument.create();
    // Strip metadata
    outDoc.setTitle(''); outDoc.setAuthor(''); outDoc.setSubject('');
    outDoc.setKeywords([]); outDoc.setProducer(''); outDoc.setCreator('');

    const offscreen = document.createElement('canvas');
    const ctx = offscreen.getContext('2d');

    for(let i=0; i<keepIndices.length; i++){
      const pageNum = keepIndices[i] + 1; // pdfjs is 1-based
      setStatus('compress','loading',`Compressing page ${i+1}/${keepIndices.length}…`);
      const page = await pdfjs.getPage(pageNum);
      const vp = page.getViewport({scale: renderScale});
      offscreen.width  = Math.round(vp.width);
      offscreen.height = Math.round(vp.height);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, offscreen.width, offscreen.height);
      await page.render({canvasContext: ctx, viewport: vp}).promise;

      // Convert canvas to JPEG bytes
      const dataUrl = offscreen.toDataURL('image/jpeg', jpegQuality);
      const b64 = dataUrl.split(',')[1];
      const imgBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(b64), c=>c.charCodeAt(0));

      // Embed as JPEG in new PDF page
      const jpgImage = await outDoc.embedJpg(imgBytes);
      // Keep original page dimensions (points = px at 72dpi; vp is at renderScale*72dpi)
      const origVp = page.getViewport({scale:1.0});
      const pdfPage = outDoc.addPage([origVp.width, origVp.height]);
      pdfPage.drawImage(jpgImage, {x:0, y:0, width:origVp.width, height:origVp.height});
    }

    const b = await outDoc.save({useObjectStreams:true});
    const saved = origByteLength - b.length;
    const delCount = keepIndices.length < pdfjs.numPages ? (pdfjs.numPages - keepIndices.length) : 0;
    const delMsg = delCount > 0 ? ` · ${delCount} page(s) removed` : '';

    setStatus('compress','ok',`✓ ${saved>0?`Saved ${fz(saved)} (${((saved/origByteLength)*100).toFixed(1)}% smaller)`:'File already optimised'} · ${fz(b.length)}${delMsg}`);
    addDL('dl-compress', b, 'compressed.pdf', 'application/pdf');

    // Show actual compression results
    const origBytes = origByteLength;
    const compBytes = b.length;
    const actPct = Math.max(0,((origBytes-compBytes)/origBytes)*100).toFixed(1);
    document.getElementById('compress-orig-size').textContent = fz(origBytes);
    document.getElementById('compress-est-size').textContent = fz(compBytes);
    document.getElementById('compress-est-savings').textContent = `${actPct}% saved`;
    document.getElementById('compress-savings-bar').style.width = actPct+'%';
    document.getElementById('compress-size-preview').style.display='block';
  }catch(e){setStatus('compress','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

// ─── ROTATE ───
const pageRotations = {}; // key: page rotations array

async function loadRotatePreview(files) {
  if(!files||!files[0]) return;
  pdfSingle('rotate', files);
  const file = files[0];
  document.getElementById('dz-rotate').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('dz-rotate-more').style.display='flex';
  document.getElementById('dz-rotate-info').textContent = file.name+' (loading pages…)';
  document.getElementById('rotate-preview-area').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('btn-rotate').disabled=false;

  const ab = await readBuf(file);
  const pdfJS = pdfjsLib;
  pdfJS.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
  const pdf = await pdfJS.getDocument({data: new Uint8Array(ab)}).promise;
  const grid = document.getElementById('rotate-page-grid');
  grid.innerHTML='';
  _gridDeletedPages['rotate-page-grid'] = new Set();
  pageRotations['rotate'] = new Array(pdf.numPages).fill(0);
  document.getElementById('dz-rotate-info').textContent = file.name+' · '+pdf.numPages+' page'+(pdf.numPages!==1?'s':'');

  // Instructions bar
  let instrBar = grid.previousElementSibling;
  if(!instrBar || !instrBar.classList.contains('pdf-preview-instructions')){
    instrBar = document.createElement('div');
    instrBar.className = 'pdf-preview-instructions';
    instrBar.innerHTML = `<span>↻ click to rotate</span><span>⠿ drag to reorder</span><span>🗑 hover & click ✕ to delete</span><span id="rotate-page-grid-del-count" style="color:var(--red);display:none"></span>`;
    grid.parentElement.insertBefore(instrBar, grid);
  }

  for(let i=1;i<=pdf.numPages;i++){
    const page = await pdf.getPage(i);
    const vp = page.getViewport({scale:0.5});
    const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
    wrapper.className='pdf-preview-page';
    wrapper.dataset.page = i;
    wrapper.dataset.idx = i-1;
    wrapper.draggable = true;
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = vp.width; canvas.height = vp.height;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    await page.render({canvasContext:ctx, viewport:vp}).promise;

    // Click to rotate
    canvas.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    canvas.title = 'Click to rotate 90° CW';
    canvas.onclick = () => rotateSinglePage('rotate', i-1, canvas, wrapper);

    // Delete/restore button
    const actions = document.createElement('div');
    actions.className = 'pdf-preview-page-actions';
    const delBtn = document.createElement('button');
    delBtn.className = 'pdf-page-action-btn pdf-page-action-del';
    delBtn.title = 'Delete page'; delBtn.textContent = '✕';
    const captI = i;
    delBtn.onclick = (e) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      const isDeleted = wrapper.classList.contains('page-deleted');
      if(isDeleted){
        wrapper.classList.remove('page-deleted');
        delBtn.textContent='✕'; delBtn.style.background=''; delBtn.style.color='';
        _gridDeletedPages['rotate-page-grid'].delete(captI-1);
      } else {
        wrapper.classList.add('page-deleted');
        delBtn.textContent='↩'; delBtn.style.background='rgba(163,255,79,0.7)'; delBtn.style.color='#0d0b1a';
        _gridDeletedPages['rotate-page-grid'].add(captI-1);
      }
      const countEl = document.getElementById('rotate-page-grid-del-count');
      if(countEl){
        const n = _gridDeletedPages['rotate-page-grid'].size;
        countEl.style.display = n>0?'inline-flex':'none';
        countEl.textContent = n+' page'+(n!==1?'s':'')+' marked for deletion';
      }
    };
    actions.appendChild(delBtn);

    const label = document.createElement('div');
    label.className='pdf-preview-page-label';
    label.innerHTML=`<span>Page ${i}</span><span id="rot-lbl-${i}">0°</span>`;

    wrapper.appendChild(canvas);
    wrapper.appendChild(actions);
    wrapper.appendChild(label);

    // Drag-to-reorder
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragstart', e => { e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', i-1); wrapper.style.opacity='0.45'; });
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragend', () => wrapper.style.opacity='1');
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragover', e => { e.preventDefault(); wrapper.classList.add('drag-over-indicator'); });
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragleave', () => wrapper.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator'));
    wrapper.addEventListener('drop', e => {
      e.preventDefault(); wrapper.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator');
      const fromIdx = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');
      const fromEl = grid.querySelector(`[data-idx="${fromIdx}"]`);
      if(!fromEl || fromEl===wrapper) return;
      const allEls = Array.from(grid.children);
      const fPos = allEls.indexOf(fromEl), tPos = allEls.indexOf(wrapper);
      if(fPos < tPos) grid.insertBefore(fromEl, wrapper.nextSibling);
      else grid.insertBefore(fromEl, wrapper);
    });

    grid.appendChild(wrapper);
  }
}

function rotateSinglePage(key, idx, canvas, wrapper) {
  pageRotations[key][idx] = (pageRotations[key][idx] + 90) % 360;
  const deg = pageRotations[key][idx];
  canvas.style.transform = `rotate(${deg}deg)`;
  canvas.style.transition = 'transform 0.3s ease';
  // Adjust wrapper height for 90/270 rotations
  if(deg===90||deg===270){
    const w=canvas.width,h=canvas.height;
    canvas.style.margin=`${(w-h)/2}px ${(h-w)/2}px`;
  } else { canvas.style.margin=''; }
  const lbl = document.getElementById('rot-lbl-'+(idx+1));
  if(lbl) lbl.textContent = deg+'°';
}

function rotateAllPages(deg) {
  const pages = document.querySelectorAll('#rotate-page-grid .pdf-preview-page');
  pages.forEach((wrapper,i)=>{
    const canvas = wrapper.querySelector('canvas');
    pageRotations['rotate'][i] = (pageRotations['rotate'][i] + deg) % 360;
    const r = pageRotations['rotate'][i];
    canvas.style.transform=`rotate(${r}deg)`;
    canvas.style.transition='transform 0.35s ease';
    if(r===90||r===270){
      const w=canvas.width,h=canvas.height;
      canvas.style.margin=`${(w-h)/2}px ${(h-w)/2}px`;
    } else { canvas.style.margin=''; }
    const lbl=document.getElementById('rot-lbl-'+(i+1));
    if(lbl) lbl.textContent=r+'°';
  });
}

async function doRotate(){
  const files=PF['rotate']; if(!files||!files[0])return;
  setStatus('rotate','loading','Applying rotations…');
  document.getElementById('dl-rotate').innerHTML='';
  try{
    const {PDFDocument,degrees}=PDFLib;
    const src=await PDFDocument.load(await readBuf(files[0]));
    const out=await PDFDocument.create();
    const grid = document.getElementById('rotate-page-grid');
    const deletedSet = _gridDeletedPages['rotate-page-grid'] || new Set();
    const orderedEls = Array.from(grid.querySelectorAll('.pdf-preview-page'));
    const keepIndices = orderedEls.map(el=>parseInt(el.dataset.idx)).filter(idx=>!deletedSet.has(idx));
    if(keepIndices.length===0) throw new Error('All pages deleted.');
    const rots = pageRotations['rotate'] || [];
    const pgs = await out.copyPages(src, keepIndices);
    pgs.forEach((p,i)=>{
      const origIdx = keepIndices[i];
      const r = rots[origIdx]||0;
      p.setRotation(degrees((p.getRotation().angle+r)%360));
      out.addPage(p);
    });
    const b=await out.save();
    const delMsg = deletedSet.size>0 ? ` · ${deletedSet.size} page(s) removed` : '';
    setStatus('rotate','ok',`✓ Rotations applied${delMsg}`);
    addDL('dl-rotate',b,'rotated.pdf','application/pdf');
  }catch(e){setStatus('rotate','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

// ─── WATERMARK PDF ───
// ─── WATERMARK PDF ───
let wmkPageBitmap = null; // stores the first page ImageBitmap for live preview

async function loadWatermarkPreview(files) {
  if(!files||!files[0]) return;
  loadGenericPreview('wmk', files);
  // Also capture page 1 as a bitmap for the live canvas preview
  try {
    const pdf = await getPDFJSFromFile(files[0]);
    const page = await pdf.getPage(1);
    const vp = page.getViewport({ scale: 1.5 });
    const offscreen = document.createElement('canvas');
    offscreen.width = vp.width;
    offscreen.height = vp.height;
    await page.render({ canvasContext: offscreen.getContext('2d'), viewport: vp }).promise;
    wmkPageBitmap = await createImageBitmap(offscreen);
    document.getElementById('wmk-live-preview-wrap').style.display = 'block';
    updateWatermarkLivePreview();
  } catch(e) { /* silently skip live preview if rendering fails */ }
}

function updateWatermarkLivePreview() {
  if (!wmkPageBitmap) return;
  const canvas = document.getElementById('wmk-live-canvas');
  if (!canvas) return;

  // Scale bitmap to max 560px wide for the preview pane
  const maxW = Math.min(560, wmkPageBitmap.width);
  const scale = maxW / wmkPageBitmap.width;
  canvas.width  = Math.round(wmkPageBitmap.width  * scale);
  canvas.height = Math.round(wmkPageBitmap.height * scale);

  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

  // Draw the page
  ctx.drawImage(wmkPageBitmap, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // Read watermark settings
  const text    = document.getElementById('wmk-text').value    || 'CONFIDENTIAL';
  const opacity = (parseInt(document.getElementById('wmk-opacity').value) || 25) / 100;
  const hex     = document.getElementById('wmk-color').value   || '#ff0000';
  // Font size: the PDF uses points; scale proportionally to the preview canvas
  // The PDF page is typically ~612pt wide; we scale fontSize by the same ratio
  const pdfFontSizePt  = parseInt(document.getElementById('wmk-size').value) || 60;
  const pdfPageWidthPt = 612; // A4/Letter approximation
  const canvasFontSize = Math.round(pdfFontSizePt * (canvas.width / pdfPageWidthPt));

  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;
  ctx.fillStyle   = hex;
  ctx.font        = `bold ${canvasFontSize}px Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif`;
  ctx.textAlign   = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';

  // Rotate 45° around the canvas center (matching PDF behaviour)
  ctx.translate(canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2);
  ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 4);
  ctx.fillText(text, 0, 0);
  ctx.restore();
}

function clearWatermarkTool() {
  pdfClear('wmk');
  wmkPageBitmap = null;
  document.getElementById('wmk-preview-area').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('wmk-live-preview-wrap').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('dz-wmk-more').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('dz-wmk').style.display = 'block';
  const c = document.getElementById('wmk-live-canvas');
  if (c) { const ctx = c.getContext('2d'); ctx.clearRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height); }
}

async function doWatermarkPDF(){
  const files=PF['wmk']; if(!files||!files[0])return;
  setStatus('wmk','loading','Adding watermark…');
  document.getElementById('dl-wmk').innerHTML='';
  try{
    const {PDFDocument,StandardFonts,rgb,degrees}=PDFLib;
    const src=await PDFDocument.load(await readBuf(files[0]));
    const out=await PDFDocument.create();
    const info = await applyGridTransformsToPDF('wmk-page-grid', src, out);
    const font=await out.embedFont(StandardFonts.HelveticaBold);
    const text=document.getElementById('wmk-text').value||'CONFIDENTIAL';
    const sz=parseInt(document.getElementById('wmk-size').value)||60;
    const op=parseInt(document.getElementById('wmk-opacity').value)/100;
    const hex=document.getElementById('wmk-color').value;
    const rv=parseInt(hex.slice(1,3),16)/255, gv=parseInt(hex.slice(3,5),16)/255, bv=parseInt(hex.slice(5,7),16)/255;
    out.getPages().forEach(page=>{
      const {width,height}=page.getSize();
      const tw=font.widthOfTextAtSize(text,sz);
      page.drawText(text,{x:width/2-tw/2,y:height/2-sz/2,size:sz,font,color:rgb(rv,gv,bv),opacity:op,rotate:degrees(45)});
    });
    const b=await out.save();
    const delMsg = info && info.deletedCount>0 ? ` · ${info.deletedCount} page(s) removed` : '';
    setStatus('wmk','ok',`✓ Watermark added${delMsg}`);
    addDL('dl-wmk',b,'watermarked.pdf','application/pdf');
  }catch(e){setStatus('wmk','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

// ─── PAGE NUMBERS ───
async function doPageNums(){
  const files=PF['pgn']; if(!files||!files[0])return;
  setStatus('pgn','loading','Adding page numbers…');
  document.getElementById('dl-pgn').innerHTML='';
  try{
    const {PDFDocument,StandardFonts,rgb}=PDFLib;
    const src=await PDFDocument.load(await readBuf(files[0]));
    const out=await PDFDocument.create();
    const info = await applyGridTransformsToPDF('pgn-page-grid', src, out);
    const font=await out.embedFont(StandardFonts.Helvetica);
    const start=parseInt(document.getElementById('pgn-start').value)||1;
    const fs=parseInt(document.getElementById('pgn-size').value)||12;
    const pos=document.getElementById('pgn-pos').value;
    const mg=20;
    out.getPages().forEach((page,i)=>{
      const {width,height}=page.getSize();
      const lbl=String(start+i);
      const tw=font.widthOfTextAtSize(lbl,fs);
      let x,y;
      if(pos==='bc'){x=width/2-tw/2;y=mg;}
      else if(pos==='br'){x=width-tw-mg;y=mg;}
      else if(pos==='bl'){x=mg;y=mg;}
      else if(pos==='tc'){x=width/2-tw/2;y=height-mg-fs;}
      else {x=width-tw-mg;y=height-mg-fs;}
      page.drawText(lbl,{x,y,size:fs,font,color:rgb(0.3,0.3,0.3)});
    });
    const b=await out.save();
    const delMsg = info && info.deletedCount>0 ? ` · ${info.deletedCount} page(s) removed` : '';
    setStatus('pgn','ok',`✓ Page numbers added${delMsg}`);
    addDL('dl-pgn',b,'numbered.pdf','application/pdf');
  }catch(e){setStatus('pgn','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

// ─── REORDER PAGES ───
let reorderPageOrder = [];
async function loadReorderPreview(files){
  if(!files||!files[0])return;
  pdfSingle('reorder',files);
  document.getElementById('dz-reorder').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('reorder-preview-area').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('btn-reorder').disabled=false;
  const ab=await readBuf(files[0]);
  pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
  const pdf=await pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:new Uint8Array(ab),verbosity:0,cMapUrl:'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/cmaps/',cMapPacked:true}).promise;
  const grid=document.getElementById('reorder-page-grid');
  grid.innerHTML=''; reorderPageOrder=[];
  _gridDeletedPages['reorder-page-grid'] = new Set();

  // Instructions bar
  let instrBar = grid.previousElementSibling;
  if(!instrBar || !instrBar.classList.contains('pdf-preview-instructions')){
    instrBar = document.createElement('div');
    instrBar.className = 'pdf-preview-instructions';
    instrBar.innerHTML = `<span>⠿ drag to reorder</span><span>🗑 hover & click ✕ to delete</span><span id="reorder-page-grid-del-count" style="color:var(--red);display:none"></span>`;
    grid.parentElement.insertBefore(instrBar, grid);
  }

  for(let i=1;i<=pdf.numPages;i++){
    reorderPageOrder.push(i-1);
    const page=await pdf.getPage(i);
    const vp=page.getViewport({scale:0.4});
    const wrapper=document.createElement('div');
    wrapper.className='pdf-preview-page';
    wrapper.draggable=true;
    wrapper.dataset.idx=i-1;

    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width=vp.width; canvas.height=vp.height;
    await page.render({canvasContext:canvas.getContext('2d'),viewport:vp}).promise;

    // Delete button
    const actions = document.createElement('div');
    actions.className = 'pdf-preview-page-actions';
    const delBtn = document.createElement('button');
    delBtn.className = 'pdf-page-action-btn pdf-page-action-del';
    delBtn.title = 'Delete page'; delBtn.textContent = '✕';
    const captI = i;
    delBtn.onclick = (e) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      const isDeleted = wrapper.classList.contains('page-deleted');
      if(isDeleted){
        wrapper.classList.remove('page-deleted');
        delBtn.textContent='✕'; delBtn.style.background=''; delBtn.style.color='';
        _gridDeletedPages['reorder-page-grid'].delete(captI-1);
      } else {
        wrapper.classList.add('page-deleted');
        delBtn.textContent='↩'; delBtn.style.background='rgba(163,255,79,0.7)'; delBtn.style.color='#0d0b1a';
        _gridDeletedPages['reorder-page-grid'].add(captI-1);
      }
      const countEl = document.getElementById('reorder-page-grid-del-count');
      if(countEl){
        const n = _gridDeletedPages['reorder-page-grid'].size;
        countEl.style.display = n>0?'inline-flex':'none';
        countEl.textContent = n+' page'+(n!==1?'s':'')+' marked for deletion';
      }
    };
    actions.appendChild(delBtn);

    const label=document.createElement('div');
    label.className='pdf-preview-page-label';
    label.innerHTML=`<span>Page ${i}</span><span style="opacity:0.5;font-size:7.5px">⠿</span>`;
    wrapper.appendChild(canvas); wrapper.appendChild(actions); wrapper.appendChild(label);
    // Drag events
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragstart',e=>{e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',wrapper.dataset.idx);wrapper.style.opacity='0.45';});
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragend',()=>wrapper.style.opacity='1');
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{e.preventDefault();wrapper.classList.add('drag-over-indicator');});
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragleave',()=>wrapper.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator'));
    wrapper.addEventListener('drop',e=>{
      e.preventDefault(); wrapper.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator');
      const fromIdx=parseInt(e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain'));
      const toIdx=parseInt(wrapper.dataset.idx);
      if(fromIdx===toIdx)return;
      const fromEl=grid.querySelector(`[data-idx="${fromIdx}"]`);
      const toEl=grid.querySelector(`[data-idx="${toIdx}"]`);
      if(!fromEl||!toEl)return;
      const allEls=Array.from(grid.children);
      const fPos=allEls.indexOf(fromEl),tPos=allEls.indexOf(toEl);
      if(fPos<tPos) grid.insertBefore(fromEl,toEl.nextSibling);
      else grid.insertBefore(fromEl,toEl);
    });
    grid.appendChild(wrapper);
  }
}

async function doReorder(){
  const files=PF['reorder'];if(!files||!files[0])return;
  setStatus('reorder','loading','Reordering pages…');
  document.getElementById('dl-reorder').innerHTML='';
  try{
    const {PDFDocument}=PDFLib;
    const src=await PDFDocument.load(await readBuf(files[0]));
    const out=await PDFDocument.create();
    const grid=document.getElementById('reorder-page-grid');
    const deletedSet = _gridDeletedPages['reorder-page-grid'] || new Set();
    const order=Array.from(grid.querySelectorAll('.pdf-preview-page'))
      .map(el=>parseInt(el.dataset.idx))
      .filter(idx=>!deletedSet.has(idx));
    if(order.length===0) throw new Error('All pages deleted.');
    const pgs=await out.copyPages(src,order);
    pgs.forEach(p=>out.addPage(p));
    const b=await out.save();
    const delMsg = deletedSet.size>0 ? ` · ${deletedSet.size} page(s) removed` : '';
    setStatus('reorder','ok',`✓ Pages reordered${delMsg} · ${fz(b.length)}`);
    addDL('dl-reorder',b,'reordered.pdf','application/pdf');
  }catch(e){setStatus('reorder','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

// ─── DELETE PAGES ───
const deletedPages = new Set();
async function loadDeletePreview(files){
  if(!files||!files[0])return;
  pdfSingle('delp',files);
  document.getElementById('dz-delp').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('delp-preview-area').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('btn-delp').disabled=false;
  deletedPages.clear();
  _gridDeletedPages['delp-page-grid'] = deletedPages;
  const ab=await readBuf(files[0]);
  pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
  const pdf=await pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:new Uint8Array(ab),verbosity:0,cMapUrl:'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/cmaps/',cMapPacked:true}).promise;
  const grid=document.getElementById('delp-page-grid');
  grid.innerHTML='';
  for(let i=1;i<=pdf.numPages;i++){
    const page=await pdf.getPage(i);
    const vp=page.getViewport({scale:0.4});
    const wrapper=document.createElement('div');
    wrapper.className='pdf-preview-page';
    wrapper.dataset.page=i;
    wrapper.dataset.idx=i-1;
    wrapper.draggable=true;
    const captI=i;
    const cb=document.createElement('input');
    cb.type='checkbox'; cb.className='pdf-preview-page-cb';
    cb.addEventListener('change',()=>{
      if(cb.checked){deletedPages.add(i);wrapper.classList.add('page-deleted');}
      else{deletedPages.delete(i);wrapper.classList.remove('page-deleted');}
      updateDeleteCount(pdf.numPages);
    });
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width=vp.width; canvas.height=vp.height;
    await page.render({canvasContext:canvas.getContext('2d'),viewport:vp}).promise;
    const label=document.createElement('div');
    label.className='pdf-preview-page-label';
    label.innerHTML=`<span>Page ${i}</span><span style="opacity:0.5;font-size:7.5px">⠿</span>`;
    wrapper.appendChild(cb); wrapper.appendChild(canvas); wrapper.appendChild(label);
    wrapper.addEventListener('click',e=>{ if(e.target===cb)return; cb.checked=!cb.checked; cb.dispatchEvent(new Event('change')); });
    // Drag to reorder
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragstart',e=>{e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',captI-1);wrapper.style.opacity='0.45';});
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragend',()=>wrapper.style.opacity='1');
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{e.preventDefault();wrapper.classList.add('drag-over-indicator');});
    wrapper.addEventListener('dragleave',()=>wrapper.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator'));
    wrapper.addEventListener('drop',e=>{
      e.preventDefault();wrapper.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator');
      const fromIdx=e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');
      const fromEl=grid.querySelector(`[data-idx="${fromIdx}"]`);
      if(!fromEl||fromEl===wrapper)return;
      const allEls=Array.from(grid.children);
      const fPos=allEls.indexOf(fromEl),tPos=allEls.indexOf(wrapper);
      if(fPos<tPos) grid.insertBefore(fromEl,wrapper.nextSibling);
      else grid.insertBefore(fromEl,wrapper);
    });
    grid.appendChild(wrapper);
  }
}
function updateDeleteCount(total){
  const el=document.getElementById('delp-selected-count');
  el.textContent=deletedPages.size>0?`${deletedPages.size} page(s) selected for deletion`:'No pages selected';
}
function clearDeleteSelection(){
  deletedPages.clear();
  document.querySelectorAll('#delp-page-grid .pdf-preview-page').forEach(w=>{
    w.querySelector('input').checked=false;
    w.style.background=''; w.style.borderColor='';
  });
  updateDeleteCount(0);
}
async function doDeletePages(){
  const files=PF['delp'];if(!files||!files[0])return;
  if(deletedPages.size===0){setStatus('delp','err','No pages selected for deletion.');return;}
  setStatus('delp','loading','Deleting pages…');
  document.getElementById('dl-delp').innerHTML='';
  try{
    const {PDFDocument}=PDFLib;
    const src=await PDFDocument.load(await readBuf(files[0]));
    const out=await PDFDocument.create();
    const grid=document.getElementById('delp-page-grid');
    // Use visual order, skipping checked (deleted) pages
    const orderedEls=Array.from(grid.querySelectorAll('.pdf-preview-page'));
    const keepIndices=orderedEls
      .map(el=>parseInt(el.dataset.idx))
      .filter(idx=>!deletedPages.has(idx+1)); // deletedPages uses 1-based
    if(keepIndices.length===0){setStatus('delp','err','Cannot delete all pages.');return;}
    const pgs=await out.copyPages(src,keepIndices);
    pgs.forEach(p=>out.addPage(p));
    const b=await out.save();
    setStatus('delp','ok',`✓ Deleted ${deletedPages.size} page(s) · ${keepIndices.length} remaining`);
    addDL('dl-delp',b,'deleted-pages.pdf','application/pdf');
  }catch(e){setStatus('delp','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

// ─── WORD TO PDF ───
let wordDocContent = '';
let wordDocHTML = '';
let wordDocFileName = '';


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// DOCX NORMALISATION PIPELINE  (browser-side port of normalize_docx.py)
//
// Applied before mammoth parses the file. Fixes the five root causes of
// pagination inflation when converting Word → PDF:
//
//  1. Font substitution  — rewrites Windows fonts to metrically-identical
//                          Linux/cross-platform equivalents so mammoth's
//                          HTML uses fonts the browser actually has.
//  2. Page size detection — reads <w:pgSz> and returns exact dimensions
//                          so the iframe is sized to the document's true
//                          page (Letter vs A4 vs custom).
//  3. Margin detection   — reads <w:pgMar> so the iframe padding matches
//                          the document's actual margins exactly.
//  4. Image EMU clamping — caps <wp:extent> values so no image can be
//                          wider than the content area or taller than one
//                          page, preventing overflow and split images.
//  5. AlternateContent   — injects missing <mc:Fallback/> elements that
//                          crash mammoth v1.6.0.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ── Font substitution map ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// Maps Windows/Office fonts → cross-platform equivalents with identical
// or near-identical advance widths. "Identical metrics" means the same
// character widths, so text reflows IDENTICALLY on any platform.
const W2P_FONT_SUBS = {
  // Perfect metric match — zero reflow guaranteed
  'Calibri':           'Carlito',
  'Cambria':           'Caladea',
  'Cambria Math':      'Caladea',
  // Near-identical — < 1% line-count change
  'Times New Roman':   'Georgia, serif',
  'Arial':             'Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
  'Arial Narrow':      'Arial Narrow, sans-serif',
  'Courier New':       'Courier New, monospace',
  // Acceptable substitutes
  'Tahoma':            'Tahoma, Geneva, sans-serif',
  'Verdana':           'Verdana, Geneva, sans-serif',
  'Georgia':           'Georgia, serif',
  'Palatino Linotype': 'Palatino, "Book Antiqua", serif',
  'Book Antiqua':      'Palatino, "Book Antiqua", serif',
  'Century Gothic':    '"Century Gothic", "Gill Sans", sans-serif',
  'Trebuchet MS':      '"Trebuchet MS", sans-serif',
  'Garamond':          'Garamond, "EB Garamond", serif',
  'Comic Sans MS':     '"Comic Sans MS", cursive',
  'Impact':            'Impact, Charcoal, sans-serif',
};

// ── Page size lookup (twips → pixels @ 96 DPI) ────────────────────────
// 1 twip = 1/1440 inch;  1 inch = 96 CSS px
const W2P_TWIPS_PER_INCH = 1440;
const W2P_DPI = 96;
function w2pTwipsToPx(twips){ return Math.round((twips / W2P_TWIPS_PER_INCH) * W2P_DPI); }

// Word default page/margin dimensions in twips
const W2P_DEFAULTS = {
  pgW: 12240, pgH: 15840,          // Letter: 8.5" × 11"
  margTop: 1440, margRight: 1800,  // 1" top/bottom, 1.25" left/right
  margBottom: 1440, margLeft: 1800,
};

/**
 * Parse the first <w:pgSz> and <w:pgMar> from document.xml.
 * Returns { pageWidthPx, pageHeightPx, margTopPx, margRightPx,
 *           margBottomPx, margLeftPx, isLetter, isA4 }
 */
function w2pDetectLayout(docXmlText) {
  // Page size
  const pgSzM = docXmlText.match(/w:pgSz[^>]*w:w="(\d+)"[^>]*w:h="(\d+)"/);
  const pgSzM2 = pgSzM || docXmlText.match(/w:pgSz[^>]*w:h="(\d+)"[^>]*w:w="(\d+)"/);

  let pgW = W2P_DEFAULTS.pgW, pgH = W2P_DEFAULTS.pgH;
  if(pgSzM){
    pgW = parseInt(pgSzM[1]);
    pgH = parseInt(pgSzM[2]);
  } else if(pgSzM2){
    pgH = parseInt(pgSzM2[1]);
    pgW = parseInt(pgSzM2[2]);
  }

  // Margins — try each attribute individually (order varies per document)
  function readMarg(xml, attr, def){
    const m = xml.match(new RegExp('w:pgMar[^>]*w:'+attr+'="(\\d+)"'));
    return m ? parseInt(m[1]) : def;
  }
  const margTop    = readMarg(docXmlText, 'top',    W2P_DEFAULTS.margTop);
  const margRight  = readMarg(docXmlText, 'right',  W2P_DEFAULTS.margRight);
  const margBottom = readMarg(docXmlText, 'bottom', W2P_DEFAULTS.margBottom);
  const margLeft   = readMarg(docXmlText, 'left',   W2P_DEFAULTS.margLeft);

  const pageWidthPx  = w2pTwipsToPx(pgW);
  const pageHeightPx = w2pTwipsToPx(pgH);

  return {
    pageWidthPx,  pageHeightPx,
    margTopPx:    w2pTwipsToPx(margTop),
    margRightPx:  w2pTwipsToPx(margRight),
    margBottomPx: w2pTwipsToPx(margBottom),
    margLeftPx:   w2pTwipsToPx(margLeft),
    isLetter: Math.abs(pgW - 12240) < 200,
    isA4:     Math.abs(pgW - 11906) < 200,
    twips: { pgW, pgH, margTop, margRight, margBottom, margLeft },
  };
}

/**
 * Substitute font names in an XML string.
 * Returns { xml, count }
 */
function w2pSubstituteFonts(xml){
  let count = 0;
  for(const [orig, sub] of Object.entries(W2P_FONT_SUBS)){
    // Match w:ascii="Calibri", w:hAnsi="Calibri", w:cs="Calibri", w:eastAsia="Calibri"
    // and a:latin typeface="Calibri"  (theme fonts)
    const esc = orig.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
    const re1 = new RegExp(`(w:(?:ascii|hAnsi|cs|eastAsia)=")${esc}(")`, 'g');
    const re2 = new RegExp(`(typeface=")${esc}(")`, 'g');
    const before = xml.length;
    xml = xml.replace(re1, (_, a, b) => { count++; return a + sub + b; });
    xml = xml.replace(re2, (_, a, b) => { count++; return a + sub + b; });
  }
  return { xml, count };
}

/**
 * Clamp <wp:extent cx="..." cy="..."/> EMU values.
 * 914400 EMU = 1 inch.
 * maxWidthIn / maxHeightIn are in inches.
 */
function w2pClampImageEMU(xml, maxWidthIn, maxHeightIn){
  const EMU = 914400;
  const maxW = maxWidthIn  * EMU;
  const maxH = maxHeightIn * EMU;
  let count = 0;
  xml = xml.replace(/<wp:extent\s+cx="(\d+)"\s+cy="(\d+)"\s*\/>/g, (m, cx, cy) => {
    let w = parseInt(cx), h = parseInt(cy);
    if(w <= 0 || h <= 0) return m;
    const scale = Math.min(w > maxW ? maxW/w : 1, h > maxH ? maxH/h : 1);
    if(scale < 1){ count++; return `<wp:extent cx="${Math.round(w*scale)}" cy="${Math.round(h*scale)}" />`; }
    return m;
  });
  // Also handle reversed attribute order
  xml = xml.replace(/<wp:extent\s+cy="(\d+)"\s+cx="(\d+)"\s*\/>/g, (m, cy, cx) => {
    let w = parseInt(cx), h = parseInt(cy);
    if(w <= 0 || h <= 0) return m;
    const scale = Math.min(w > maxW ? maxW/w : 1, h > maxH ? maxH/h : 1);
    if(scale < 1){ count++; return `<wp:extent cy="${Math.round(h*scale)}" cx="${Math.round(w*scale)}" />`; }
    return m;
  });
  return { xml, count };
}

/**
 * Fix missing mc:Fallback elements (mammoth v1.6.0 bug).
 * Same logic as before but now inside the unified pipeline.
 */
function w2pPatchAlternateContent(text){
  if(!text.includes('<mc:AlternateContent')) return text;
  let patched = '', pos = 0;
  const OPEN = '<mc:AlternateContent', CLOSE = '</mc:AlternateContent>';
  while(pos < text.length){
    const start = text.indexOf(OPEN, pos);
    if(start === -1){ patched += text.slice(pos); break; }
    patched += text.slice(pos, start);
    const end = text.indexOf(CLOSE, start);
    if(end === -1){ patched += text.slice(start); break; }
    const block = text.slice(start, end + CLOSE.length);
    patched += block.includes('<mc:Fallback')
      ? block
      : block.slice(0, -CLOSE.length) + '<mc:Fallback/>' + CLOSE;
    pos = end + CLOSE.length;
  }
  return patched;
}

/**
 * Master normalisation function.
 * Accepts an ArrayBuffer (the raw DOCX), returns:
 *   { buffer: ArrayBuffer, layout: {...}, report: {...} }
 *
 * buffer  — patched DOCX ready for mammoth
 * layout  — page dimensions and margins for the iframe
 * report  — counts of changes made (for status messages)
 */
async function normalizeDocxBuffer(arrayBuffer){
  const report = { fontsSubstituted: 0, imagesResized: 0, altContentFixed: 0 };
  let layout = w2pDetectLayout(''); // defaults
  if(typeof JSZip === 'undefined') return { buffer: arrayBuffer, layout, report };

  try{
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(arrayBuffer);

    // ── 1. Read and patch document.xml ──────────────────────────────────
    let docXml = await zip.file('word/document.xml').async('text');

    // Detect layout BEFORE any substitutions (we need the raw twip values)
    layout = w2pDetectLayout(docXml);

    // Compute content-area dimensions for image clamping
    const contentWidthIn  = (layout.twips.pgW - layout.twips.margLeft - layout.twips.margRight) / W2P_TWIPS_PER_INCH;
    const contentHeightIn = (layout.twips.pgH - layout.twips.margTop  - layout.twips.margBottom) / W2P_TWIPS_PER_INCH;

    // Apply fixes
    const fontResult = w2pSubstituteFonts(docXml);
    docXml = fontResult.xml;
    report.fontsSubstituted += fontResult.count;

    const imgResult = w2pClampImageEMU(docXml, contentWidthIn, contentHeightIn - 0.3);
    docXml = imgResult.xml;
    report.imagesResized += imgResult.count;

    const patched = w2pPatchAlternateContent(docXml);
    if(patched !== docXml) report.altContentFixed++;
    docXml = patched;

    zip.file('word/document.xml', docXml);

    // ── 2. Patch styles.xml fonts ────────────────────────────────────────
    const stylesFile = zip.file('word/styles.xml');
    if(stylesFile){
      let stylesXml = await stylesFile.async('text');
      const sr = w2pSubstituteFonts(stylesXml);
      report.fontsSubstituted += sr.count;
      if(sr.count > 0) zip.file('word/styles.xml', sr.xml);
    }

    // ── 3. Patch theme XML fonts ─────────────────────────────────────────
    const themeFiles = Object.keys(zip.files).filter(n => /^word\/theme\/.+\.xml$/i.test(n));
    for(const name of themeFiles){
      const themeFile = zip.file(name);
      if(!themeFile) continue;
      let themeXml = await themeFile.async('text');
      const tr = w2pSubstituteFonts(themeXml);
      report.fontsSubstituted += tr.count;
      if(tr.count > 0) zip.file(name, tr.xml);
    }

    // ── 4. Patch all other XML files for AlternateContent ────────────────
    const xmlFiles = Object.keys(zip.files)
      .filter(n => (n.endsWith('.xml') || n.endsWith('.rels')) && n !== 'word/document.xml');
    for(const name of xmlFiles){
      const f = zip.file(name);
      if(!f) continue;
      let text = await f.async('text');
      if(!text.includes('<mc:AlternateContent')) continue;
      const fixed = w2pPatchAlternateContent(text);
      if(fixed !== text){ zip.file(name, fixed); report.altContentFixed++; }
    }

    const patchedBuffer = await zip.generateAsync({
      type: 'arraybuffer',
      compression: 'DEFLATE',
      compressionOptions: { level: 1 }, // fast — quality doesn't matter here
    });

    return { buffer: patchedBuffer, layout, report };

  }catch(e){
    console.warn('normalizeDocxBuffer failed, using original:', e);
    return { buffer: arrayBuffer, layout, report };
  }
}

// Legacy wrapper kept for any other callers
async function patchDocxForMammoth(arrayBuffer){
  const { buffer } = await normalizeDocxBuffer(arrayBuffer);
  return buffer;
}


async function loadWordToPDF(files){
  if(!files||!files[0])return;
  const file=files[0];
  const ext=file.name.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
  const fl=document.getElementById('fl-w2p');
  fl.innerHTML=`<div class="fchip"><span class="fchip-icon">📝</span><span class="fchip-name">${file.name}</span><span class="fchip-size">${fz(file.size)}</span></div>`;
  document.getElementById('btn-w2p').disabled=false;
  setStatus('w2p','loading','Reading document…');
  wordDocContent=''; wordDocHTML=''; wordDocFileName=file.name;
  try{
    let ab=await readBuf(file);
    if(ext==='docx'||ext==='doc'){
      // Use mammoth.js for proper docx extraction with full fidelity
      if(typeof mammoth!=='undefined'){
        // ── Full normalisation: fonts, page size, margins, image dims, AlternateContent
        setStatus('w2p','loading','Normalising document (fonts, page size, images)…');
        const normResult = await normalizeDocxBuffer(ab);
        ab = normResult.buffer;
        // Store layout so doWordToPDF can size the iframe to match exactly
        window._w2pLayout = normResult.layout;
        const r = normResult.report;
        const normMsg = [
          r.fontsSubstituted  > 0 ? `${r.fontsSubstituted} font(s) substituted`      : '',
          r.imagesResized     > 0 ? `${r.imagesResized} image(s) resized`             : '',
          r.altContentFixed   > 0 ? `${r.altContentFixed} XML block(s) patched`       : '',
        ].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
        if(normMsg) setStatus('w2p','loading', '✓ ' + normMsg + ' — converting…');
        // Convert images to data URIs so they render in the preview/print
        const imgOptions = {
          convertImage: mammoth.images.imgElement(function(image) {
            return image.read('base64').then(function(imageBase64) {
              return { src: 'data:' + image.contentType + ';base64,' + imageBase64 };
            }).catch(function(){ return {}; });
          })
        };
        // PRIMARY: use convertToHtml — captures tables, lists, images, headings
        setStatus('w2p','loading','Converting document…');
        try{
          const htmlResult=await mammoth.convertToHtml({arrayBuffer:ab,...imgOptions});
          wordDocHTML=htmlResult.value||'';
          // Derive plain text from the HTML (preserves table cell text too)
          const tmp=document.createElement('div');
          tmp.innerHTML=wordDocHTML;
          // For tables: join cell text with tabs/newlines for a readable plain-text version
          tmp.querySelectorAll('tr').forEach(tr=>{
            const cells=Array.from(tr.querySelectorAll('td,th')).map(c=>c.textContent.trim());
            const row=document.createTextNode(cells.join('\t')+'\n');
            tr.parentNode.insertBefore(row,tr);
            tr.remove();
          });
          wordDocContent=tmp.textContent||tmp.innerText||'';
        }catch(e2){
          // Fallback if convertToHtml fails — try raw text only
          try{
            const result=await mammoth.extractRawText({arrayBuffer:ab});
            wordDocContent=result.value||'';
          }catch(e3){
            // Last resort: manual XML extraction directly from original bytes
            const origAb = await readBuf(file);
            const decoder=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:false});
            const raw=decoder.decode(new Uint8Array(origAb));
            const paras=raw.match(/<w:p[ >][\s\S]*?<\/w:p>/g)||[];
            wordDocContent=paras.map(p=>{
              const ts=(p.match(/<w:t[^>]*>([^<]*)<\/w:t>/g)||[]).map(m=>m.replace(/<[^>]+>/g,''));
              return ts.join('');
            }).filter(t=>t.trim()).join('\n\n');
          }
        }
      } else {
        // Fallback: manual XML extraction from docx zip
        const decoder=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:false});
        const raw=decoder.decode(new Uint8Array(ab));
        const paras=raw.match(/<w:p[ >][\s\S]*?<\/w:p>/g)||[];
        const paraTexts=paras.map(p=>{
          const ts=(p.match(/<w:t[^>]*>([^<]*)<\/w:t>/g)||[]).map(m=>m.replace(/<[^>]+>/g,''));
          return ts.join('');
        }).filter(t=>t.trim());
        wordDocContent=paraTexts.join('\n\n');
      }
    } else if(ext==='rtf'){
      // Basic RTF text extraction — strip control words
      const decoder=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:false});
      const raw=decoder.decode(new Uint8Array(ab));
      wordDocContent=raw
        .replace(/\{[^{}]*\}/g,' ')
        .replace(/\\[a-z]+\d*\s?/gi,' ')
        .replace(/[{}\\]/g,'')
        .replace(/\s+/g,' ')
        .trim();
    } else if(ext==='txt'||ext==='text'||ext==='md'){
      wordDocContent=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:false}).decode(new Uint8Array(ab));
    } else if(ext==='odt'){
      // ODT is a zip; extract content.xml
      if(typeof JSZip!=='undefined'){
        const zip=await JSZip.loadAsync(ab);
        const contentXml=zip.file('content.xml');
        if(contentXml){
          const xmlStr=await contentXml.async('text');
          wordDocContent=xmlStr.replace(/<[^>]+>/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
        }
      } else {
        wordDocContent='ODT extraction requires JSZip (unavailable).';
      }
    } else {
      // Try UTF-8 text as fallback
      wordDocContent=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:false}).decode(new Uint8Array(ab));
    }

    if(!wordDocContent.trim()&&!wordDocHTML.trim()){
      setStatus('w2p','err','No content could be extracted from this file.');
      document.getElementById('btn-w2p').disabled=true;
      return;
    }
    const preview=document.getElementById('w2p-preview');
    const previewContent=document.getElementById('w2p-preview-content');
    preview.style.display='block';
    if(wordDocHTML){
      // Show styled preview with all HTML including images
      previewContent.innerHTML=wordDocHTML;
    } else {
      previewContent.textContent=wordDocContent.slice(0,3000)+(wordDocContent.length>3000?'\n\n[…more content]':'');
    }
    const _wordCount = wordDocContent.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length.toLocaleString();
    const _layInfo = window._w2pLayout
      ? ` · ${window._w2pLayout.isLetter ? 'Letter' : window._w2pLayout.isA4 ? 'A4' : 'Custom'} (${window._w2pLayout.pageWidthPx}×${window._w2pLayout.pageHeightPx}px)`
      : '';
    setStatus('w2p','ok',`✓ Document read · ${_wordCount} words${_layInfo} · Ready to convert`);
  }catch(e){setStatus('w2p','err','Error reading file: '+e.message);}
}

/**
 * Returns true if a canvas slice is visually blank (all-white or near-white).
 * Samples a grid of pixels; if >99.5% are white/near-white → blank.
 */
function w2pIsBlankCanvas(canvas, sx, sy, sw, sh){
  if(sw <= 0 || sh <= 0) return true;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  // Sample a 20×20 grid across the slice for speed
  const SAMPLES = 20;
  const data = ctx.getImageData(sx, sy, sw, sh).data;
  const W = sw, H = sh;
  let nonWhite = 0;
  const threshold = 245; // allow slight anti-aliasing
  const stepX = Math.max(1, Math.floor(W / SAMPLES));
  const stepY = Math.max(1, Math.floor(H / SAMPLES));
  let total = 0;
  for(let y = 0; y < H; y += stepY){
    for(let x = 0; x < W; x += stepX){
      const i = (y * W + x) * 4;
      if(data[i] < threshold || data[i+1] < threshold || data[i+2] < threshold) nonWhite++;
      total++;
    }
  }
  // Blank if >99.5% white pixels
  return nonWhite / total < 0.005;
}

async function doWordToPDF(){
  if(!wordDocContent.trim()&&!wordDocHTML.trim()){
    setStatus('w2p','err','No content loaded. Please upload a document first.'); return;
  }
  setStatus('w2p','loading','Setting up render engine…');
  document.getElementById('dl-w2p').innerHTML='';

  try{
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // PIPELINE OVERVIEW
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // 1. Inject mammoth HTML into a hidden, A4-width iframe with full CSS.
    // 2. Pre-process every <img> inside the iframe:
    //      a. Enforce a hard max-height so no single image can exceed one
    //         page-content area height → it can NEVER be split.
    //      b. Collect the bounding rect (top, bottom) of every image.
    // 3. Use those bounding rects to compute "safe" page-cut Y positions —
    //    pixel boundaries that do NOT fall inside any image's vertical span.
    // 4. Use html2canvas to capture the entire document in one high-res pass.
    // 5. Cut the canvas at the safe Y positions instead of fixed intervals.
    // 6. Embed each slice as a PDF page via pdf-lib.
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    // ── Layout — use values detected from the DOCX by normalizeDocxBuffer ──
    // Falls back to A4 defaults if the document hasn't been normalised yet.
    const _lay = window._w2pLayout || {};
    const A4_W_PX    = _lay.pageWidthPx   || 794;   // Letter=816, A4=794
    const A4_H_PX    = _lay.pageHeightPx  || 1123;  // Letter=1056, A4=1123
    const MARGIN_L   = _lay.margLeftPx    || 90;
    const MARGIN_R   = _lay.margRightPx   || 90;
    const MARGIN_T   = _lay.margTopPx     || 96;
    const MARGIN_B   = _lay.margBottomPx  || 96;
    const SCALE      = 2;    // 2× capture for print-quality sharpness (≈192 DPI)

    // Use detected margins for content area calculations
    const CONTENT_W_PX  = A4_W_PX - MARGIN_L - MARGIN_R;
    const CONTENT_H_PX  = A4_H_PX - MARGIN_T - MARGIN_B;
    const MAX_IMG_H_PX  = CONTENT_H_PX - 32;   // 32px breathing room
    const MAX_IMG_W_PX  = CONTENT_W_PX;

    // PDF output dimensions in points (1 pt = 1/72 inch)
    // Convert from detected page twips: 1440 twips = 1 inch = 72 pt
    const _tw = (_lay.twips || {});
    const PDF_W = _tw.pgW ? Math.round(_tw.pgW / 1440 * 72) : 595;
    const PDF_H = _tw.pgH ? Math.round(_tw.pgH / 1440 * 72) : 842;
    // Letter=612×792pt, A4=595×842pt

    // ── Build styled HTML ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const docHtml = wordDocHTML ||
      wordDocContent.split('\n').map(l => l.trim() ? `<p>${l}</p>` : '<br>').join('');

    const frameHtml = `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="UTF-8">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Carlito:ital,wght@0,400;0,700;1,400;1,700&family=Caladea:ital,wght@0,400;0,700;1,400;1,700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<style>
/* ── Reset ── */
*, *::before, *::after { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
html, body {
  width: ${A4_W_PX}px;
  background: #ffffff;
  color: #000;
  /* Carlito/Caladea = metrically identical to Calibri/Cambria (zero reflow).
     Loaded via Google Fonts above. Falls back to Calibri if locally installed. */
  font-family: 'Carlito', 'Calibri', 'Caladea', 'Liberation Sans',
               'Helvetica Neue', Arial, sans-serif;
  font-size: 11pt;
  line-height: 1.5;
  -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
  print-color-adjust: exact;
}
body {
  /* Exact margins read from the DOCX <w:pgMar> element */
  padding-top:    ${MARGIN_T}px;
  padding-right:  ${MARGIN_R}px;
  padding-bottom: ${MARGIN_B}px;
  padding-left:   ${MARGIN_L}px;
}

/* ── Headings ── */
h1 { font-size: 22pt; font-weight: 700; margin: 18px 0 10px; color: #1a1a1a;
     break-inside: avoid; page-break-inside: avoid; }
h2 { font-size: 17pt; font-weight: 700; margin: 16px 0 8px;  color: #1a1a1a;
     break-inside: avoid; page-break-inside: avoid; }
h3 { font-size: 13pt; font-weight: 700; margin: 12px 0 6px;  color: #1a1a1a;
     break-inside: avoid; page-break-inside: avoid; }
h4, h5, h6 { font-size: 11pt; font-weight: 700; margin: 10px 0 5px;
              break-inside: avoid; page-break-inside: avoid; }

/* ── Paragraphs ── */
p  { margin: 0 0 8px; orphans: 3; widows: 3; }
/* Preserve mammoth's inline text-align styles */
p[style*="text-align"],
div[style*="text-align"] { /* let inline style win */ }
p[align="center"], div[align="center"] { text-align: center; }
p[align="right"],  div[align="right"]  { text-align: right; }
p[align="justify"],div[align="justify"]{ text-align: justify; }
br { display: block; margin: 4px 0; }

/* ── Lists ── */
ul, ol { margin: 6px 0 10px 26px; padding: 0; }
li     { margin: 2px 0 4px; }
ul li  { list-style-type: disc; }
ol li  { list-style-type: decimal; }
ul ul  { list-style-type: circle; margin-top: 2px; }

/* ── Tables ── */
table {
  border-collapse: collapse;
  width: 100%;
  margin: 10px 0 14px;
  font-size: 10pt;
  table-layout: auto;
}
th, td {
  border: 1px solid #888;
