  padding: 5px 8px;
  vertical-align: top;
  word-break: break-word;
}
th, td[align], th[align] { /* let inline align attributes apply */ }
td[align="center"], th[align="center"] { text-align: center; }
td[align="right"],  th[align="right"]  { text-align: right; }
th { background: #e8e8e8; font-weight: 700; }
tr:nth-child(even) td { background: #f7f7f7; }
/* Preserve explicit widths on table cells */
td[style*="width"], th[style*="width"] { /* let inline width win */ }

/* ── Images ──
   These constraints are enforced AGAIN programmatically after load,
   but setting them in CSS as well ensures the layout is already
   correct before getBoundingClientRect is called. */
img {
  display: block;
  max-width: ${MAX_IMG_W_PX}px !important;
  max-height: ${MAX_IMG_H_PX}px !important;
  width: auto !important;
  height: auto !important;
  margin: 8px auto 12px;
  /* Do NOT use break-inside here — we handle it in JS with safe cuts */
}

/* ── Code ── */
pre, code {
  font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
  font-size: 9pt;
  background: #f4f4f4;
  border: 1px solid #ddd;
  border-radius: 3px;
}
code  { padding: 1px 4px; }
pre   { padding: 10px 14px; margin: 8px 0 12px; white-space: pre-wrap; word-break: break-word; }

/* ── Misc ── */
/* Ensure mammoth's inline font-size, color, and weight spans are respected */
span[style] { /* let inline styles win — do not override */ }
blockquote { border-left: 3px solid #aaa; margin: 8px 0 8px 10px;
             padding: 4px 12px; color: #444; font-style: italic; }
hr  { border: none; border-top: 1px solid #ccc; margin: 12px 0; }
strong, b { font-weight: 700; }
em, i     { font-style: italic; }
u         { text-decoration: underline; }
s, del    { text-decoration: line-through; }
a         { color: #1155cc; text-decoration: underline; }
</style>
</head><body>${docHtml}</body></html>`;

    // ── Mount hidden iframe ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    setStatus('w2p','loading','Rendering document layout…');
    const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
    iframe.style.cssText =
      `position:fixed;top:-99999px;left:-99999px;width:${A4_W_PX}px;border:none;visibility:hidden;`;
    document.body.appendChild(iframe);

    await new Promise(resolve => {
      iframe.onload = resolve;
      iframe.srcdoc = frameHtml;
      setTimeout(resolve, 4000); // hard timeout
    });

    const iframeDoc = iframe.contentDocument || iframe.contentWindow.document;

    // ── Step 1: Wait for all images to load ───────────────────────────────
    const allImgs = Array.from(iframeDoc.querySelectorAll('img'));
    if(allImgs.length){
      setStatus('w2p','loading',`Loading ${allImgs.length} image(s)…`);
      await Promise.allSettled(allImgs.map(img =>
        img.complete ? Promise.resolve() :
        new Promise(r => { img.onload = img.onerror = r; setTimeout(r, 5000); })
      ));
    }

    // ── Step 2: Enforce per-image size caps BEFORE measuring ──────────────
    // If an image is taller than MAX_IMG_H_PX, it would inevitably be split
    // no matter how we cut pages.  Force it down to fit.
    for(const img of allImgs){
      const nw = img.naturalWidth  || img.width  || 1;
      const nh = img.naturalHeight || img.height || 1;
      const aspect = nw / nh;

      let tw = Math.min(nw, MAX_IMG_W_PX);
      let th = tw / aspect;
      if(th > MAX_IMG_H_PX){
        th = MAX_IMG_H_PX;
        tw = th * aspect;
      }
      img.style.setProperty('width',  Math.round(tw) + 'px', 'important');
      img.style.setProperty('height', Math.round(th) + 'px', 'important');
      img.style.setProperty('max-width',  Math.round(tw) + 'px', 'important');
      img.style.setProperty('max-height', Math.round(th) + 'px', 'important');
    }

    // Force a reflow so getBoundingClientRect is accurate after resizing
    void iframeDoc.body.offsetHeight;

    // ── Step 3: Collect element bounding rects ────────────────────────────
    // We collect rects for images AND for table rows (a row should not be
    // split if it's short enough to fit on a page).
    // Each entry: { top, bottom } in body-local pixel coordinates.
    const protectedRects = [];

    const bodyTop = iframeDoc.body.getBoundingClientRect().top;

    function addProtected(el){
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const top    = r.top    - bodyTop;
      const bottom = r.bottom - bodyTop;
      if(bottom > top) protectedRects.push({ top, bottom });
    }

    // Protect every image
    for(const img of allImgs) addProtected(img);

    // Protect table rows that are short enough to fit on one page
    for(const tr of iframeDoc.querySelectorAll('tr')){
      const r = tr.getBoundingClientRect();
      const h = r.bottom - r.top;
      if(h > 0 && h <= CONTENT_H_PX) addProtected(tr);
    }

    // ── Step 4: Resize iframe to full content height ───────────────────────
    const totalH = Math.max(
      iframeDoc.body.scrollHeight,
      iframeDoc.body.offsetHeight,
      iframeDoc.documentElement.scrollHeight
    );

    if(!totalH){
      document.body.removeChild(iframe);
      setStatus('w2p','err','Document rendered empty. Please try again.');
      return;
    }
    iframe.style.height = totalH + 'px';

    // ── Step 5: Compute safe page-cut positions ────────────────────────────
    //
    // Algorithm:
    //   • Start with naive cut at each A4_H_PX boundary.
    //   • For each naive cut Y, check whether any protected rect straddles it
    //     (i.e. rect.top < Y < rect.bottom).
    //   • If yes, move the cut UP to rect.top (start a new page just before
    //     the protected element).
    //   • If that would make the page too short (< MIN_PAGE_H), move it DOWN
    //     to rect.bottom instead (accept the element starting near page top).
    //   • Clamp to avoid infinite loops.
    //
    const MIN_PAGE_H = Math.round(A4_H_PX * 0.25); // never cut a page shorter than 25%

    function findSafeCut(naiveCut, currentPageStart){
      // Is this naive cut safe?  Check every protected rect.
      const straddling = protectedRects.filter(
        r => r.top < naiveCut && r.bottom > naiveCut
      );
      if(straddling.length === 0) return naiveCut; // already safe

      // Find the element that starts latest before the cut — move cut to its top.
      const bestTop = Math.max(...straddling.map(r => r.top));
      const candidate = bestTop;

      // Would this make the page too short?
      if(candidate - currentPageStart >= MIN_PAGE_H) return candidate;

      // Too short — push cut to after the element ends instead.
      const bestBottom = Math.min(...straddling.map(r => r.bottom));
      return bestBottom;
    }

    const pageCuts = []; // Y positions (in rendered body px) where pages end
    let pageStart = 0;
    while(pageStart < totalH){
      const naiveCut = pageStart + A4_H_PX;
      if(naiveCut >= totalH){
        pageCuts.push(totalH); // last page
        break;
      }
      const safeCut = findSafeCut(naiveCut, pageStart);
      // Guard against infinite loop: always advance at least MIN_PAGE_H
      const finalCut = Math.max(safeCut, pageStart + MIN_PAGE_H);
      pageCuts.push(finalCut);
      pageStart = finalCut;
    }

    // ── Step 6: Capture the full document via html2canvas ─────────────────
    setStatus('w2p','loading','Capturing document at print resolution…');
    if(typeof html2canvas === 'undefined'){
      document.body.removeChild(iframe);
      setStatus('w2p','err','html2canvas failed to load. Check your connection and refresh.');
      return;
    }

    let fullCanvas;
    try{
      fullCanvas = await html2canvas(iframeDoc.body, {
        scale: SCALE,
        useCORS: true,
        allowTaint: true,
        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
        logging: false,
        width:        A4_W_PX,
        height:       totalH,
        windowWidth:  A4_W_PX,
        windowHeight: totalH,
        scrollX: 0,
        scrollY: 0,
        foreignObjectRendering: false,
      });
    }catch(canvasErr){
      document.body.removeChild(iframe);
      setStatus('w2p','err','Capture error: ' + canvasErr.message);
      return;
    }
    document.body.removeChild(iframe);

    // ── Step 7: Slice the canvas at safe cuts and build PDF ───────────────
    setStatus('w2p','loading','Paginating with image-safe cuts…');
    const {PDFDocument} = PDFLib;
    const pdfDoc = await PDFDocument.create();
    const totalScaledW = fullCanvas.width; // = A4_W_PX * SCALE

    let prevCutPx = 0; // in scaled (canvas) pixels
    let blankPagesSkipped = 0;
    for(let p = 0; p < pageCuts.length; p++){
      setStatus('w2p','loading',`Building page ${p+1} of ${pageCuts.length}…`);

      const cutPx      = Math.round(pageCuts[p] * SCALE);
      const sliceTop   = prevCutPx;
      const sliceH     = Math.min(cutPx - sliceTop, fullCanvas.height - sliceTop);
      if(sliceH <= 0){ prevCutPx = cutPx; continue; }

      // ── Blank page detection: skip entirely-white slices ─────────────────
      if(w2pIsBlankCanvas(fullCanvas, 0, sliceTop, totalScaledW, sliceH)){
        blankPagesSkipped++;
        prevCutPx = cutPx;
        continue;
      }

      // The slice may be shorter than a full A4 page if a safe-cut moved
      // it; we pad the remainder with white.
      const pageCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      pageCanvas.width  = totalScaledW;
      pageCanvas.height = Math.round(A4_H_PX * SCALE); // always full A4
      const ctx = pageCanvas.getContext('2d');
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, pageCanvas.width, pageCanvas.height);
      ctx.drawImage(
        fullCanvas,
        0, sliceTop, totalScaledW, sliceH,  // src
        0, 0,        totalScaledW, sliceH   // dst (top-aligned; white below)
      );

      // PNG → pdf-lib
      const dataUrl = pageCanvas.toDataURL('image/png');
      const b64     = dataUrl.split(',')[1];
      const bin     = atob(b64);
      const bytes   = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
      for(let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) bytes[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);

      const pngImg = await pdfDoc.embedPng(bytes);
      const page   = pdfDoc.addPage([PDF_W, PDF_H]);
      page.drawImage(pngImg, { x:0, y:0, width:PDF_W, height:PDF_H });

      prevCutPx = cutPx;
    }

    const pdfBytes = await pdfDoc.save();
    const baseName = wordDocFileName ? wordDocFileName.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '') : 'converted';
    const finalPageCount = pdfDoc.getPageCount();
    const pgLabel  = finalPageCount === 1 ? '1 page' : `${finalPageCount} pages`;
    const blankMsg = blankPagesSkipped > 0
      ? ` · ${blankPagesSkipped} blank page${blankPagesSkipped>1?'s':''} removed`
      : '';
    setStatus('w2p','ok',
      `✓ PDF created · ${pgLabel}${blankMsg} · no images split across pages · ${fz(pdfBytes.length)}`
    );
    addDL('dl-w2p', pdfBytes, baseName + '.pdf', 'application/pdf');

  }catch(e){
    setStatus('w2p','err','Error: ' + e.message);
    console.error('doWordToPDF:', e);
    document.querySelectorAll('iframe[style*="-99999px"]').forEach(f => f.remove());
  }
}

function clearWordToPDF(){
  wordDocContent=''; wordDocHTML=''; wordDocFileName='';
  document.getElementById('fl-w2p').innerHTML='';
  document.getElementById('w2p-preview').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('btn-w2p').disabled=true;
  document.getElementById('dl-w2p').innerHTML='';
  clearStatus('w2p');
}

// ─── PDF TO WORD (.docx generation) ───
async function doPDFtoWord(){
  const files=PF['p2w'];if(!files||!files[0])return;
  setStatus('p2w','loading','Rendering PDF pages (preserving all images & scans)…');
  document.getElementById('dl-p2w').innerHTML='';
  try{
    pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
    const ab=await readBuf(files[0]);
    const pdf=await pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:new Uint8Array(ab),verbosity:0,cMapUrl:'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/cmaps/',cMapPacked:true}).promise;

    // ── Strategy: render every page to a high-res PNG canvas ──
    // Preserves exact page dimensions, text size, alignment and rendering.
    // Each page is rendered at 2× resolution and embedded as a full-bleed
    // image into a Word section sized to exactly match the original PDF page.

    const escape=s=>s.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
    const RENDER_SCALE=2; // 2× for sharp print quality
    // PDF points → twips: 1 pt = 1/72 inch, 1 twip = 1/1440 inch → 1 pt = 20 twips
    const PT_TO_TWIP=20;
    // EMU per point: 1 inch = 914400 EMU, 1 pt = 914400/72 = 12700 EMU
    const PT_TO_EMU=12700;
    const pageImages=[]; // {b64, ptW, ptH, textRuns}

    for(let i=1;i<=pdf.numPages;i++){
      setStatus('p2w','loading',`Rendering page ${i} of ${pdf.numPages}…`);
      const page=await pdf.getPage(i);
      // Viewport at scale=1 gives dimensions in PDF points (true page size)
      const vpPt=page.getViewport({scale:1});
      const ptW=vpPt.width, ptH=vpPt.height;
      // Render at 2× for sharpness
      const vp=page.getViewport({scale:RENDER_SCALE});
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width=vp.width; canvas.height=vp.height;
      const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
      ctx.fillStyle='#ffffff';
      ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
      await page.render({canvasContext:ctx,viewport:vp}).promise;
      const dataUrl=canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
      const b64=dataUrl.split(',')[1];
      let textRuns=[];
      try{
        const tc=await page.getTextContent({includeMarkedContent:false});
        textRuns=tc.items.filter(it=>it.str&&it.str.trim());
      }catch(e){}
      pageImages.push({b64,ptW,ptH,widthPx:vp.width,heightPx:vp.height,textRuns});
    }

    if(!pageImages.length){setStatus('p2w','err','Could not render any pages.');return;}

    // ── Build OOXML docx: each page gets its own section sized to the PDF page ──
    // Zero margins so the image fills the page exactly, matching original layout.
    const zip=new JSZip();
    const imgRels=[];

    pageImages.forEach((pg,idx)=>{
      const rId=`rId${idx+2}`;
      const imgName=`image${idx+1}.png`;
      imgRels.push({rId,imgName,ptW:pg.ptW,ptH:pg.ptH});
      const binStr=atob(pg.b64);
      const bytes=new Uint8Array(binStr.length);
      for(let b=0;b<binStr.length;b++) bytes[b]=binStr.charCodeAt(b);
      zip.file(`word/media/${imgName}`,bytes);
    });

    // Build paragraph+section XML for each page
    // Each page: zero-margin section sized exactly to the PDF page, image fills it fully
    const paraXml=pageImages.map((pg,idx)=>{
      const rId=imgRels[idx].rId;
      const imgName=imgRels[idx].imgName;
      const imgId=idx+1;
      // Image EMU = page points × PT_TO_EMU (fills 100% of page, zero margin)
      const emuW=Math.round(pg.ptW*PT_TO_EMU);
      const emuH=Math.round(pg.ptH*PT_TO_EMU);
      // Page size in twips
      const pgWTwip=Math.round(pg.ptW*PT_TO_TWIP);
      const pgHTwip=Math.round(pg.ptH*PT_TO_TWIP);
      // Landscape flag
      const orient=pg.ptW>pg.ptH?` w:orient="landscape"`:'';
      const drawing=`<w:drawing>
  <wp:inline xmlns:wp="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/wordprocessingDrawing" distT="0" distB="0" distL="0" distR="0">
    <wp:extent cx="${emuW}" cy="${emuH}"/>
    <wp:docPr id="${imgId}" name="Page${imgId}"/>
    <a:graphic xmlns:a="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/main">
      <a:graphicData uri="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/picture">
        <pic:pic xmlns:pic="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/picture">
          <pic:nvPicPr><pic:cNvPr id="${imgId}" name="Page${imgId}"/><pic:cNvPicPr/></pic:nvPicPr>
          <pic:blipFill>
            <a:blip r:embed="${rId}" xmlns:r="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships"/>
            <a:stretch><a:fillRect/></a:stretch>
          </pic:blipFill>
          <pic:spPr><a:xfrm><a:off x="0" y="0"/><a:ext cx="${emuW}" cy="${emuH}"/></a:xfrm>
            <a:prstGeom prst="rect"><a:avLst/></a:prstGeom>
          </pic:spPr>
        </pic:pic>
      </a:graphicData>
    </a:graphic>
  </wp:inline>
</w:drawing>`;
      // Hidden text runs for searchability (invisible white text, 1pt)
      const hiddenText=pg.textRuns.slice(0,30).map(it=>`<w:r><w:rPr><w:color w:val="FFFFFF"/><w:sz w:val="2"/><w:szCs w:val="2"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">${escape(it.str)}</w:t></w:r>`).join('');
      // Each page is its own section with exact page dimensions and zero margins
      // sectPr inside the paragraph = section break after this page (except last page uses body sectPr)
      const sectPr=`<w:sectPr>
  <w:pgSz w:w="${pgWTwip}" w:h="${pgHTwip}"${orient}/>
  <w:pgMar w:top="0" w:right="0" w:bottom="0" w:left="0" w:header="0" w:footer="0" w:gutter="0"/>
</w:sectPr>`;
      return `<w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:before="0" w:after="0"/><w:jc w:val="left"/>${idx<pageImages.length-1?sectPr:''}</w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:noProof/></w:rPr>${drawing}</w:r>${hiddenText}</w:p>`;
    }).join('\n');

    // Last page section goes in body sectPr
    const lastPg=pageImages[pageImages.length-1];
    const lastWTwip=Math.round(lastPg.ptW*PT_TO_TWIP);
    const lastHTwip=Math.round(lastPg.ptH*PT_TO_TWIP);
    const lastOrient=lastPg.ptW>lastPg.ptH?` w:orient="landscape"`:'';

    // Word relationships for images
    const relEntries=imgRels.map(r=>`<Relationship Id="${r.rId}" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/image" Target="media/${r.imgName}"/>`).join('');

    const xmlContent=`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<w:document xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main"
  xmlns:wp="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/wordprocessingDrawing"
  xmlns:r="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships"
  xmlns:a="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/main"
  xmlns:pic="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/picture">
<w:body>
${paraXml}
<w:sectPr>
  <w:pgSz w:w="${lastWTwip}" w:h="${lastHTwip}"${lastOrient}/>
  <w:pgMar w:top="0" w:right="0" w:bottom="0" w:left="0" w:header="0" w:footer="0" w:gutter="0"/>
</w:sectPr>
</w:body></w:document>`;

    const stylesXml=`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><w:styles xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main"><w:docDefaults><w:rPrDefault><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:sz w:val="24"/></w:rPr></w:rPrDefault></w:docDefaults><w:style w:type="paragraph" w:default="1" w:styleId="Normal"><w:name w:val="Normal"/></w:style></w:styles>`;
    const relsContent=`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships"><Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" Target="word/document.xml"/></Relationships>`;
    const wordRelsContent=`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships"><Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/styles" Target="styles.xml"/>${relEntries}</Relationships>`;
    const contentTypesContent=`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types"><Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/><Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/><Default Extension="png" ContentType="image/png"/><Override PartName="/word/document.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document.main+xml"/><Override PartName="/word/styles.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.styles+xml"/></Types>`;

    zip.file('[Content_Types].xml',contentTypesContent);
    zip.file('_rels/.rels',relsContent);
    zip.file('word/document.xml',xmlContent);
    zip.file('word/styles.xml',stylesXml);
    zip.file('word/_rels/document.xml.rels',wordRelsContent);
    const blob=await zip.generateAsync({type:'uint8array',compression:'DEFLATE',compressionOptions:{level:6}});
    const baseName=files[0].name.replace(/\.pdf$/i,'');
    setStatus('p2w','ok',`✓ Converted ${pdf.numPages} page(s) — all images, scans & visuals fully preserved · ${fz(blob.length)}`);
    addDL('dl-p2w',blob,baseName+'_converted.docx','application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document');
  }catch(e){setStatus('p2w','err','Error: '+e.message);console.error(e);}
}

// ─── PDF TO TEXT ───
async function doPDFtoText(){
  const files=PF['p2t'];if(!files||!files[0])return;
  setStatus('p2t','loading','Extracting text…');
  document.getElementById('dl-p2t').innerHTML='';
  try{
    pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
    const ab=await readBuf(files[0]);
    const pdf=await pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:new Uint8Array(ab),verbosity:0,cMapUrl:'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/cmaps/',cMapPacked:true}).promise;
    let fullText='';
    for(let i=1;i<=pdf.numPages;i++){
      setStatus('p2t','loading',`Extracting page ${i} of ${pdf.numPages}…`);
      const page=await pdf.getPage(i);
      const tc=await page.getTextContent();
      let pt='';
      // Preserve line breaks using transform y-positions
      let lastY=null;
      tc.items.forEach(item=>{
        if(!item.str)return;
        const y=item.transform?item.transform[5]:null;
        if(lastY!==null&&y!==null&&Math.abs(y-lastY)>5) pt+='\n';
        pt+=item.str+(item.hasEOL?'\n':' ');
        lastY=y;
      });
      fullText+=`--- Page ${i} ---\n${pt.trim()}\n\n`;
    }
    const out=document.getElementById('p2t-output');
    const ta=document.getElementById('p2t-text');
    out.style.display='block'; ta.value=fullText;
    const bytes=new TextEncoder().encode(fullText);
    setStatus('p2t','ok',`✓ Extracted ${pdf.numPages} page(s) · ${fullText.length.toLocaleString()} characters`);
    addDL('dl-p2t',bytes,'extracted-text.txt','text/plain');
  }catch(e){setStatus('p2t','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════
// PDF EDITOR
// ═══════════════════════════════════════
let editorTool = 'select';
let editorColor = '#1a1c22';
let editorSize = 4;
let editorFontFamily = 'Helvetica';
let isDrawing = false;
let editorAnnotations = {}; // pageNum -> [{type, ...}]
let editorTextItems = {};   // pageNum -> [{str, x, y, w, h, fontSize, fontName, transform}]
let editorPDF = null;
let editorNumPages = 0;
let editorCurrentFile = null;
let editorPageViewports = {}; // pageNum -> viewport (scale factor info)

function getPDFJSDoc(data) {
  // Always use workerSrc + suppress font warnings
  pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
  return pdfjsLib.getDocument({
    data,
    verbosity: 0,           // suppress TT/font warnings
    cMapUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/cmaps/',
    cMapPacked: true,
    standardFontDataUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/standard_fonts/',
  }).promise;
}

async function loadPDFEditor(files) {
  if (!files || !files[0]) return;
  editorCurrentFile = files[0];
  setStatus('edit', 'loading', 'Loading PDF…');
  document.getElementById('pdf-edit-upload-card').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('pdf-editor-container').style.display = 'block';

  const ab = await readBuf(editorCurrentFile);
  editorPDF = await getPDFJSDoc(new Uint8Array(ab));
  editorNumPages = editorPDF.numPages;
  editorAnnotations = {};
  editorTextItems = {};
  editorPageViewports = {};

  const viewport = document.getElementById('pdf-editor-viewport');
  viewport.innerHTML = '';

  for (let i = 1; i <= editorNumPages; i++) {
    editorAnnotations[i] = [];
    await renderEditorPage(i);
  }

  document.getElementById('pdf-editor-info').textContent =
    editorCurrentFile.name + ' · ' + editorNumPages + ' page(s)';
  setStatus('edit', 'ok', '');

  // Detect dominant font from page 1 and set toolbar default
  detectAndSetDefaultFont();
}

async function detectAndSetDefaultFont() {
  if (!editorPDF) return;
  try {
    const page = await editorPDF.getPage(1);
    const tc = await page.getTextContent({ includeMarkedContent: false });
    // Gather font names from text items
    const fontCounts = {};
    tc.items.forEach(item => {
      if (item.fontName) {
        fontCounts[item.fontName] = (fontCounts[item.fontName] || 0) + (item.str || '').length;
      }
    });
    if (Object.keys(fontCounts).length === 0) return;
    // Pick the most-used font by character count
    const dominant = Object.entries(fontCounts).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0][0];
    // Map PDF internal font name to a CSS/toolbar font
    const mapped = mapPDFFontToCSS(dominant);
    if (mapped) {
      editorFontFamily = mapped;
      const sel = document.getElementById('edit-font-family');
      if (sel) {
        // Try to find it in options
        for (let i = 0; i < sel.options.length; i++) {
          if (sel.options[i].value === mapped) { sel.selectedIndex = i; break; }
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
}

function mapPDFFontToCSS(pdfFont) {
  if (!pdfFont) return null;
  const f = pdfFont.toLowerCase();
  if (f.includes('helvetica') || f.includes('arial') || f.includes('sans')) return 'Helvetica';
  if (f.includes('times') || f.includes('serif') && !f.includes('sans')) return 'Times New Roman';
  if (f.includes('courier') || f.includes('mono')) return 'Courier New';
  if (f.includes('georgia')) return 'Georgia';
  if (f.includes('palatino') || f.includes('palatin')) return 'Palatino Linotype';
  if (f.includes('garamond')) return 'Garamond';
  if (f.includes('verdana')) return 'Verdana';
  if (f.includes('tahoma')) return 'Tahoma';
  if (f.includes('trebuchet')) return 'Trebuchet MS';
  if (f.includes('impact')) return 'Impact';
  if (f.includes('comic')) return 'Comic Sans MS';
  if (f.includes('gothic') || f.includes('century')) return 'Century Gothic';
  if (f.includes('lucida')) return 'Lucida Sans';
  return null;
}

async function renderEditorPage(pageNum) {
  const page = await editorPDF.getPage(pageNum);
  const vp = page.getViewport({ scale: 1.4 });
  editorPageViewports[pageNum] = vp;

  // Extract existing text items for "Edit Text" tool
  const tc = await page.getTextContent({ includeMarkedContent: false });
  editorTextItems[pageNum] = [];
  tc.items.forEach(item => {
    if (!item.str || !item.str.trim()) return;
    // item.transform = [scaleX, skewY, skewX, scaleY, offsetX, offsetY]
    const [sx, , , sy, tx, ty] = item.transform;
    const scaledFontSize = Math.abs(sy) * vp.scale;
    // Convert PDF coords (bottom-left origin) to canvas coords (top-left origin)
    const canvasX = tx * vp.scale;
    const canvasY = vp.height - ty * vp.scale - scaledFontSize;
    const canvasW = (item.width || 0) * vp.scale + 4;
    const canvasH = scaledFontSize * 1.4;
    editorTextItems[pageNum].push({
      str: item.str,
      x: canvasX,
      y: canvasY,
      w: canvasW,
      h: canvasH,
      fontSize: scaledFontSize,
      fontName: item.fontName || '',
      // Original PDF coords for save
      pdfX: tx,
      pdfY: ty,
      pdfFontSize: Math.abs(sy),
      transform: item.transform,
      hidden: false, // set true when user edits it (we cover with an overlay)
    });
  });

  // Build DOM
  const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
  wrapper.className = 'pdf-page-wrapper';
  wrapper.id = `editor-page-${pageNum}`;
  wrapper.style.width = vp.width + 'px';
  wrapper.style.height = vp.height + 'px';

  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = vp.width; canvas.height = vp.height;
  canvas.style.display = 'block';
  await page.render({ canvasContext: canvas.getContext('2d'), viewport: vp }).promise;

  // Drawing canvas overlay
  const drawCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  drawCanvas.className = 'pdf-drawing-canvas';
  drawCanvas.width = vp.width; drawCanvas.height = vp.height;
  drawCanvas.id = `draw-canvas-${pageNum}`;

  // Annotation layer (for text boxes)
  const annLayer = document.createElement('div');
  annLayer.className = 'pdf-annotation-layer interactive';
  annLayer.id = `ann-layer-${pageNum}`;

  // Text-hit overlay (invisible hit areas over existing PDF text)
  const textHitLayer = document.createElement('div');
  textHitLayer.className = 'pdf-annotation-layer';
  textHitLayer.id = `text-hit-${pageNum}`;
  textHitLayer.style.pointerEvents = 'none'; // toggled by tool
  buildTextHitAreas(textHitLayer, pageNum);

  wrapper.appendChild(canvas);
  wrapper.appendChild(drawCanvas);
  wrapper.appendChild(textHitLayer);
  wrapper.appendChild(annLayer);

  const label = document.createElement('div');
  label.className = 'pdf-page-num';
  label.textContent = 'Page ' + pageNum;

  const container = document.createElement('div');
  container.style.cssText = 'display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center';
  container.appendChild(wrapper);
  container.appendChild(label);
  document.getElementById('pdf-editor-viewport').appendChild(container);

  setupPageInteractions(wrapper, drawCanvas, annLayer, textHitLayer, pageNum, vp.width, vp.height);
}

function buildTextHitAreas(layer, pageNum) {
  layer.innerHTML = '';
  (editorTextItems[pageNum] || []).forEach((item, idx) => {
    if (item.hidden) return;
    const hit = document.createElement('div');
    hit.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${item.x}px;top:${item.y}px;width:${Math.max(item.w,20)}px;height:${Math.max(item.h,14)}px;cursor:text;box-sizing:border-box;`;
    hit.dataset.itemIdx = idx;
    hit.dataset.page = pageNum;
    // Hover highlight
    hit.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
      if (editorTool === 'edit-text') hit.style.outline = '2px solid rgba(232,80,58,0.5)';
    });
    hit.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { hit.style.outline = ''; });
    hit.addEventListener('click', e => {
      if (editorTool !== 'edit-text') return;
      e.stopPropagation();
      openExistingTextEditor(pageNum, idx, hit);
    });
    layer.appendChild(hit);
  });
}

function openExistingTextEditor(pageNum, itemIdx, hitEl) {
  const item = editorTextItems[pageNum][itemIdx];
  if (!item) return;

  // Prevent double-editing same item
  if (item.hidden) return;

  // Set toolbar to match the item's font
  const mappedFont = mapPDFFontToCSS(item.fontName) || editorFontFamily;
  editorFontFamily = mappedFont;
  const fontSel = document.getElementById('edit-font-family');
  if (fontSel) {
    for (let i = 0; i < fontSel.options.length; i++) {
      if (fontSel.options[i].value === mappedFont) { fontSel.selectedIndex = i; break; }
    }
  }
  const fsSel = document.getElementById('edit-font-size');
  const roundedFS = Math.round(item.fontSize);
  if (fsSel) {
    let best = 0, bestDiff = Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < fsSel.options.length; i++) {
      const diff = Math.abs(parseInt(fsSel.options[i].value) - roundedFS);
      if (diff < bestDiff) { bestDiff = diff; best = i; }
    }
    fsSel.selectedIndex = best;
  }

  // Mark original as hidden
  item.hidden = true;
  hitEl.style.visibility = 'hidden';

  // ── Immediately erase the original text on the draw canvas ──
  // This makes it look like you're editing in place, not overlaying
  const drawCanvas = document.getElementById(`draw-canvas-${pageNum}`);
  const vp = editorPageViewports[pageNum];
  if (drawCanvas && vp) {
    const dc = drawCanvas.getContext('2d');
    // White-out rectangle slightly larger than the text item
    const padding = 2;
    dc.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    dc.fillRect(
      item.x - padding,
      item.y - padding,
      Math.max(item.w, 40) + padding * 4,
      item.h + padding * 3
    );
    // Mark this white-out as a saved annotation too so it persists in the PDF
    // (the PDF save already handles this via item.hidden, so just keep canvas in sync)
  }

  // Create editable overlay exactly over the original text
  const annLayer = document.getElementById(`ann-layer-${pageNum}`);
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'pdf-text-input editing';
  el.contentEditable = 'true';
  el.spellcheck = false;
  el.dataset.placeholder = 'false';
  el.style.left = item.x + 'px';
  el.style.top = item.y + 'px';
  el.style.minWidth = Math.max(item.w + 8, 60) + 'px';
  el.style.fontSize = item.fontSize + 'px';
  el.style.fontFamily = mappedFont + ', sans-serif';
  el.style.lineHeight = '1.2';
  // Use the page's background color (white) so it truly replaces the text
  el.style.background = '#ffffff';
  el.style.border = '2px solid rgba(163,255,79,0.8)';
  el.style.color = '#111111';
  el.style.padding = '1px 3px';
  el.textContent = item.str;

  // Track as annotation
  const ann = { type: 'textEl', el, isExistingText: true, originalItem: item, itemIdx, pageNum };
  editorAnnotations[pageNum].push(ann);

  makeDraggable(el, pageNum);
  el.addEventListener('click', e => e.stopPropagation());
  el.addEventListener('mousedown', e => e.stopPropagation());
  el.addEventListener('contextmenu', e => {
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    el.remove();
    item.hidden = false;
    hitEl.style.visibility = '';
    // Restore canvas (redraw the page canvas for that region)
    if (drawCanvas) {
      const dc = drawCanvas.getContext('2d');
      // Clear the white-out by clearing that region on draw canvas
      dc.clearRect(item.x - 4, item.y - 4, Math.max(item.w, 40) + 12, item.h + 12);
    }
    buildTextHitAreas(document.getElementById(`text-hit-${pageNum}`), pageNum);
    editorAnnotations[pageNum] = editorAnnotations[pageNum].filter(a => a.el !== el);
  });

  annLayer.appendChild(el);

  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    el.focus();
    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(el);
    const sel = window.getSelection();
    if (sel) { sel.removeAllRanges(); sel.addRange(range); }
  });

  setHint('Type to replace text · Right-click to cancel · Save PDF when done');
}

function makeDraggable(el, pageNum) {
  let isDrag = false, dragOX, dragOY;

  el.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
    // Only drag in select mode; in text mode let normal typing work
    if (editorTool !== 'select') return;
    isDrag = true;
    dragOX = e.clientX - parseFloat(el.style.left || 0);
    dragOY = e.clientY - parseFloat(el.style.top || 0);
    el.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
    e.stopPropagation();
    e.preventDefault();
  });

  const onMove = e => {
    if (!isDrag) return;
    el.style.left = (e.clientX - dragOX) + 'px';
    el.style.top  = (e.clientY - dragOY) + 'px';
  };
  const onUp = () => {
    if (!isDrag) return;
    isDrag = false;
    el.style.cursor = 'text';
  };

  document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
  document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
}

function setupPageInteractions(wrapper, drawCanvas, annLayer, textHitLayer, pageNum, w, h) {
  const ctx = drawCanvas.getContext('2d');
  let startX, startY, tempPath = [];

  function getPos(e) {
    const r = wrapper.getBoundingClientRect();
    const scaleX = w / r.width, scaleY = h / r.height;
    const clientX = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;
    const clientY = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;
    return { x: (clientX - r.left) * scaleX, y: (clientY - r.top) * scaleY };
  }

  // Single unified click handler on wrapper for text tool
  function handleClick(e) {
    if (editorTool !== 'text') return;
    // Don't trigger if click was on an existing text input
    if (e.target.classList.contains('pdf-text-input') || e.target.closest('.pdf-text-input')) return;
    e.stopPropagation();
    e.preventDefault();
    const pos = getPos(e);
    addTextAnnotation(annLayer, pageNum, pos.x, pos.y);
  }

  function handleDown(e) {
    const nonDrawTools = ['select', 'text', 'edit-text', 'signature'];
    if (nonDrawTools.includes(editorTool)) return;
    const pos = getPos(e);
    startX = pos.x; startY = pos.y;
    isDrawing = true;
    if (editorTool === 'draw') {
      tempPath = [pos];
      drawCanvas.style.pointerEvents = 'all';
    }
    e.preventDefault();
  }

  function handleMove(e) {
    if (!isDrawing) return;
    const pos = getPos(e);
    if (editorTool === 'draw') {
      tempPath.push(pos);
      redrawPage(ctx, pageNum, w, h, tempPath);
    } else if (editorTool === 'highlight' || editorTool === 'rect' || editorTool === 'arrow') {
      redrawPage(ctx, pageNum, w, h, null, { tool: editorTool, sx: startX, sy: startY, ex: pos.x, ey: pos.y });
    }
  }

  function handleUp(e) {
    if (!isDrawing) return;
    isDrawing = false;
    drawCanvas.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
    const pos = getPos(e);
    if (editorTool === 'draw' && tempPath.length > 1) {
      editorAnnotations[pageNum].push({ type: 'draw', path: [...tempPath], color: editorColor, size: editorSize });
      tempPath = [];
    } else if (editorTool === 'highlight') {
      const rw = pos.x - startX, rh = pos.y - startY;
      if (Math.abs(rw) > 5 && Math.abs(rh) > 5)
        editorAnnotations[pageNum].push({ type: 'highlight', x: startX, y: startY, w: rw, h: rh });
    } else if (editorTool === 'rect') {
      editorAnnotations[pageNum].push({ type: 'rect', x: startX, y: startY, w: pos.x - startX, h: pos.y - startY, color: editorColor, size: editorSize });
    } else if (editorTool === 'arrow') {
      editorAnnotations[pageNum].push({ type: 'arrow', sx: startX, sy: startY, ex: pos.x, ey: pos.y, color: editorColor, size: editorSize });
    }
    redrawPage(ctx, pageNum, w, h);
  }

  // Text tool: use click (not mousedown) so text inputs can handle their own events first
  wrapper.addEventListener('click', handleClick);
  annLayer.addEventListener('click', handleClick);

  // Draw tools: use mousedown/move/up
  wrapper.addEventListener('mousedown', handleDown);
  wrapper.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMove);
  wrapper.addEventListener('mouseup', handleUp);
  wrapper.addEventListener('touchstart', handleDown, { passive: false });
  wrapper.addEventListener('touchmove', handleMove, { passive: false });
  wrapper.addEventListener('touchend', handleUp);
}

function redrawPage(ctx, pageNum, w, h, livePath, liveShape) {
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
  for (const ann of (editorAnnotations[pageNum] || [])) {
    if (ann.type !== 'textEl') drawAnnotation(ctx, ann);
  }
  if (livePath && livePath.length > 1) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = editorColor; ctx.lineWidth = editorSize;
    ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(livePath[0].x, livePath[0].y);
    for (let i = 1; i < livePath.length; i++) ctx.lineTo(livePath[i].x, livePath[i].y);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  if (liveShape) {
    const { tool, sx, sy, ex, ey } = liveShape;
    ctx.strokeStyle = editorColor; ctx.lineWidth = editorSize;
    if (tool === 'highlight') { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,235,0,0.35)'; ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, ex - sx, ey - sy); }
    else if (tool === 'rect') { ctx.strokeRect(sx, sy, ex - sx, ey - sy); }
    else if (tool === 'arrow') drawArrow(ctx, sx, sy, ex, ey);
  }
}

function drawAnnotation(ctx, ann) {
  if (ann.type === 'draw') {
    ctx.strokeStyle = ann.color || editorColor; ctx.lineWidth = ann.size || editorSize;
    ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ann.path[0].x, ann.path[0].y);
    for (let i = 1; i < ann.path.length; i++) ctx.lineTo(ann.path[i].x, ann.path[i].y);
    ctx.stroke();
  } else if (ann.type === 'highlight') {
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,235,0,0.38)';
    ctx.fillRect(ann.x, ann.y, ann.w, ann.h);
  } else if (ann.type === 'rect') {
    ctx.strokeStyle = ann.color || editorColor; ctx.lineWidth = ann.size || editorSize;
    ctx.strokeRect(ann.x, ann.y, ann.w, ann.h);
  } else if (ann.type === 'arrow') {
    ctx.strokeStyle = ann.color || editorColor; ctx.lineWidth = ann.size || editorSize;
    drawArrow(ctx, ann.sx, ann.sy, ann.ex, ann.ey);
  }
}

function drawArrow(ctx, sx, sy, ex, ey) {
  const headlen = 14;
  const angle = Math.atan2(ey - sy, ex - sx);
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(sx, sy); ctx.lineTo(ex, ey);
  ctx.lineTo(ex - headlen * Math.cos(angle - Math.PI / 6), ey - headlen * Math.sin(angle - Math.PI / 6));
  ctx.moveTo(ex, ey);
  ctx.lineTo(ex - headlen * Math.cos(angle + Math.PI / 6), ey - headlen * Math.sin(angle + Math.PI / 6));
  ctx.stroke();
}

function addTextAnnotation(annLayer, pageNum, x, y) {
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'pdf-text-input';
  el.contentEditable = 'true';
  el.spellcheck = false;
  el.style.left = x + 'px';
  el.style.top = y + 'px';
  el.style.color = editorColor;
  const fsSel = document.getElementById('edit-font-size');
  const fontSize = fsSel ? parseInt(fsSel.value) || 14 : 14;
  el.style.fontSize = fontSize + 'px';
  el.style.fontFamily = editorFontFamily + ', sans-serif';
  el.style.fontWeight = editorBold ? '700' : '400';
  el.style.fontStyle = editorItalic ? 'italic' : 'normal';
  el.style.textDecoration = editorUnderline ? 'underline' : 'none';

  // Placeholder approach - clear on first focus
  el.dataset.placeholder = 'true';
  el.textContent = 'Type here';
  el.style.opacity = '0.6';

  el.addEventListener('focus', function onFirstFocus() {
    if (el.dataset.placeholder === 'true') {
      el.textContent = '';
      el.style.opacity = '1';
      el.dataset.placeholder = 'false';
    }
    el.removeEventListener('focus', onFirstFocus);
  });

  // Prevent clicks on the text box from bubbling up to the page (which would add another)
  el.addEventListener('click', e => e.stopPropagation());
  el.addEventListener('mousedown', e => e.stopPropagation());

  // Right-click to delete
  el.addEventListener('contextmenu', e => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    el.remove();
    editorAnnotations[pageNum] = editorAnnotations[pageNum].filter(a => a.el !== el);
  });

  const ann = { type: 'textEl', el, isExistingText: false, pageNum };
  editorAnnotations[pageNum].push(ann);

  makeDraggable(el, pageNum);
  annLayer.appendChild(el);

  // Focus after a brief tick so the DOM is settled
  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    el.focus();
    // Place cursor at end
    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(el);
    range.collapse(false);
    const sel = window.getSelection();
    if (sel) { sel.removeAllRanges(); sel.addRange(range); }
  });

  setHint('Type your text · Right-click to delete · Select tool to drag');
}



function setEditorTool(tool, btn) {
  editorTool = tool;
  document.querySelectorAll('.pdf-editor-tool[id^=edit-tool-]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('active');

  // Update text-hit-layer pointer events for edit-text tool
  const editTextActive = (tool === 'edit-text');
  document.querySelectorAll('[id^=text-hit-]').forEach(layer => {
    layer.style.pointerEvents = editTextActive ? 'all' : 'none';
    layer.style.cursor = editTextActive ? 'text' : 'default';
    layer.querySelectorAll('div').forEach(hit => {
      hit.style.background = editTextActive ? 'rgba(232,80,58,0.06)' : 'transparent';
    });
  });

  // Set page wrapper cursor based on tool
  const cursorMap = { select: 'default', text: 'text', 'edit-text': 'text', highlight: 'crosshair', draw: 'crosshair', rect: 'crosshair', arrow: 'crosshair' };
  document.querySelectorAll('.pdf-page-wrapper').forEach(w => {
    w.style.cursor = cursorMap[tool] || 'default';
  });

  if (tool === 'signature') { openSigModal(); return; }
  const hints = {
    select: 'Click a text box or image to select and drag it',
    text: 'Click anywhere on the page to place a text box, then type',
    'edit-text': 'Click on any existing text in the PDF to edit it',
    highlight: 'Click and drag over text to highlight it',
    draw: 'Click and drag to draw freehand',
    rect: 'Click and drag to draw a rectangle',
    arrow: 'Click and drag to draw an arrow',
    image: 'Select an image file to insert it onto the page',
    signature: 'Draw your signature below then click "Use Signature"',
  };
  setHint(hints[tool] || '');
}

// ── Bold / Italic / Underline toggles ──
let editorBold = false, editorItalic = false, editorUnderline = false;
function toggleTextBold(btn) {
  editorBold = !editorBold;
  btn.style.fontWeight = editorBold ? '700' : '400';
  btn.classList.toggle('active', editorBold);
}
function toggleTextItalic(btn) {
  editorItalic = !editorItalic;
  btn.style.fontStyle = editorItalic ? 'italic' : 'normal';
  btn.classList.toggle('active', editorItalic);
}
function toggleTextUnderline(btn) {
  editorUnderline = !editorUnderline;
  btn.style.textDecoration = editorUnderline ? 'underline' : 'none';
  btn.classList.toggle('active', editorUnderline);
}

// ── Insert Image ──
function triggerImageInsert() {
  document.getElementById('fi-insert-img').click();
}
async function handleInsertImage(files) {
  if (!files || !files[0]) return;
  if (!editorPDF) { alert('Please load a PDF first.'); return; }
  const file = files[0];
  // Default: insert on page 1 at center-top
  const pageNum = 1;
  const vp = editorPageViewports[pageNum];
  if (!vp) return;
  const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(file);
  // Scale to fit max 300px wide while maintaining ratio
  const maxW = Math.min(300, vp.width * 0.5);
  const scale = maxW / bitmap.width;
  const imgW = Math.round(bitmap.width * scale);
  const imgH = Math.round(bitmap.height * scale);
  const x = Math.round((vp.width - imgW) / 2);
  const y = Math.round(vp.height * 0.1);
  // Draw image to canvas to get data URL
  const tmpCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  tmpCanvas.width = bitmap.width; tmpCanvas.height = bitmap.height;
  tmpCanvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0);
  const dataUrl = tmpCanvas.toDataURL('image/png');
  // Create draggable image element
  const annLayer = document.getElementById(`ann-layer-${pageNum}`);
  if (!annLayer) return;
  const el = document.createElement('img');
  el.src = dataUrl;
  el.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${x}px;top:${y}px;width:${imgW}px;height:${imgH}px;cursor:move;border:2px dashed var(--red);border-radius:2px;resize:both;overflow:hidden;display:block;max-width:none;`;
  el.draggable = false;
  // Make resizable via handle
  const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
  wrapper.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${x}px;top:${y}px;width:${imgW}px;height:${imgH}px;cursor:move;border:2px dashed var(--red);border-radius:2px;overflow:visible;`;
  const img = document.createElement('img');
  img.src = dataUrl;
  img.style.cssText = `width:100%;height:100%;display:block;pointer-events:none;`;
  const resizeHandle = document.createElement('div');
  resizeHandle.style.cssText = `position:absolute;right:-5px;bottom:-5px;width:14px;height:14px;background:var(--red);border-radius:50%;cursor:se-resize;z-index:10;`;
  wrapper.appendChild(img);
  wrapper.appendChild(resizeHandle);
  const ann = { type: 'imageEl', el: wrapper, dataUrl, pageNum };
  editorAnnotations[pageNum].push(ann);
  // Drag logic
  let isDrag = false, dragOX, dragOY;
  wrapper.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
    if (e.target === resizeHandle) return;
    isDrag = true;
    dragOX = e.clientX - parseFloat(wrapper.style.left);
    dragOY = e.clientY - parseFloat(wrapper.style.top);
    e.stopPropagation();
  });
  document.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
    if (!isDrag) return;
    wrapper.style.left = (e.clientX - dragOX) + 'px';
    wrapper.style.top = (e.clientY - dragOY) + 'px';
  });
  document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => isDrag = false);
  // Resize logic
  let isResize = false, resStartX, resStartY, resStartW, resStartH;
  resizeHandle.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
    isResize = true;
    resStartX = e.clientX; resStartY = e.clientY;
    resStartW = parseFloat(wrapper.style.width);
    resStartH = parseFloat(wrapper.style.height);
    e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault();
  });
  document.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
    if (!isResize) return;
    const dw = e.clientX - resStartX, dh = e.clientY - resStartY;
    const nw = Math.max(30, resStartW + dw);
    const nh = Math.max(20, resStartH + dh);
    wrapper.style.width = nw + 'px';
    wrapper.style.height = nh + 'px';
  });
  document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => isResize = false);
  // Right-click to delete
  wrapper.addEventListener('contextmenu', e => {
    e.preventDefault();
    wrapper.remove();
    editorAnnotations[pageNum] = editorAnnotations[pageNum].filter(a => a.el !== wrapper);
  });
  annLayer.appendChild(wrapper);
  setHint('Image inserted — drag to reposition, resize corner handle. Right-click to delete.');
  // Reset file input
  document.getElementById('fi-insert-img').value = '';
}

// ── Signature Modal ──
// ═══════════════════════════
// SIGNATURE MODAL
// ═══════════════════════════
let sigDrawing = false, sigCtx = null, sigLastX = 0, sigLastY = 0;
let sigCurrentTab = 'draw';
let sigSelectedStyle = 0;
let sigInitialsStyle = 0;

const SIG_FONTS = [
  { name: 'Elegant Script', font: '500 42px "Dancing Script", cursive', url: 'https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Dancing+Script:wght@500&display=swap' },
  { name: 'Classic Serif',  font: 'italic 40px Georgia, serif',          url: null },
  { name: 'Bold Print',     font: '700 36px Arial, sans-serif',           url: null },
  { name: 'Thin Modern',    font: '300 38px "Helvetica Neue", sans-serif', url: null },
];

// Load Google Fonts for signature styles
(function loadSigFonts(){
  const link = document.createElement('link');
  link.rel = 'stylesheet';
  link.href = 'https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Dancing+Script:wght@500&family=Pacifico&family=Great+Vibes&display=swap';
  document.head.appendChild(link);
})();

const SIG_STYLES = [
  { name: 'Script',    font: '44px "Dancing Script", cursive',      color: '#1a1a2e' },
  { name: 'Elegant',   font: 'italic 40px "Great Vibes", cursive',  color: '#1a1a2e' },
  { name: 'Retro',     font: '38px "Pacifico", cursive',            color: '#1a1a2e' },
  { name: 'Bold',      font: 'bold 36px Georgia, serif',            color: '#1a1a2e' },
];

function setSigTab(tab) {
  sigCurrentTab = tab;
  ['draw','type','initials'].forEach(t => {
    document.getElementById(`sig-tab-${t}`).classList.toggle('active', t === tab);
    document.getElementById(`sig-panel-${t}`).style.display = t === tab ? 'block' : 'none';
  });
  if (tab === 'type') renderSigStyleGrid('sig-style-grid', sigSelectedStyle, 'sig', () => renderTypedSig());
  if (tab === 'initials') renderSigStyleGrid('sig-initials-style-grid', sigInitialsStyle, 'inits', () => renderInitialsSig());
}

function renderSigStyleGrid(gridId, selectedIdx, prefix, onSelect) {
  const grid = document.getElementById(gridId);
  if (!grid) return;
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  SIG_STYLES.forEach((s, i) => {
    const card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'sig-style-card' + (i === selectedIdx ? ' selected' : '');
    card.style.fontFamily = s.font.replace(/^[^"']*["']?([^"',]+)["']?.*/, '$1');
    card.style.font = s.font;
    card.style.color = s.color;
    const nameEl = document.getElementById(prefix === 'sig' ? 'sig-type-name' : 'sig-initials-name');
    card.textContent = (nameEl && nameEl.value) ? nameEl.value : (prefix === 'sig' ? 'Your Name' : 'A.B.');
    card.onclick = () => {
      if (prefix === 'sig') sigSelectedStyle = i; else sigInitialsStyle = i;
      grid.querySelectorAll('.sig-style-card').forEach((c,j) => c.classList.toggle('selected', j===i));
      onSelect();
    };
    grid.appendChild(card);
  });
}

function renderTextOnSigCanvas(canvasId, text, styleIdx) {
  const canvas = document.getElementById(canvasId);
  if (!canvas) return;
  canvas.style.display = 'block';
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  const style = SIG_STYLES[styleIdx] || SIG_STYLES[0];
  ctx.font = style.font;
  ctx.fillStyle = style.color;
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText(text || '', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2);
}

function renderTypedSig() {
  const name = document.getElementById('sig-type-name').value;
  renderSigStyleGrid('sig-style-grid', sigSelectedStyle, 'sig', () => renderTypedSig());
  renderTextOnSigCanvas('sig-type-canvas', name, sigSelectedStyle);
}

function renderInitialsSig() {
  const initials = document.getElementById('sig-initials-name').value;
  renderSigStyleGrid('sig-initials-style-grid', sigInitialsStyle, 'inits', () => renderInitialsSig());
  renderTextOnSigCanvas('sig-initials-canvas', initials, sigInitialsStyle);
}

function openSigModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('sig-modal');
  modal.style.display = 'flex';
  setSigTab('draw');
  // Set up draw canvas
  const canvas = document.getElementById('sig-canvas');
  sigCtx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  sigCtx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  sigCtx.strokeStyle = '#1a1c22';
  sigCtx.lineWidth = 2.5;
  sigCtx.lineCap = 'round';
  sigCtx.lineJoin = 'round';

  function getSigPos(e, c) {
    const r = c.getBoundingClientRect();
    const scaleX = c.width / r.width;
    const scaleY = c.height / r.height;
    const t = e.touches ? e.touches[0] : e;
    return { x: (t.clientX - r.left) * scaleX, y: (t.clientY - r.top) * scaleY };
  }

  canvas.onmousedown = e => { sigDrawing = true; const p = getSigPos(e, canvas); sigLastX = p.x; sigLastY = p.y; };
  canvas.onmousemove = e => {
    if (!sigDrawing) return;
    const p = getSigPos(e, canvas);
    sigCtx.beginPath(); sigCtx.moveTo(sigLastX, sigLastY); sigCtx.lineTo(p.x, p.y); sigCtx.stroke();
    sigLastX = p.x; sigLastY = p.y;
  };
  canvas.onmouseup = canvas.onmouseleave = () => sigDrawing = false;
  canvas.ontouchstart = e => { e.preventDefault(); sigDrawing = true; const p = getSigPos(e, canvas); sigLastX = p.x; sigLastY = p.y; };
  canvas.ontouchmove = e => { e.preventDefault(); if (!sigDrawing) return; const p = getSigPos(e, canvas); sigCtx.beginPath(); sigCtx.moveTo(sigLastX, sigLastY); sigCtx.lineTo(p.x, p.y); sigCtx.stroke(); sigLastX = p.x; sigLastY = p.y; };
  canvas.ontouchend = () => sigDrawing = false;
}

function clearSigCanvas() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('sig-canvas');
  if (sigCtx) sigCtx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
}

function closeSigModal() {
  document.getElementById('sig-modal').style.display = 'none';
  setEditorTool('select', document.getElementById('edit-tool-select'));
}

async function useSigModal(tab) {
  let dataUrl;
  if (tab === 'draw') {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('sig-canvas');
    dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
  } else if (tab === 'type') {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('sig-type-canvas');
    if (canvas.style.display === 'none') { alert('Please type your name first.'); return; }
    dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
  } else {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('sig-initials-canvas');
    if (canvas.style.display === 'none') { alert('Please type your initials first.'); return; }
    dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
  }

  document.getElementById('sig-modal').style.display = 'none';
  if (!editorPDF) return;
  const pageNum = 1;
  const vp = editorPageViewports[pageNum];
  if (!vp) return;
  const annLayer = document.getElementById(`ann-layer-${pageNum}`);
  if (!annLayer) return;

  const sigW = tab === 'initials' ? 120 : 240;
  const sigH = tab === 'initials' ? 60 : 80;
  const x = Math.round((vp.width - sigW) / 2);
  const y = Math.round(vp.height * 0.75);

  const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
  wrapper.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${x}px;top:${y}px;width:${sigW}px;height:${sigH}px;cursor:move;border:2px dashed rgba(163,255,79,0.6);border-radius:2px;overflow:visible;z-index:50;`;
  const img = document.createElement('img');
  img.src = dataUrl;
  img.style.cssText = `width:100%;height:100%;display:block;pointer-events:none;`;
  const resizeHandle = document.createElement('div');
  resizeHandle.style.cssText = `position:absolute;right:-6px;bottom:-6px;width:14px;height:14px;background:var(--accent);border-radius:50%;cursor:se-resize;z-index:10;box-shadow:0 0 6px rgba(163,255,79,0.5);`;
  resizeHandle.title = 'Drag to resize';
  wrapper.appendChild(img); wrapper.appendChild(resizeHandle);

  const ann = { type: 'imageEl', el: wrapper, dataUrl, pageNum };
  editorAnnotations[pageNum].push(ann);

  // Drag
  let isDrag = false, dragOX, dragOY;
  wrapper.addEventListener('mousedown', e => { if (e.target === resizeHandle) return; isDrag = true; dragOX = e.clientX - parseFloat(wrapper.style.left); dragOY = e.clientY - parseFloat(wrapper.style.top); e.stopPropagation(); });
  document.addEventListener('mousemove', e => { if (!isDrag) return; wrapper.style.left = (e.clientX - dragOX) + 'px'; wrapper.style.top = (e.clientY - dragOY) + 'px'; });
  document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => isDrag = false);
  // Resize
  let isResize = false, resStartX, resStartY, resStartW, resStartH;
  resizeHandle.addEventListener('mousedown', e => { isResize = true; resStartX = e.clientX; resStartY = e.clientY; resStartW = parseFloat(wrapper.style.width); resStartH = parseFloat(wrapper.style.height); e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); });
  document.addEventListener('mousemove', e => { if (!isResize) return; wrapper.style.width = Math.max(30, resStartW + e.clientX - resStartX) + 'px'; wrapper.style.height = Math.max(20, resStartH + e.clientY - resStartY) + 'px'; });
  document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => isResize = false);
  wrapper.addEventListener('contextmenu', e => { e.preventDefault(); wrapper.remove(); editorAnnotations[pageNum] = editorAnnotations[pageNum].filter(a => a.el !== wrapper); });

  annLayer.appendChild(wrapper);
  setEditorTool('select', document.getElementById('edit-tool-select'));
  setHint('Signature placed — drag to reposition · resize corner · right-click to delete');
}

function setHint(msg) {
  const el = document.getElementById('pdf-editor-hint');
  if (el) el.textContent = msg || 'Select a tool to start editing';
}

function updateEditorColor(val) { editorColor = val; }
function updateEditorSize(val) { editorSize = parseInt(val); }
function updateEditorFont(val) { editorFontFamily = val; }
function updateEditorFontSize(val) { /* font size is read live from the select */ }

function undoLastAnnotation() {
  for (let p = editorNumPages; p >= 1; p--) {
    const anns = editorAnnotations[p];
    if (anns && anns.length > 0) {
      const last = anns[anns.length - 1];
      if (last.type === 'textEl') {
        last.el.remove();
        if (last.isExistingText && last.originalItem) {
          last.originalItem.hidden = false;
          buildTextHitAreas(document.getElementById(`text-hit-${p}`), p);
        }
      } else if (last.type === 'imageEl') {
        last.el.remove();
      }
      anns.pop();
      const dc = document.getElementById(`draw-canvas-${p}`);
      if (dc) redrawPage(dc.getContext('2d'), p, dc.width, dc.height);
      break;
    }
  }
}

function clearPageAnnotations() {
  for (let p = 1; p <= editorNumPages; p++) {
    if (editorAnnotations[p]) {
      editorAnnotations[p].forEach(a => {
        if (a.type === 'textEl') a.el.remove();
        if (a.type === 'imageEl') a.el.remove();
        if (a.isExistingText && a.originalItem) a.originalItem.hidden = false;
      });
      editorAnnotations[p] = [];
      const dc = document.getElementById(`draw-canvas-${p}`);
      if (dc) dc.getContext('2d').clearRect(0, 0, dc.width, dc.height);
    }
    // Rebuild hit areas
    const hl = document.getElementById(`text-hit-${p}`);
    if (hl) buildTextHitAreas(hl, p);
  }
}

async function savePDFEdits() {
  if (!editorCurrentFile) { setStatus('edit', 'err', 'No file loaded.'); return; }
  setStatus('edit', 'loading', 'Saving PDF with edits…');
  try {
    const { PDFDocument, rgb, StandardFonts } = PDFLib;
    const ab = await readBuf(editorCurrentFile);
    const doc = await PDFDocument.load(ab, { ignoreEncryption: true });
    const pages = doc.getPages();

    // Embed all fonts we might need
    const embeddedFonts = {};
    const sfMap = {
      'Helvetica': StandardFonts.Helvetica,
      'Arial': StandardFonts.Helvetica,
      'Calibri': StandardFonts.Helvetica,
      'Times New Roman': StandardFonts.TimesRoman,
      'Georgia': StandardFonts.TimesRoman,
      'Palatino Linotype': StandardFonts.TimesRoman,
      'Garamond': StandardFonts.TimesRoman,
      'Book Antiqua': StandardFonts.TimesRoman,
      'Courier New': StandardFonts.Courier,
      'Lucida Console': StandardFonts.Courier,
      'Lucida Sans': StandardFonts.Helvetica,
      'Verdana': StandardFonts.Helvetica,
      'Tahoma': StandardFonts.Helvetica,
      'Trebuchet MS': StandardFonts.Helvetica,
      'Century Gothic': StandardFonts.Helvetica,
      'Impact': StandardFonts.HelveticaBold,
      'Comic Sans MS': StandardFonts.Helvetica,
    };
    async function getFont(name) {
      if (embeddedFonts[name]) return embeddedFonts[name];
      const sf = sfMap[name] || StandardFonts.Helvetica;
      const f = await doc.embedFont(sf);
      embeddedFonts[name] = f;
      return f;
    }

    for (let p = 1; p <= editorNumPages; p++) {
      const pdfPage = pages[p - 1];
      const { width, height } = pdfPage.getSize();
      const vp = editorPageViewports[p];
      if (!vp) continue;
      const sx = width / vp.width, sy = height / vp.height;

      // First pass: white-out original text that has been edited
      for (const item of (editorTextItems[p] || [])) {
        if (item.hidden) {
          // Cover original text with a white rectangle
          pdfPage.drawRectangle({
            x: item.pdfX - 1,
            y: item.pdfY - item.pdfFontSize * 0.2,
            width: (item.w / vp.scale) + 2,
            height: item.pdfFontSize * 1.4,
            color: rgb(1, 1, 1),
            opacity: 1,
          });
        }
      }

      // Second pass: draw all annotations
      for (const ann of (editorAnnotations[p] || [])) {
        if (ann.type === 'draw') {
          if (ann.path.length < 2) continue;
          const c = hexToRgb(ann.color || '#1a1c22');
          for (let i = 1; i < ann.path.length; i++) {
            pdfPage.drawLine({
              start: { x: ann.path[i - 1].x * sx, y: height - ann.path[i - 1].y * sy },
              end: { x: ann.path[i].x * sx, y: height - ann.path[i].y * sy },
              thickness: (ann.size || 2) * sx, color: rgb(c.r, c.g, c.b), opacity: 0.9,
            });
          }
        } else if (ann.type === 'highlight') {
          pdfPage.drawRectangle({
            x: ann.x * sx, y: height - (ann.y + Math.abs(ann.h)) * sy,
            width: Math.abs(ann.w) * sx, height: Math.abs(ann.h) * sy,
            color: rgb(1, 0.92, 0.1), opacity: 0.38,
          });
        } else if (ann.type === 'rect') {
          const c = hexToRgb(ann.color || '#e8503a');
          pdfPage.drawRectangle({
            x: ann.x * sx, y: height - (ann.y + Math.abs(ann.h)) * sy,
            width: Math.abs(ann.w) * sx, height: Math.abs(ann.h) * sy,
            borderWidth: (ann.size || 2) * sx, borderColor: rgb(c.r, c.g, c.b),
            color: rgb(1, 1, 1), opacity: 0,
          });
        } else if (ann.type === 'arrow') {
          const c = hexToRgb(ann.color || '#e8503a');
          pdfPage.drawLine({
            start: { x: ann.sx * sx, y: height - ann.sy * sy },
            end: { x: ann.ex * sx, y: height - ann.ey * sy },
            thickness: (ann.size || 2) * sx, color: rgb(c.r, c.g, c.b),
          });
          // Arrowhead
          const angle = Math.atan2(-(ann.ey - ann.sy), ann.ex - ann.sx);
          const hl = 12 * sx;
          const ex = ann.ex * sx, ey = height - ann.ey * sy;
          pdfPage.drawLine({ start: { x: ex, y: ey }, end: { x: ex - hl * Math.cos(angle - Math.PI / 6), y: ey + hl * Math.sin(angle - Math.PI / 6) }, thickness: (ann.size || 2) * sx, color: rgb(c.r, c.g, c.b) });
          pdfPage.drawLine({ start: { x: ex, y: ey }, end: { x: ex - hl * Math.cos(angle + Math.PI / 6), y: ey + hl * Math.sin(angle + Math.PI / 6) }, thickness: (ann.size || 2) * sx, color: rgb(c.r, c.g, c.b) });
        } else if (ann.type === 'textEl') {
          const el = ann.el;
          // Get actual text - skip placeholder
          const rawText = el.dataset.placeholder === 'true' ? '' : (el.innerText || el.textContent || '');
          const text = rawText.trim();
          if (!text) continue;
          // For edited existing text: derive color from original item if available
          let textColor = el.style.color || editorColor;
          if (ann.isExistingText && (!el.style.color || el.style.color === '#111111' || el.style.color === 'rgb(17, 17, 17)')) {
            textColor = editorColor; // fall back to current editor color
          }
          const c = hexToRgb(textColor);
          const fs = parseFloat(el.style.fontSize) || 14;
          const lx = parseFloat(el.style.left) || 0;
          const ly = parseFloat(el.style.top) || 0;
          const isBold = (el.style.fontWeight === '700' || el.style.fontWeight === 'bold');
          const isItalic = (el.style.fontStyle === 'italic');
          const isUnderline = (el.style.textDecoration === 'underline' || el.style.textDecoration?.includes('underline'));
          const fontName = el.style.fontFamily ? el.style.fontFamily.split(',')[0].trim() : editorFontFamily;
          // pick bold/italic variant if needed
          let sfVariant = sfMap[fontName] || StandardFonts.Helvetica;
          if (isBold && isItalic) sfVariant = StandardFonts.HelveticaBoldOblique;
          else if (isBold) sfVariant = StandardFonts.HelveticaBold;
          else if (isItalic) sfVariant = StandardFonts.HelveticaOblique;
          const font = await (async () => {
            if (embeddedFonts[sfVariant]) return embeddedFonts[sfVariant];
            const f = await doc.embedFont(sfVariant);
            embeddedFonts[sfVariant] = f;
            return f;
          })();
          const pdfX = lx * sx;
          const pdfY = height - (ly + fs * 1.2) * sy;
          const pdfFS = fs * sx;
          const lines = text.split('\n');
          lines.forEach((line, li) => {
            if (!line) return;
            const lineY = pdfY - li * pdfFS * 1.3;
            pdfPage.drawText(line, {
              x: pdfX,
              y: lineY,
              size: Math.max(pdfFS, 4),
              font,
              color: rgb(c.r, c.g, c.b),
            });
            // Draw underline as a thin line just below the baseline
            if (isUnderline) {
              const lineWidth = font.widthOfTextAtSize(line, Math.max(pdfFS, 4));
              const underlineY = lineY - pdfFS * 0.15;
              pdfPage.drawLine({
                start: { x: pdfX, y: underlineY },
                end: { x: pdfX + lineWidth, y: underlineY },
                thickness: Math.max(pdfFS * 0.07, 0.5),
                color: rgb(c.r, c.g, c.b),
              });
            }
          });
        } else if (ann.type === 'imageEl') {
          // Embed the image into the PDF
          try {
            const wrapper = ann.el;
            const dataUrl = ann.dataUrl;
            const imgEl = wrapper.querySelector('img');
            // Get current position/size from DOM
            const lx = parseFloat(wrapper.style.left) || 0;
            const ly = parseFloat(wrapper.style.top) || 0;
            const iw = parseFloat(wrapper.style.width) || 100;
            const ih = parseFloat(wrapper.style.height) || 80;
            // Convert data URL to bytes
            const base64 = dataUrl.split(',')[1];
            const binary = atob(base64);
            const bytes2 = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
            for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) bytes2[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
            const embImg = await doc.embedPng(bytes2);
            const pdfImgX = lx * sx;
            const pdfImgY = height - (ly + ih) * sy;
            pdfPage.drawImage(embImg, {
              x: pdfImgX,
              y: pdfImgY,
              width: iw * sx,
              height: ih * sy,
            });
          } catch(imgErr) { console.warn('Could not embed image:', imgErr); }
        }
      }
    }

    const bytes = await doc.save();
    setStatus('edit', 'ok', '✓ Saved · ' + fz(bytes.length));
    const a = makeDL(bytes, 'edited.pdf', 'application/pdf');
    // Append download button to status bar
    const statusBar = document.getElementById('pdf-editor-status');
    const existing = statusBar.querySelector('.dl-btn');
    if (existing) existing.remove();
    statusBar.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
  } catch (e) { setStatus('edit', 'err', 'Error saving: ' + e.message); console.error(e); }
}

function hexToRgb(hex) {
  // Handle named colors too
  if (!hex || !hex.startsWith('#')) {
    const c = document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d');
    c.fillStyle = hex;
    hex = c.fillStyle;
  }
  const r = parseInt(hex.slice(1, 3), 16) / 255;
  const g = parseInt(hex.slice(3, 5), 16) / 255;
  const b = parseInt(hex.slice(5, 7), 16) / 255;
  return { r, g, b };
}

function closePDFEditor() {
  document.getElementById('pdf-edit-upload-card').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('pdf-editor-container').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('pdf-editor-viewport').innerHTML = '';
  editorPDF = null; editorAnnotations = {}; editorTextItems = {}; editorPageViewports = {};
  document.getElementById('fi-edit').value = '';
  clearStatus('edit');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════
// IMAGE STATE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════
const IF = {};

function imgAdd(key,files){
  // For i2p: accumulate files across multiple selections
  if(key==='i2p'){
    if(!IF[key]) IF[key]=[];
    const newFiles=Array.from(files).filter(f=>f.type.startsWith('image/')||key==='i2p');
    IF[key]=IF[key].concat(newFiles);
  } else {
    IF[key]=Array.from(files).filter(f=>f.type.startsWith('image/')||key==='i2p');
  }
  renderImgGrid(key);
  if(key==='i2p') renderI2PPreview();
  const btn=document.getElementById('btn-'+key); if(btn) btn.disabled=!IF[key].length;
  return true;
}

async function renderI2PPreview(){
  const files=IF['i2p']||[];
  const previewArea=document.getElementById('i2p-preview-area');
  const grid=document.getElementById('i2p-page-grid');
  const countEl=document.getElementById('i2p-preview-count');
  const dzMore=document.getElementById('dz-i2p-more');
  const dzCount=document.getElementById('dz-i2p-count');
  if(!files.length){
    previewArea.style.display='none';grid.innerHTML='';
    if(dzMore)dzMore.style.display='none';
    return;
  }
  if(dzMore){dzMore.style.display='flex';dzCount.textContent=files.length+' image'+(files.length!==1?'s':'')+' added';}
  previewArea.style.display='block';
  countEl.textContent=files.length+' image'+(files.length!==1?'s':'');
  grid.innerHTML='';
  
  // Show drag hint if multiple files
  let hintEl = document.getElementById('i2p-drag-hint');
  if(!hintEl){
    hintEl = document.createElement('div');
    hintEl.id='i2p-drag-hint';
    hintEl.style.cssText='font-family:"DM Mono",monospace;font-size:10px;color:var(--ink5);margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;';
    hintEl.innerHTML='<span>⠿ Drag to reorder · ✕ to remove images</span>';
    previewArea.insertBefore(hintEl, previewArea.querySelector('.pdf-preview-grid'));
  }
  hintEl.style.display = files.length > 1 ? 'flex' : 'none';

  for(let i=0;i<files.length;i++){
    const f=files[i];
    const wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='pdf-preview-page';
    wrap.style.cursor='grab';
    wrap.draggable=true;
    wrap.dataset.idx=i;
    
    // Delete button
    const delBtn=document.createElement('button');
    delBtn.style.cssText='position:absolute;top:5px;right:5px;background:rgba(220,38,38,0.85);color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:50%;width:20px;height:20px;cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;z-index:10;line-height:1;font-weight:bold;transition:background 0.12s;';
    delBtn.textContent='×';
    delBtn.title='Remove image';
    delBtn.addEventListener('mouseenter',()=>delBtn.style.background='rgba(185,28,28,0.95)');
    delBtn.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>delBtn.style.background='rgba(220,38,38,0.85)');
    delBtn.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      e.stopPropagation();
      IF['i2p'].splice(parseInt(wrap.dataset.idx),1);
      renderI2PPreview();
      const btn=document.getElementById('btn-i2p');
      if(btn) btn.disabled=!(IF['i2p']&&IF['i2p'].length>0);
      const dz=document.getElementById('dz-i2p-count');
      if(dz) dz.textContent=IF['i2p'].length+' image'+(IF['i2p'].length!==1?'s':'')+' added';
    });
    
    // Drag handle
    const dragHandle=document.createElement('div');
    dragHandle.style.cssText='position:absolute;top:5px;left:5px;background:rgba(26,28,34,0.7);color:rgba(255,255,255,0.8);border:none;border-radius:4px;width:20px;height:20px;cursor:grab;font-size:11px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;z-index:10;';
    dragHandle.textContent='⠿';
    dragHandle.title='Drag to reorder';

    // Order badge
    const orderBadge=document.createElement('div');
    orderBadge.style.cssText='position:absolute;bottom:22px;left:5px;background:var(--red);color:#fff;border-radius:3px;font-family:"DM Mono",monospace;font-size:8px;padding:1px 5px;z-index:10;';
    orderBadge.textContent='#'+(i+1);
    
    // Canvas for image preview
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.style.width='100%';canvas.style.height='auto';canvas.style.display='block';
    wrap.appendChild(canvas);
    wrap.appendChild(delBtn);
    wrap.appendChild(dragHandle);
    wrap.appendChild(orderBadge);
    
    const label=document.createElement('div');
    label.className='pdf-preview-page-label';
    label.innerHTML=`<span style="overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;max-width:100px">${f.name}</span><span>${fz(f.size)}</span>`;
    wrap.appendChild(label);
    grid.appendChild(wrap);
    
    // Draw image into canvas
    try{
      const bitmap=await createImageBitmap(f);
      const scale=Math.min(140/bitmap.width,160/bitmap.height,1);
      canvas.width=Math.round(bitmap.width*scale);
      canvas.height=Math.round(bitmap.height*scale);
      canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(bitmap,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
    }catch(e){
      canvas.width=140;canvas.height=100;
      const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
      ctx.fillStyle='#e8e0d0';ctx.fillRect(0,0,140,100);
      ctx.fillStyle='#9ca3af';ctx.font='12px sans-serif';ctx.textAlign='center';
      ctx.fillText('Preview N/A',70,55);
    }
    
    // Drag events for reordering
    wrap.addEventListener('dragstart',e=>{
      e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',wrap.dataset.idx);
      wrap.style.opacity='0.45';
      wrap.style.cursor='grabbing';
    });
    wrap.addEventListener('dragend',()=>{
      wrap.style.opacity='1';
      wrap.style.cursor='grab';
      document.querySelectorAll('#i2p-page-grid .pdf-preview-page').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator'));
    });
    wrap.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{
      e.preventDefault();
      wrap.classList.add('drag-over-indicator');
    });
    wrap.addEventListener('dragleave',()=>wrap.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator'));
    wrap.addEventListener('drop',e=>{
      e.preventDefault();
      wrap.classList.remove('drag-over-indicator');
      const fromIdx=parseInt(e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain'));
      const toIdx=parseInt(wrap.dataset.idx);
      if(fromIdx===toIdx)return;
      // Reorder the IF array
      const moved=IF['i2p'].splice(fromIdx,1)[0];
      IF['i2p'].splice(toIdx,0,moved);
      renderI2PPreview();
    });
  }
}
function imgSingle(key,files){
  IF[key]=[files[0]]; renderImgGrid(key);
  const btn=document.getElementById('btn-'+key); if(btn) btn.disabled=false;
  if(key==='crop'){const img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{document.getElementById('crop-info').textContent=`Image: ${img.naturalWidth}×${img.naturalHeight}px`;document.getElementById('crop-w').value=img.naturalWidth;document.getElementById('crop-h').value=img.naturalHeight;URL.revokeObjectURL(img.src);};img.src=URL.createObjectURL(files[0]);}
  if(key==='ir'){const img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{document.getElementById('ir-w').value=img.naturalWidth;document.getElementById('ir-h').value=img.naturalHeight;URL.revokeObjectURL(img.src);};img.src=URL.createObjectURL(files[0]);}
}
function renderImgGrid(key){
  const c=document.getElementById('ig-'+key); if(!c)return;
  c.innerHTML='';
  (IF[key]||[]).forEach((f,i)=>{
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(f);
    const d=document.createElement('div');
    d.className='img-thumb';
    d.style.cssText='position:relative;border-radius:var(--r10);overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--border-subtle);background:var(--surface2);cursor:default;';
    const img=document.createElement('img');
    img.src=url; img.alt=f.name;
    img.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block;';
    img.onload=()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    const lbl=document.createElement('div');
    lbl.className='img-lbl';
    lbl.innerHTML=`<span style="overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;max-width:70px">${f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'')}</span><span>${fz(f.size)}</span>`;
    d.appendChild(img); d.appendChild(lbl);
    c.appendChild(d);
  });
}
function imgClear(key){
  IF[key]=[];renderImgGrid(key);
  if(key==='i2p'){
    const pa=document.getElementById('i2p-preview-area');if(pa)pa.style.display='none';
    const pg=document.getElementById('i2p-page-grid');if(pg)pg.innerHTML='';
    const dm=document.getElementById('dz-i2p-more');if(dm)dm.style.display='none';
  }
  const btn=document.getElementById('btn-'+key); if(btn) btn.disabled=true;
  const dl=document.getElementById('dl-'+key); if(dl) dl.innerHTML='';
  clearStatus(key);
}

// ─── IMAGES TO PDF ───
async function doImagesToPDF(){
  const files=IF['i2p']; if(!files||!files.length)return;
  setStatus('i2p','loading','Creating PDF…'); document.getElementById('dl-i2p').innerHTML='';
  try{
    const {PDFDocument}=PDFLib;
    const doc=await PDFDocument.create();
    const szMap={a4:[595,842],letter:[612,792],a3:[842,1191]};
    const sk=document.getElementById('i2p-size').value;
    const orient=document.getElementById('i2p-orient').value;
    let added=0;
    for(const f of files){
      try{
        // Draw image onto a canvas to get raw PNG bytes — handles ALL formats
        const bitmap=await createImageBitmap(f);
        const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width=bitmap.width; canvas.height=bitmap.height;
        canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(bitmap,0,0);
        const pngBytes=await new Promise((res,rej)=>canvas.toBlob(b=>{
          if(!b){rej(new Error('Canvas blob failed'));return;}
          b.arrayBuffer().then(ab=>res(new Uint8Array(ab)));
        },'image/png'));
        const img=await doc.embedPng(pngBytes);
        let [pw,ph]=sk==='fit'?[img.width,img.height]:(szMap[sk]||[595,842]);
        if(orient==='landscape'&&sk!=='fit')[pw,ph]=[ph,pw];
        const page=doc.addPage([pw,ph]);
        const scale=Math.min(pw/img.width,ph/img.height);
        const iw=img.width*scale, ih=img.height*scale;
        page.drawImage(img,{x:(pw-iw)/2,y:(ph-ih)/2,width:iw,height:ih});
        added++;
