      }catch(imgErr){console.warn('Skipped image:',f.name,imgErr);}
    }
    if(!added){setStatus('i2p','err','No images could be processed. Check file formats.');return;}
    const b=await doc.save();
    setStatus('i2p','ok',`✓ PDF created · ${added} page(s) · ${fz(b.length)}`);
    addDL('dl-i2p',b,'images.pdf','application/pdf');
  }catch(e){setStatus('i2p','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

// ─── PDF TO IMAGES ───
const p2iSkippedPages = new Set();

async function loadP2IPreview(files) {
  if (!files || !files[0]) return;
  pdfSingle('p2i', files);
  p2iSkippedPages.clear();
  document.getElementById('dz-p2i').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('dz-p2i-more').style.display = 'flex';
  document.getElementById('dz-p2i-info').textContent = files[0].name;
  document.getElementById('p2i-preview-area').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('btn-p2i').disabled = false;
  const ab = await readBuf(files[0]);
  pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
  const pdf = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: new Uint8Array(ab), verbosity: 0, cMapUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/cmaps/', cMapPacked: true }).promise;
  const grid = document.getElementById('p2i-page-grid');
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  for (let i = 1; i <= pdf.numPages; i++) {
    const page = await pdf.getPage(i);
    const vp = page.getViewport({ scale: 0.4 });
    const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
    wrapper.className = 'pdf-preview-page';
    wrapper.dataset.page = i;
    wrapper.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = vp.width; canvas.height = vp.height;
    await page.render({ canvasContext: canvas.getContext('2d'), viewport: vp }).promise;
    const label = document.createElement('div');
    label.className = 'pdf-preview-page-label';
    label.innerHTML = `<span>Page ${i}</span><span id="p2i-lbl-${i}">✓</span>`;
    wrapper.appendChild(canvas); wrapper.appendChild(label);
    wrapper.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (p2iSkippedPages.has(i)) {
        p2iSkippedPages.delete(i);
        wrapper.style.opacity = '1'; wrapper.style.borderColor = '';
        document.getElementById(`p2i-lbl-${i}`).textContent = '✓';
        document.getElementById(`p2i-lbl-${i}`).style.color = '';
      } else {
        p2iSkippedPages.add(i);
        wrapper.style.opacity = '0.35'; wrapper.style.borderColor = '#fca5a5';
        document.getElementById(`p2i-lbl-${i}`).textContent = '✕ skip';
        document.getElementById(`p2i-lbl-${i}`).style.color = 'var(--red)';
      }
      updateP2ISkipCount(pdf.numPages);
    });
    grid.appendChild(wrapper);
  }
  updateP2ISkipCount(pdf.numPages);
}
function updateP2ISkipCount(total) {
  const el = document.getElementById('p2i-skip-count');
  if (!el) return;
  const skipped = p2iSkippedPages.size;
  el.textContent = skipped > 0 ? `${skipped} page(s) excluded · ${total - skipped} included` : 'All pages included';
}
function p2iClearSkips() {
  p2iSkippedPages.clear();
  document.querySelectorAll('#p2i-page-grid .pdf-preview-page').forEach(w => {
    const i = parseInt(w.dataset.page);
    w.style.opacity = '1'; w.style.borderColor = '';
    const lbl = document.getElementById(`p2i-lbl-${i}`);
    if (lbl) { lbl.textContent = '✓'; lbl.style.color = ''; }
  });
  const total = document.querySelectorAll('#p2i-page-grid .pdf-preview-page').length;
  updateP2ISkipCount(total);
}
function p2iClearAll() {
  pdfClear('p2i');
  p2iSkippedPages.clear();
  document.getElementById('p2i-preview-area').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('dz-p2i-more').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('dz-p2i').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('ig-p2i').innerHTML = '';
}

async function doPDFtoImg(){
  const files=PF['p2i']; if(!files||!files[0])return;
  const outputFmt = document.getElementById('p2i-output-fmt')?.value || 'zip';
  setStatus('p2i','loading','Rendering PDF pages…');
  document.getElementById('dl-p2i').innerHTML='';
  document.getElementById('ig-p2i').innerHTML='';
  try{
    pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
    const ab=await readBuf(files[0]);
    const pdf=await pdfjsLib.getDocument({data:new Uint8Array(ab),verbosity:0,cMapUrl:'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/cmaps/',cMapPacked:true}).promise;
    const scale=parseFloat(document.getElementById('p2i-scale').value)||2;
    const dlArea=document.getElementById('dl-p2i');
    const igArea=document.getElementById('ig-p2i');
    const pageBlobs = [];
    let renderedCount = 0;

    for(let i=1;i<=pdf.numPages;i++){
      if (p2iSkippedPages.has(i)) continue;
      setStatus('p2i','loading',`Rendering page ${i} of ${pdf.numPages}…`);
      const page=await pdf.getPage(i);
      const vp=page.getViewport({scale});
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width=vp.width; canvas.height=vp.height;
      await page.render({canvasContext:canvas.getContext('2d'),viewport:vp}).promise;
      await new Promise(res=>canvas.toBlob(blob=>{
        if(!blob){res();return;}
        const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const thumb=document.createElement('div');
        thumb.className='img-thumb';
        thumb.innerHTML=`<img src="${url}" alt="Page ${i}"><div class="img-lbl">Page ${i}</div>`;
        igArea.appendChild(thumb);
        pageBlobs.push({ blob, name: `page-${String(i).padStart(2,'0')}.png` });
        renderedCount++;
        res();
      },'image/png'));
    }

    if (renderedCount === 0) { setStatus('p2i','err','No pages to render.'); return; }

    if (outputFmt === 'pdf') {
      // Build single PDF from rendered images
      setStatus('p2i','loading','Building single PDF…');
      const {PDFDocument} = PDFLib;
      const outDoc = await PDFDocument.create();
      for (const {blob} of pageBlobs) {
        const arrBuf = await blob.arrayBuffer();
        const imgBytes = new Uint8Array(arrBuf);
        const pngImg = await outDoc.embedPng(imgBytes);
        const page = outDoc.addPage([pngImg.width, pngImg.height]);
        page.drawImage(pngImg, { x:0, y:0, width:pngImg.width, height:pngImg.height });
      }
      const pdfBytes = await outDoc.save();
      const baseName = files[0].name.replace(/\.pdf$/i,'');
      addDL('dl-p2i', pdfBytes, `${baseName}_images.pdf`, 'application/pdf');
      setStatus('p2i','ok',`✓ Rendered ${renderedCount} page(s) → single PDF`);
    } else {
      // ZIP of PNGs
      setStatus('p2i','loading','Packaging ZIP…');
      const zip = new JSZip();
      for (const {blob, name} of pageBlobs) {
        const arrBuf = await blob.arrayBuffer();
        zip.file(name, arrBuf);
      }
      const zipBlob = await zip.generateAsync({type:'uint8array', compression:'DEFLATE', compressionOptions:{level:6}});
      const baseName = files[0].name.replace(/\.pdf$/i,'');
      addDL('dl-p2i', zipBlob, `${baseName}_images.zip`, 'application/zip');
      setStatus('p2i','ok',`✓ Rendered ${renderedCount} page(s) · Download as ZIP`);
    }
  }catch(e){setStatus('p2i','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

function doHTMLtoPDF(){
  const html=document.getElementById('html-inp').value.trim();
  if(!html){setStatus&&setStatus('html-pdf','err','Please enter some HTML first.');return;}
  // Wrap bare content in a full document if needed
  const fullHtml=html.includes('<html')?html:`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><style>body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;margin:40px;font-size:13px;line-height:1.6;color:#1a1c22}</style></head><body>${html}</body></html>`;
  const w=window.open('','_blank','width=800,height=600');
  if(!w){alert('Popup blocked. Please allow popups for this site and try again.');return;}
  w.document.open();
  w.document.write(fullHtml);
  w.document.close();
  w.focus();
  setTimeout(()=>{w.print();},600);
}

  document.getElementById('ir-px-cfg').style.display=m==='px'?'block':'none';
  document.getElementById('ir-pct-cfg').style.display=m==='pct'?'block':'none';
  updateResizePreview();
}

// ─── CONVERT ───
async function doConvertImg(){
  const files=IF['icv']; if(!files||!files.length)return;
  const fmt=document.getElementById('icv-fmt').value;
  const qual=parseInt(document.getElementById('icv-q').value)/100;
  const ext=fmt.split('/')[1];
  setStatus('icv','loading','Converting…'); document.getElementById('dl-icv').innerHTML='';
  for(const f of files){
    const bmp=await createImageBitmap(f);
    const c=Object.assign(document.createElement('canvas'),{width:bmp.width,height:bmp.height});
    c.getContext('2d').drawImage(bmp,0,0);
    const blob=await canBlob(c,fmt,qual);
    addDL('dl-icv',await blobU8(blob),f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'.'+ext),fmt);
  }
  setStatus('icv','ok',`✓ Converted ${files.length} file(s) to ${ext.toUpperCase()}`);
}

// ─── CROP with click-to-start / click-to-stop ───
let cropBitmap = null;
let cropPhase = 'idle'; // 'idle' | 'started' | 'done'
let cropStartX = 0, cropStartY = 0;
let cropScaleX = 1, cropScaleY = 1;
let cropAnimFrame = null;
let cropLiveX = 0, cropLiveY = 0;

async function imgLoadCrop(files){
  if(!files||!files[0])return;
  IF['crop']=[files[0]];
  document.getElementById('btn-crop').disabled=false;
  cropBitmap=await createImageBitmap(files[0]);
  const canvas=document.getElementById('crop-display-canvas');
  const maxW=Math.min(700,cropBitmap.width);
  const scale=maxW/cropBitmap.width;
  cropScaleX=scale; cropScaleY=scale;
  canvas.width=Math.round(cropBitmap.width*scale);
  canvas.height=Math.round(cropBitmap.height*scale);
  canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(cropBitmap,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  document.getElementById('crop-canvas-area').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('crop-info').textContent=`Original: ${cropBitmap.width}×${cropBitmap.height}px`;
  // Default: full image
  document.getElementById('crop-x').value=0;
  document.getElementById('crop-y').value=0;
  document.getElementById('crop-w').value=cropBitmap.width;
  document.getElementById('crop-h').value=cropBitmap.height;
  cropPhase='idle';
  setCropHint('idle');
  setupCropClick(canvas);
  updateCropOverlay();
}

function setCropHint(phase){
  const el=document.getElementById('crop-mode-hint');
  if(!el)return;
  if(phase==='idle') el.textContent='Click on the image to set the start corner of your crop';
  else if(phase==='started') el.textContent='Move mouse to size crop, then click again to finish';
  else el.textContent='Selection done — adjust with inputs or click on image to restart';
}

function cropSelectAll(){
  if(!cropBitmap)return;
  document.getElementById('crop-x').value=0;
  document.getElementById('crop-y').value=0;
  document.getElementById('crop-w').value=cropBitmap.width;
  document.getElementById('crop-h').value=cropBitmap.height;
  cropPhase='done';
  setCropHint('done');
  updateCropOverlay();
}

function cropClearSelection(){
  if(!cropBitmap)return;
  cropPhase='idle';
  setCropHint('idle');
  const canvas=document.getElementById('crop-display-canvas');
  const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  ctx.drawImage(cropBitmap,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  document.getElementById('crop-preview').style.display='none';
}

function setupCropClick(canvas){
  // Remove old listeners by cloning
  const newCanvas=canvas.cloneNode(true);
  canvas.parentNode.replaceChild(newCanvas,canvas);
  const c=document.getElementById('crop-display-canvas');
  c.style.cursor='crosshair';

  c.addEventListener('mousedown', e=>{
    if(cropPhase==='idle'||cropPhase==='done'){
      // Start new crop
      const r=c.getBoundingClientRect();
      cropStartX=Math.max(0,Math.min(cropBitmap.width,Math.round((e.clientX-r.left)/cropScaleX)));
      cropStartY=Math.max(0,Math.min(cropBitmap.height,Math.round((e.clientY-r.top)/cropScaleY)));
      cropPhase='dragging';
      setCropHint('started');
    }
  });

  c.addEventListener('mousemove', e=>{
    if(cropPhase!=='dragging')return;
    const r=c.getBoundingClientRect();
    const ex=Math.max(0,Math.min(cropBitmap.width,Math.round((e.clientX-r.left)/cropScaleX)));
    const ey=Math.max(0,Math.min(cropBitmap.height,Math.round((e.clientY-r.top)/cropScaleY)));
    const x=Math.min(cropStartX,ex), y=Math.min(cropStartY,ey);
    const w=Math.max(1,Math.abs(ex-cropStartX)), h=Math.max(1,Math.abs(ey-cropStartY));
    document.getElementById('crop-x').value=x;
    document.getElementById('crop-y').value=y;
    document.getElementById('crop-w').value=w;
    document.getElementById('crop-h').value=h;
    updateCropOverlay();
  });

  c.addEventListener('mouseup', e=>{
    if(cropPhase==='dragging'){
      const w=parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-w').value)||1;
      const h=parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-h').value)||1;
      if(w < 5 || h < 5){
        // Too small — treat as single click to reset
        cropPhase='idle'; setCropHint('idle'); return;
      }
      cropPhase='done';
      setCropHint('done');
      renderCropPreview();
    }
  });

  // Touch support
  c.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>{e.preventDefault();const t=e.touches[0];c.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousedown',{clientX:t.clientX,clientY:t.clientY}));},{passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('touchmove',e=>{e.preventDefault();const t=e.touches[0];c.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousemove',{clientX:t.clientX,clientY:t.clientY}));},{passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('touchend',e=>{e.preventDefault();c.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mouseup',{}));},{passive:false});
}

function updateCropOverlay(){
  if(!cropBitmap)return;
  const canvas=document.getElementById('crop-display-canvas');
  const overlay=document.getElementById('crop-overlay');
  const x=parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-x').value)||0;
  const y=parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-y').value)||0;
  const w=parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-w').value)||1;
  const h=parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-h').value)||1;
  // Redraw base image
  const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  ctx.drawImage(cropBitmap,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  // Draw dimmed overlay outside crop area
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.45)';
  ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  // Clear the crop region to show it bright
  ctx.clearRect(Math.round(x*cropScaleX),Math.round(y*cropScaleY),Math.round(w*cropScaleX),Math.round(h*cropScaleY));
  ctx.drawImage(cropBitmap,x,y,w,h,Math.round(x*cropScaleX),Math.round(y*cropScaleY),Math.round(w*cropScaleX),Math.round(h*cropScaleY));
  // Draw border
  ctx.strokeStyle='var(--coral,#e8503a)';ctx.lineWidth=2;
  ctx.strokeRect(Math.round(x*cropScaleX)+1,Math.round(y*cropScaleY)+1,Math.round(w*cropScaleX)-2,Math.round(h*cropScaleY)-2);
  renderCropPreview();
}

function renderCropPreview(){
  if(!cropBitmap)return;
  const x=parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-x').value)||0;
  const y=parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-y').value)||0;
  const w=Math.max(1,parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-w').value)||1);
  const h=Math.max(1,parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-h').value)||1);
  const prev=document.getElementById('crop-result-canvas');
  prev.width=w; prev.height=h;
  prev.getContext('2d').drawImage(cropBitmap,x,y,w,h,0,0,w,h);
  document.getElementById('crop-preview-dims').textContent=`${w}×${h}px`;
  document.getElementById('crop-preview').style.display='flex';
}

async function doCropImg(){
  const files=IF['crop']; if(!files||!files[0])return;
  setStatus('crop','loading','Cropping…'); document.getElementById('dl-crop').innerHTML='';
  try{
    const f=files[0]; const bmp=cropBitmap||await createImageBitmap(f);
    const x=parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-x').value),y=parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-y').value);
    const cw=parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-w').value),ch=parseInt(document.getElementById('crop-h').value);
    const c=Object.assign(document.createElement('canvas'),{width:cw,height:ch});
    c.getContext('2d').drawImage(bmp,x,y,cw,ch,0,0,cw,ch);
    const blob=await canBlob(c,f.type||'image/jpeg',0.95);
    const ext=f.name.split('.').pop();
    addDL('dl-crop',await blobU8(blob),f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,`_cropped.${ext}`),blob.type);
    setStatus('crop','ok',`✓ Cropped to ${cw}×${ch}px`);
  }catch(e){setStatus('crop','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

function imgClearCrop(){
  IF['crop']=[]; cropBitmap=null;
  document.getElementById('btn-crop').disabled=true;
  document.getElementById('dl-crop').innerHTML='';
  document.getElementById('crop-canvas-area').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('crop-preview').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('crop-info').textContent='';
  clearStatus('crop');
}

// ─── ROTATE with live preview ───
let irotBitmap = null;
let irotCurrentAction = 'r90'; // default
function setRotateAction(act, btn){
  irotCurrentAction = act;
  document.querySelectorAll('.irot-action-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
  if(btn) btn.classList.add('active');
  updateRotatePreview();
}
async function imgAddRotate(files){
  imgAdd('irot',files);
  if(!files||!files[0])return;
  irotBitmap=await createImageBitmap(files[0]);
  // Auto-select first button if none active
  const active = document.querySelector('.irot-action-btn.active');
  if(!active){
    const first = document.getElementById('irot-btn-r90ccw');
    if(first){ first.classList.add('active'); irotCurrentAction='r270'; }
  }
  updateRotatePreview();
}
async function updateRotatePreview(){
  if(!irotBitmap)return;
  const act = irotCurrentAction || 'r90';
  let cw=irotBitmap.width,ch=irotBitmap.height,rot=0,sx=1,sy=1;
  if(act==='r90'){cw=irotBitmap.height;ch=irotBitmap.width;rot=Math.PI/2;}
  else if(act==='r180')rot=Math.PI;
  else if(act==='r270'){cw=irotBitmap.height;ch=irotBitmap.width;rot=-Math.PI/2;}
  else if(act==='fh')sx=-1;
  else sy=-1;
  const prev=document.getElementById('irot-preview-canvas');
  prev.width=cw; prev.height=ch;
  const ctx=prev.getContext('2d');
  ctx.translate(cw/2,ch/2);ctx.rotate(rot);ctx.scale(sx,sy);
  ctx.drawImage(irotBitmap,-irotBitmap.width/2,-irotBitmap.height/2);
  const labels={'r90':'Rotate 90° Right','r180':'Rotate 180°','r270':'Rotate 90° Left','fh':'Flip Horizontal','fv':'Flip Vertical'};
  document.getElementById('irot-preview-dims').textContent=`${cw}×${ch}px`;
  document.getElementById('irot-preview-label').textContent=labels[act]||act;
  document.getElementById('irot-preview').style.display='flex';
}

// ─── ROTATE IMAGES (updated to use live bitmap) ───
async function doRotateImg(){
  const files=IF['irot']; if(!files||!files.length)return;
  const act = irotCurrentAction || 'r90';
  setStatus('irot','loading','Transforming…'); document.getElementById('dl-irot').innerHTML='';
  for(const f of files){
    const bmp=f===files[0]&&irotBitmap?irotBitmap:await createImageBitmap(f);
    let cw=bmp.width,ch=bmp.height,rot=0,sx=1,sy=1;
    if(act==='r90'){cw=bmp.height;ch=bmp.width;rot=Math.PI/2;}
    else if(act==='r180')rot=Math.PI;
    else if(act==='r270'){cw=bmp.height;ch=bmp.width;rot=-Math.PI/2;}
    else if(act==='fh')sx=-1;
    else sy=-1;
    const c=Object.assign(document.createElement('canvas'),{width:cw,height:ch});
    const ctx=c.getContext('2d');ctx.translate(cw/2,ch/2);ctx.rotate(rot);ctx.scale(sx,sy);ctx.drawImage(bmp,-bmp.width/2,-bmp.height/2);
    const blob=await canBlob(c,f.type||'image/jpeg',0.95);
    const ext=f.name.split('.').pop();
    addDL('dl-irot',await blobU8(blob),f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,`_${act}.${ext}`),blob.type);
  }
  setStatus('irot','ok',`✓ Transformed ${files.length} image(s)`);
}

// ─── GRAYSCALE with live preview ───
let gsBitmap = null;
async function imgAddGrayscale(files){
  imgAdd('gs',files);
  if(!files||!files[0])return;
  gsBitmap=await createImageBitmap(files[0]);
  renderGrayscalePreview();
}
function renderGrayscalePreview(){
  if(!gsBitmap)return;
  const prev=document.getElementById('gs-preview-canvas');
  prev.width=gsBitmap.width; prev.height=gsBitmap.height;
  const ctx=prev.getContext('2d');
  ctx.drawImage(gsBitmap,0,0);
  const id=ctx.getImageData(0,0,prev.width,prev.height);
  for(let i=0;i<id.data.length;i+=4){const g=(id.data[i]*.299+id.data[i+1]*.587+id.data[i+2]*.114)|0;id.data[i]=id.data[i+1]=id.data[i+2]=g;}
  ctx.putImageData(id,0,0);
  document.getElementById('gs-preview-dims').textContent=`${prev.width}×${prev.height}px`;
  document.getElementById('gs-preview').style.display='flex';
}

// ─── GRAYSCALE (updated) ───
async function doGrayscale(){
  const files=IF['gs']; if(!files||!files.length)return;
  setStatus('gs','loading','Converting…'); document.getElementById('dl-gs').innerHTML='';
  for(const f of files){
    const bmp=f===files[0]&&gsBitmap?gsBitmap:await createImageBitmap(f);
    const c=Object.assign(document.createElement('canvas'),{width:bmp.width,height:bmp.height});
    const ctx=c.getContext('2d'); ctx.drawImage(bmp,0,0);
    const id=ctx.getImageData(0,0,bmp.width,bmp.height);
    for(let i=0;i<id.data.length;i+=4){const g=(id.data[i]*.299+id.data[i+1]*.587+id.data[i+2]*.114)|0;id.data[i]=id.data[i+1]=id.data[i+2]=g;}
    ctx.putImageData(id,0,0);
    const blob=await canBlob(c,f.type||'image/jpeg',0.95);
    const ext=f.name.split('.').pop();
    addDL('dl-gs',await blobU8(blob),f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,`_gray.${ext}`),blob.type);
  }
  setStatus('gs','ok',`✓ Converted ${files.length} image(s) to grayscale`);
}

// ─── WATERMARK with live preview ───
let iwmBitmap = null;
async function imgAddWatermark(files){
  imgAdd('iwm',files);
  if(!files||!files[0])return;
  iwmBitmap=await createImageBitmap(files[0]);
  updateWatermarkPreview();
}
function updateWatermarkPreview(){
  if(!iwmBitmap)return;
  const txt=document.getElementById('iwm-txt').value;
  const pos=document.getElementById('iwm-pos').value;
  const fs=parseInt(document.getElementById('iwm-sz').value)||36;
  const col=document.getElementById('iwm-col').value;
  const op=parseInt(document.getElementById('iwm-op').value)/100;
  const prev=document.getElementById('iwm-preview-canvas');
  prev.width=iwmBitmap.width; prev.height=iwmBitmap.height;
  const ctx=prev.getContext('2d');
  ctx.drawImage(iwmBitmap,0,0);
  ctx.globalAlpha=op; ctx.font=`bold ${fs}px sans-serif`; ctx.fillStyle=col;
  const tw=ctx.measureText(txt).width; const mg=18;
  let x,y;
  if(pos==='br'){x=iwmBitmap.width-tw-mg;y=iwmBitmap.height-mg;}
  else if(pos==='bl'){x=mg;y=iwmBitmap.height-mg;}
  else if(pos==='tr'){x=iwmBitmap.width-tw-mg;y=fs+mg;}
  else if(pos==='tl'){x=mg;y=fs+mg;}
  else{x=(iwmBitmap.width-tw)/2;y=(iwmBitmap.height+fs)/2;}
  ctx.fillText(txt,x,y);
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  document.getElementById('iwm-preview-dims').textContent=`${prev.width}×${prev.height}px`;
  document.getElementById('iwm-preview').style.display='flex';
}

// ─── WATERMARK IMAGES (updated) ───
async function doWatermarkImg(){
  const files=IF['iwm']; if(!files||!files.length)return;
  const txt=document.getElementById('iwm-txt').value;
  const pos=document.getElementById('iwm-pos').value;
  const fs=parseInt(document.getElementById('iwm-sz').value);
  const col=document.getElementById('iwm-col').value;
  const op=parseInt(document.getElementById('iwm-op').value)/100;
  setStatus('iwm','loading','Watermarking…'); document.getElementById('dl-iwm').innerHTML='';
  for(const f of files){
    const bmp=f===files[0]&&iwmBitmap?iwmBitmap:await createImageBitmap(f);
    const c=Object.assign(document.createElement('canvas'),{width:bmp.width,height:bmp.height});
    const ctx=c.getContext('2d'); ctx.drawImage(bmp,0,0);
    ctx.globalAlpha=op; ctx.font=`bold ${fs}px sans-serif`; ctx.fillStyle=col;
    const tw=ctx.measureText(txt).width; const mg=18;
    let x,y;
    if(pos==='br'){x=bmp.width-tw-mg;y=bmp.height-mg;}
    else if(pos==='bl'){x=mg;y=bmp.height-mg;}
    else if(pos==='tr'){x=bmp.width-tw-mg;y=fs+mg;}
    else if(pos==='tl'){x=mg;y=fs+mg;}
    else{x=(bmp.width-tw)/2;y=(bmp.height+fs)/2;}
    ctx.fillText(txt,x,y);
    const blob=await canBlob(c,f.type||'image/jpeg',0.95);
    const ext=f.name.split('.').pop();
    addDL('dl-iwm',await blobU8(blob),f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,`_wm.${ext}`),blob.type);
  }
  setStatus('iwm','ok',`✓ Watermarked ${files.length} image(s)`);
}

// ─── COMPRESS with live preview ───
let icBitmap = null;
async function imgAddCompress(files){
  imgAdd('ic',files);
  if(!files||!files[0])return;
  icBitmap=await createImageBitmap(files[0]);
  updateCompressPreview();
}
async function updateCompressPreview(){
  if(!icBitmap)return;
  const qual=parseInt(document.getElementById('ic-q').value)/100;
  const c=Object.assign(document.createElement('canvas'),{width:icBitmap.width,height:icBitmap.height});
  c.getContext('2d').drawImage(icBitmap,0,0);
  const blob=await canBlob(c,'image/jpeg',qual);
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const prev=document.getElementById('ic-preview-canvas');
  const img=new Image();
  img.onload=()=>{
    prev.width=icBitmap.width; prev.height=icBitmap.height;
    prev.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,0,0);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    document.getElementById('ic-preview-info').textContent=`${(blob.size/1024).toFixed(1)} KB · ${icBitmap.width}×${icBitmap.height}px`;
    document.getElementById('ic-preview').style.display='flex';
  };
  img.src=url;
}

// ─── COMPRESS IMAGES (updated) ───
async function doCompressImg(){
  const files=IF['ic']; if(!files||!files.length)return;
  const qual=parseInt(document.getElementById('ic-q').value)/100;
  setStatus('ic','loading','Compressing…'); document.getElementById('dl-ic').innerHTML='';
  for(const f of files){
    const bmp=f===files[0]&&icBitmap?icBitmap:await createImageBitmap(f);
    const c=Object.assign(document.createElement('canvas'),{width:bmp.width,height:bmp.height});
    c.getContext('2d').drawImage(bmp,0,0);
    const blob=await canBlob(c,'image/jpeg',qual);
    addDL('dl-ic',await blobU8(blob),f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'')+'_compressed.jpg','image/jpeg');
  }
  setStatus('ic','ok',`✓ Compressed ${files.length} image(s)`);
}

// ─── RESIZE with live preview ───
let irBitmap = null;
async function imgLoadResize(files){
  imgSingle('ir',files);
  if(!files||!files[0])return;
  irBitmap=await createImageBitmap(files[0]);
  document.getElementById('ir-w').value=irBitmap.width;
  document.getElementById('ir-h').value=irBitmap.height;
  updateResizePreview();
}
function updateResizePreview(){
  if(!irBitmap)return;
  let w,h;
  if(resizeMode==='pct'){
    const p=parseInt(document.getElementById('ir-pct').value)/100||0.5;
    w=Math.max(1,Math.round(irBitmap.width*p));
    h=Math.max(1,Math.round(irBitmap.height*p));
  } else {
    w=parseInt(document.getElementById('ir-w').value)||irBitmap.width;
    if(document.getElementById('ir-ratio').checked) h=Math.round(w*(irBitmap.height/irBitmap.width));
    else h=parseInt(document.getElementById('ir-h').value)||irBitmap.height;
  }
  const prev=document.getElementById('ir-preview-canvas');
  // Display at original size but note target dims
  prev.width=w; prev.height=h;
  prev.getContext('2d').drawImage(irBitmap,0,0,w,h);
  document.getElementById('ir-preview-dims').textContent=`${w}×${h}px`;
  document.getElementById('ir-preview').style.display='flex';
}

// ─── RESIZE (updated) ───
async function doResizeImg(){
  const files=IF['ir']; if(!files||!files[0])return;
  setStatus('ir','loading','Resizing…'); document.getElementById('dl-ir').innerHTML='';
  try{
    const f=files[0]; const bmp=irBitmap||await createImageBitmap(f);
    let w,h;
    if(resizeMode==='pct'){const p=parseInt(document.getElementById('ir-pct').value)/100;w=Math.round(bmp.width*p);h=Math.round(bmp.height*p);}
    else{w=parseInt(document.getElementById('ir-w').value);h=parseInt(document.getElementById('ir-h').value);if(document.getElementById('ir-ratio').checked)h=Math.round(w*(bmp.height/bmp.width));}
    const c=Object.assign(document.createElement('canvas'),{width:w,height:h});
    c.getContext('2d').drawImage(bmp,0,0,w,h);
    const blob=await canBlob(c,f.type||'image/jpeg',0.95);
    const ext=f.name.split('.').pop();
    addDL('dl-ir',await blobU8(blob),f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/,`_${w}x${h}.${ext}`),blob.type);
    setStatus('ir','ok',`✓ Resized to ${w}×${h}px`);
  }catch(e){setStatus('ir','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════
// CALCULATORS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════
function RC(l,v,s,hi){ return `<div class="rc${hi?' hi':''}"><div class="rc-l">${l}</div><div class="rc-v">${v}</div>${s?`<div class="rc-s">${s}</div>`:''}</div>`; }

// ── SALARY & TAX RATES DATA (2025 Government Sources) ──
// Progressive bracket calculator — works for any country
function calcProgressiveTax(income, brackets, deduction, opts) {
  opts = opts || {};
  const taxable = Math.max(0, income - (deduction||0));
  let tax = 0;
  for(const b of brackets) {
    if(taxable <= b.min) break;
    tax += (Math.min(taxable, b.max) - b.min) * b.rate;
  }
  // Section 87A rebate (India): applies when TAXABLE income <= rebate threshold
  // Rebate = min(tax, maxRebate). Marginal relief: if taxable just over threshold,
  // tax capped at (taxable - rebateThreshold).
  if(opts.rebate87A && opts.rebate87AMax != null) {
    if(taxable <= opts.rebate87A) {
      tax = Math.max(0, tax - Math.min(tax, opts.rebate87AMax));
    } else if(taxable <= opts.rebate87A + 75000) {
      // Marginal relief: tax can't exceed income above rebate threshold
      const marginalCap = taxable - opts.rebate87A;
      tax = Math.min(tax, marginalCap);
    }
  }
  // Health & Education Cess (India 4%)
  const cessRate = opts.cessRate || 0;
  const cess = tax * cessRate;
  const totalTax = tax + cess;
  return { tax: totalTax, taxBeforeCess: tax, cess, effectiveRate: income > 0 ? (totalTax/income)*100 : 0, taxable };
}

    regions: { 'Dubai': { state: 0 }, 'Abu Dhabi': { state: 0 }, 'Sharjah': { state: 0 }, 'Other Emirates': { state: 0 } }
  },
  NZ: {
    currency: 'NZ$', social: 1.39, pension: 3.0, bracket: 'NZ_FED',
    socialLabel: 'ACC Levy', pensionLabel: 'KiwiSaver',
    note: '2025 IRD progressive: 10.5% \u2264NZ$14k, 17.5% \u2264$48k, 30% \u2264$70k, 33% \u2264$180k, 39% above. ACC levy ~1.39%. KiwiSaver employee 3%. Federal tax auto-computed.',
    regions: { 'New Zealand (National)': { state: 0 } }
  },
  SG: {
    currency: 'S$', social: 20.0, pension: 0, bracket: 'SG_FED',
    socialLabel: 'CPF (Employee)', pensionLabel: null,
    note: '2025 IRAS resident: 0% \u2264S$20k, rising progressively to 24% above S$1M. CPF employee contribution ~20% (age-dependent). Federal tax auto-computed.',
    regions: { 'Singapore': { state: 0 } }
  },
  OTHER: { currency: '$', social: 0, pension: 0, note: 'Custom mode — enter your own rates below.', regions: {} },
};

// ── Federal tax override state ──
let salFedOverride = false;

function salToggleFedOverride() {
  salFedOverride = !salFedOverride;
  const taxInput = document.getElementById('sal-tax');
  const btn = document.getElementById('sal-fed-override-btn');
  if (salFedOverride) {
    taxInput.readOnly = false;
    taxInput.style.opacity = '';
    taxInput.style.outline = '2px solid var(--accent)';
    taxInput.style.outlineOffset = '2px';
    taxInput.title = 'Auto-computed from 2025 progressive tax brackets based on your income';
    btn.textContent = '↺ Auto';
    btn.style.background = 'var(--accent-bg2)';
    btn.style.borderColor = 'var(--accent)';
    btn.style.color = 'var(--accent)';
    btn.title = 'Restore auto-computed income tax from progressive brackets';
    taxInput.focus();
    taxInput.select();
  } else {
    taxInput.readOnly = true;
    taxInput.style.opacity = '0.6';
    taxInput.style.outline = '';
    taxInput.style.outlineOffset = '';
    taxInput.title = 'Auto-computed from 2025 progressive tax brackets based on your income';
    btn.textContent = 'Override';
    btn.style.background = 'var(--accent-bg)';
    btn.style.borderColor = 'var(--border2)';
    btn.style.color = 'var(--accent)';
    btn.title = 'Override the auto-computed income tax with a custom rate';
    calcSalary();
  }
}

function salCountryChange() {
  const country = document.getElementById('sal-country').value;
  const regionGroup = document.getElementById('sal-region-group');
  const regionSel = document.getElementById('sal-region');
  regionSel.innerHTML = '<option value="">\u2014 Select region \u2014</option>';
  // Reset override when country changes
  salFedOverride = false;
  const overrideBtn2 = document.getElementById('sal-fed-override-btn');
  if(overrideBtn2) { overrideBtn2.style.display='none'; overrideBtn2.textContent='Override'; }
  const taxInput2 = document.getElementById('sal-tax');
  if(taxInput2) { taxInput2.readOnly=false; taxInput2.style.opacity=''; taxInput2.style.outline=''; }

  // Reset labels to generic defaults
  const niLabel = document.getElementById('sal-ni-label');
  const penLabel = document.getElementById('sal-pen-label');
  const taxLabelEl = document.getElementById('sal-tax-label');
  const penGrp = document.getElementById('sal-pen-grp');
  const stateGrp = document.getElementById('sal-state-grp');
  if(niLabel) niLabel.innerHTML = 'Social Contributions %';
  if(penLabel) penLabel.innerHTML = 'Pension / Retirement %';
  if(penGrp) penGrp.style.display = '';
  if(stateGrp) stateGrp.style.display = '';
  if(taxLabelEl) taxLabelEl.childNodes[0].textContent = 'Income Tax ';

  if (!country || country === 'OTHER') {
    regionGroup.style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('sal-rate-note').style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }
  const data = SAL_TAX_DATA[country];
  if (!data) return;
  const regionLabel = country==='CA' ? 'Province' : country==='AU' ? 'State / Territory' : country==='AE' ? 'Emirate' : 'State / Region';
  document.getElementById('sal-region-label').textContent = regionLabel;
  Object.keys(data.regions).forEach(r => {
    const opt = document.createElement('option'); opt.value = r; opt.textContent = r;
    regionSel.appendChild(opt);
  });
  regionGroup.style.display = 'flex';
  if (Object.keys(data.regions).length === 1) { regionSel.selectedIndex = 1; salRegionChange(); }
}

function salRegionChange() {
  const country = document.getElementById('sal-country').value;
  const region = document.getElementById('sal-region').value;
  if (!country || !region) return;
  const data = SAL_TAX_DATA[country];
  if (!data) return;
  const rates = data.regions[region];
  if (!rates) return;
  const curSel = document.getElementById('sal-cur');
  Array.from(curSel.options).forEach((o,i) => { if(o.value===data.currency||o.text===data.currency) curSel.selectedIndex=i; });
  // Reset override state when region changes
  salFedOverride = false;
  const hasBracket = !!data.bracket;
  const taxInput = document.getElementById('sal-tax');
  const fedNote = document.getElementById('sal-fed-note');
  const overrideBtn = document.getElementById('sal-fed-override-btn');

  // ── Dynamic UI labels ──
  const niLabel = document.getElementById('sal-ni-label');
  const penLabel = document.getElementById('sal-pen-label');
  const taxLabel = document.getElementById('sal-tax-label');
  const stateLabel = document.getElementById('sal-state-label');
  const penGrp = document.getElementById('sal-pen-grp');
  const stateGrp = document.getElementById('sal-state-grp');

  if(niLabel) niLabel.innerHTML = (data.socialLabel || 'Social Contributions') + ' %';
  if(penLabel) penLabel.innerHTML = (data.pensionLabel || 'Pension / Retirement') + ' %';
  if(penGrp) penGrp.style.display = (data.pensionLabel === null) ? 'none' : '';

  // Country-specific income tax label
  const taxLabelText = {
    US:'Federal Income Tax', CA:'Federal Income Tax', GB:'Income Tax',
    AU:'Income Tax', DE:'Einkommensteuer', FR:'Impôt sur le Revenu',
    IN:'Income Tax (New Regime)', NZ:'Income Tax', SG:'Income Tax',
    AE:'Income Tax', OTHER:'Income Tax'
  }[country] || 'Income Tax';
  if(taxLabel) {
    // Preserve the (auto-computed) span and override button
    taxLabel.childNodes[0].textContent = taxLabelText + ' ';
  }
  // State label
  const stateLabelText = {US:'State Income Tax %', CA:'Provincial Tax %', AU:'No State Tax (federal only)', GB:'No Regional Tax', IN:'No State Income Tax', DE:'Solidarity Surcharge %', FR:'No Regional Tax'}[country] || 'State / Regional Tax %';
  if(stateLabel) stateLabel.textContent = stateLabelText;
  if(stateGrp) stateGrp.style.display = ['GB','IN','NZ','SG','AE','FR','DE'].includes(country) ? 'none' : '';

  // ── UAE: no tax, hide income tax field ──
  const taxRow = taxInput ? taxInput.closest('.ctrl-g') : null;
  if(country === 'AE') {
    if(taxRow) taxRow.style.display = 'none';
    if(overrideBtn) overrideBtn.style.display = 'none';
  } else {
    if(taxRow) taxRow.style.display = '';
  }

  if(fedNote) fedNote.style.display = hasBracket ? 'inline' : 'none';
  if(overrideBtn) {
    overrideBtn.style.display = (hasBracket && country !== 'AE') ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
    overrideBtn.textContent = 'Override';
    overrideBtn.style.background = 'var(--accent-bg)';
    overrideBtn.style.borderColor = 'var(--border2)';
  }
  if(hasBracket) {
    taxInput.value = '0';
    taxInput.readOnly = true;
    taxInput.title = 'Auto-computed from 2025 progressive tax brackets based on your income';
    taxInput.style.opacity = '0.6';
    taxInput.style.outline = '';
  } else {
    taxInput.value = rates.fed || 0;
    taxInput.readOnly = false;
    taxInput.title = '';
    taxInput.style.opacity = '';
    taxInput.style.outline = '';
  }
  document.getElementById('sal-state-tax').value = rates.state || 0;
  document.getElementById('sal-ni').value = data.social;
  document.getElementById('sal-pen').value = data.pension;
  const noteEl = document.getElementById('sal-rate-note');
  noteEl.style.display = 'block';
  noteEl.innerHTML = '<strong>'+(country==='CA'?'Province':'Region')+':</strong> '+region+' &nbsp;|&nbsp; '+data.note;

  // ── Update table headers ──
  const tblFed = document.getElementById('tbl-fed-hdr');
  const tblState = document.getElementById('tbl-state-hdr');
  const tblNi = document.getElementById('tbl-ni-hdr');
  const tblPen = document.getElementById('tbl-pen-hdr');
  if(tblFed) tblFed.textContent = taxLabelText;
  if(tblState) tblState.textContent = stateLabelText.replace(' %','').replace(' (federal only)','');
  if(tblNi) tblNi.textContent = data.socialLabel || 'Social';
  if(tblPen) tblPen.textContent = data.pensionLabel || 'Pension';

  calcSalary();
}

// ── SALARY CALC ──
function calcSalary(){
  const g=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sal-gross').value)||0;
  const stTax=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sal-state-tax').value)||0;
  const ni=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sal-ni').value)||0;
  const pen=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sal-pen').value)||0;
  const oth=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sal-other').value)||0;
  const cur=document.getElementById('sal-cur').value;
  const country=document.getElementById('sal-country').value;
  const data = country ? SAL_TAX_DATA[country] : null;

  // Country-specific labels for results
  const socialLbl = (data && data.socialLabel) ? data.socialLabel : 'Social Contributions';
  const penLbl = (data && data.pensionLabel !== undefined && data.pensionLabel !== null) ? data.pensionLabel : 'Pension';
  const incomeTaxLbl = {
    US:'Federal Income Tax', CA:'Federal Income Tax', GB:'Income Tax',
    AU:'Income Tax', DE:'Einkommensteuer', FR:'Impôt sur le Revenu',
    IN:'Income Tax (New Regime)', NZ:'Income Tax', SG:'Income Tax',
    AE:'Income Tax (None)', OTHER:'Income Tax'
  }[country] || 'Income Tax';

  let fedA=0, fedTaxPct=0, bracketHtml='';

  // UAE or no-tax countries: skip income tax entirely
  if(country === 'AE') {
    fedA = 0; fedTaxPct = 0;
    bracketHtml = RC('Tax Regime','No Income Tax','UAE — zero income tax for residents',false);
  } else if(data && data.bracket && g > 0 && !salFedOverride) {
    const bConfig = BRACKETS[data.bracket];
    // France: apply 10% employment deduction before standard bracket deduction
    let effectiveDeduction = bConfig.deduction || 0;
    if(bConfig.empDeductionPct) {
      effectiveDeduction = Math.min(g * bConfig.empDeductionPct, bConfig.empDeductionMax || Infinity);
    }
    const result = calcProgressiveTax(g, bConfig.tiers, effectiveDeduction, bConfig.opts || {});
    fedA = result.tax;
    fedTaxPct = result.effectiveRate;
    document.getElementById('sal-tax').value = fedTaxPct.toFixed(2);
    const taxable = result.taxable;
    let marginalRate = bConfig.tiers[0].rate * 100;
    for(const b of bConfig.tiers){ if(taxable > b.min) marginalRate = b.rate * 100; }
    const dedStr = effectiveDeduction > 0 ? 'after '+cur+(effectiveDeduction).toLocaleString()+' deduction' : 'no std deduction';
    const cessLine = result.cess > 0 ? RC('Health & Ed. Cess (4%)', f2(result.cess,cur),'on income tax',false) : '';
    // India-specific: show standard deduction applied
    const inStdDed = (country==='IN' && effectiveDeduction>0) ? RC('Std Deduction (₹75,000)', f2(effectiveDeduction,cur),'auto-applied',false) : '';
    const rebateLine = (country==='IN' && result.taxBeforeCess < (bConfig.opts && bConfig.opts.rebate87AMax||0)+1 && taxable <= (bConfig.opts&&bConfig.opts.rebate87A||0))
      ? RC('Sec 87A Rebate','Full rebate applied','taxable ≤ ₹7L',false) : '';
    bracketHtml = inStdDed
                + RC('Taxable Income', f2(taxable,cur), dedStr, false)
                + RC('Marginal Rate', marginalRate.toFixed(1)+'%','top bracket',false)
                + RC('Effective Tax Rate', fedTaxPct.toFixed(2)+'%','actual rate',false)
                + rebateLine
                + cessLine;
  } else if(country !== 'AE') {
    fedTaxPct=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sal-tax').value)||0;
    fedA=g*fedTaxPct/100;
  }

  const stA= (['GB','IN','NZ','SG','AE','FR','DE'].includes(country)) ? 0 : g*stTax/100;
  const nA=g*ni/100, pA=g*pen/100;
  const net=g-fedA-stA-nA-pA-oth;

  document.getElementById('sal-rc').innerHTML=
    RC('Gross Annual',f2(g,cur),'',false)+
    RC(incomeTaxLbl,f2(fedA,cur),(country==='AE'?'0%':fedTaxPct.toFixed(2)+'%'),false)+
    (stA>0 ? RC('State / Provincial Tax',f2(stA,cur),stTax+'%',false) : '')+
    RC(socialLbl,f2(nA,cur),ni+'%',false)+
    (pen>0 ? RC(penLbl,f2(pA,cur),pen+'%',false) : '')+
    bracketHtml+
    RC('Net Annual',f2(net,cur),'take-home/year',true)+
    RC('Monthly',f2(net/12,cur),'per month',false)+
    RC('Weekly',f2(net/52,cur),'per week',false)+
    RC('Daily',f2(net/260,cur),'per working day',false);

  document.getElementById('sal-tbl').innerHTML=['Annual','Monthly','Weekly','Daily'].map((p,i)=>{
    const d=[1,12,52,260][i];
    return '<tr><td>'+p+'</td><td>'+f2(g/d,cur)+'</td><td>'+f2(fedA/d,cur)+'</td><td>'+f2(stA/d,cur)+'</td><td>'+f2(nA/d,cur)+'</td><td>'+f2(pA/d,cur)+'</td><td style="font-weight:600;color:var(--red)">'+f2(net/d,cur)+'</td></tr>';
  }).join('');
}

// ── EMI ──
let emiSchedVisible=false;
function toggleEMISched(){emiSchedVisible=!emiSchedVisible;document.getElementById('emi-sched-wrap').style.display=emiSchedVisible?'block':'none';document.getElementById('emi-sched-btn').textContent=emiSchedVisible?'📋 Hide Schedule':'📋 Show Schedule';}
function calcEMI(){
  const P=parseFloat(document.getElementById('emi-amt').value)||0;
  const r=(parseFloat(document.getElementById('emi-rate').value)||0)/1200;
  const n=(parseFloat(document.getElementById('emi-yrs').value)||0)*12;
  const cur=document.getElementById('emi-cur').value;
  if(!P||!r||!n)return;
  const emi=P*(r*Math.pow(1+r,n))/(Math.pow(1+r,n)-1);
  const tot=emi*n,int=tot-P;
  document.getElementById('emi-rc').innerHTML=RC('Monthly EMI',f2(emi,cur),'',true)+RC('Total Payment',f2(tot,cur),'principal + interest',false)+RC('Total Interest',f2(int,cur),((int/P)*100).toFixed(1)+'% of principal',false)+RC('Principal',f2(P,cur),'loan amount',false);
  const tb=document.getElementById('emi-tbl'); tb.innerHTML=''; let bal=P;
  for(let i=1;i<=n;i++){const ii=bal*r,pr=emi-ii;bal=Math.max(0,bal-pr);tb.innerHTML+=`<tr><td>${i}</td><td>${f2(emi,cur)}</td><td>${f2(pr,cur)}</td><td>${f2(ii,cur)}</td><td>${f2(bal,cur)}</td></tr>`;}
}

// ── GPA ──
const GPA_GRADES={4:['A+','A','A-','B+','B','B-','C+','C','C-','D+','D','D-','F'],5:['O','A+','A','B+','B','C','F'],10:['O','A+','A','B+','B','C','D','F'],100:['A','B','C','D','F']};
const GPA_PTS={4:{'A+':4.0,'A':4.0,'A-':3.7,'B+':3.3,'B':3.0,'B-':2.7,'C+':2.3,'C':2.0,'C-':1.7,'D+':1.3,'D':1.0,'D-':0.7,'F':0},5:{'O':5.0,'A+':4.5,'A':4.0,'B+':3.5,'B':3.0,'C':2.5,'F':0},10:{'O':10,'A+':9,'A':8,'B+':7,'B':6,'C':5,'D':4,'F':0},100:{'A':90,'B':75,'C':60,'D':50,'F':0}};
let gpaRows=[];
function initGPARows(){if(!gpaRows.length)gpaRows=[{name:'Course 1',grade:'A',cr:3},{name:'Course 2',grade:'B+',cr:3}];renderGPARows();}
function renderGPARows(){
  const scale=parseInt(document.getElementById('gpa-scale').value);
  const grades=GPA_GRADES[scale];
  document.getElementById('gpa-rows').innerHTML=gpaRows.map((row,i)=>`<div class="gpa-row"><span class="gpa-num">${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}</span><div class="ctrl-g"><div class="ctrl-l">Course</div><input class="ctrl-i" value="${row.name}" onchange="gpaRows[${i}].name=this.value" style="width:155px"></div><div class="ctrl-g"><div class="ctrl-l">Grade</div><select class="ctrl-i" onchange="gpaRows[${i}].grade=this.value">${grades.map(g=>`<option${g===row.grade?' selected':''}>${g}</option>`).join('')}</select></div><div class="ctrl-g"><div class="ctrl-l">Credits</div><input class="ctrl-i" type="number" value="${row.cr}" min="0.5" step="0.5" onchange="gpaRows[${i}].cr=parseFloat(this.value)||3" style="width:75px"></div><button class="fchip-rm" onclick="gpaRows.splice(${i},1);renderGPARows()" style="margin-bottom:0;align-self:flex-end">×</button></div>`).join('');
}
function gpaAddRow(){const scale=parseInt(document.getElementById('gpa-scale').value);gpaRows.push({name:'Course '+(gpaRows.length+1),grade:GPA_GRADES[scale][0],cr:3});renderGPARows();}
function gpaClear(){gpaRows=[];renderGPARows();document.getElementById('gpa-result').innerHTML='';}
function gpaRebuild(){const scale=parseInt(document.getElementById('gpa-scale').value);gpaRows=gpaRows.map(r=>({...r,grade:GPA_GRADES[scale][0]}));renderGPARows();}
function calcGPA(){
  const scale=parseInt(document.getElementById('gpa-scale').value);
  const pts=GPA_PTS[scale];
  let tp=0,tc=0;
  document.querySelectorAll('.gpa-row').forEach((row,i)=>{
    const grade=row.querySelectorAll('.ctrl-i')[1].value;
    const cr=parseFloat(row.querySelectorAll('.ctrl-i')[2].value)||0;
    tp+=(pts[grade]??0)*cr; tc+=cr;
  });
  if(!tc){document.getElementById('gpa-result').innerHTML='';return;}
  const gpa=tp/tc, pct=(gpa/scale)*100;
  document.getElementById('gpa-result').innerHTML=`<div class="rc-grid">${RC('GPA',gpa.toFixed(3),'Cumulative',true)}${RC('Scale',scale+'.0','',false)}${RC('Credits',tc,'total',false)}${RC('Percentage',pct.toFixed(1)+'%','approx.',false)}</div><div class="prog"><div class="prog-fill" style="width:${pct}%"></div></div><div style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;color:var(--ink6);margin-top:5px;text-align:right">${pct.toFixed(2)}% of ${scale}.0</div>`;
}

// ── GPA CONVERTER ──
function calcGPAConvert(){
  const v=parseFloat(document.getElementById('gpac-val').value);
  const from=parseFloat(document.getElementById('gpac-from').value);
  if(isNaN(v)){document.getElementById('gpac-rc').innerHTML='';return;}
  const pct=(v/from)*100;
  let html='<div class="rc-grid">';
  if(from!==4)html+=RC('4.0 Scale',Math.min(4,(pct/100)*4).toFixed(2),'US Standard',true);
  if(from!==5)html+=RC('5.0 Scale',Math.min(5,(pct/100)*5).toFixed(2),'Extended',false);
  if(from!==10)html+=RC('10.0 CGPA',Math.min(10,(pct/100)*10).toFixed(2),'India',false);
  if(from!==100)html+=RC('Percentage',pct.toFixed(1)+'%','approx.',false);
  document.getElementById('gpac-rc').innerHTML=html+'</div>';
}

// ── PERCENTAGE ──
let pctMode='of';
function setPctMode(m,btn){pctMode=m;['of','what','change','incdec'].forEach(id=>document.getElementById('pct-'+id).style.display='none');document.getElementById('pct-'+m).style.display='block';document.querySelectorAll('#p-calc-percent .tab').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));btn.classList.add('active');}
function calcPct(m){
  if(m==='of'){const p=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pof-p').value),n=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pof-n').value),r=document.getElementById('pof-rc');if(!isNaN(p)&&!isNaN(n)){r.style.display='grid';r.innerHTML=RC(`${p}% of ${n}`,f2(p*n/100),'',true)+RC('Remaining',f2(n-p*n/100),'',false);}else r.style.display='none';}
  if(m==='what'){const x=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pwh-x').value),y=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pwh-y').value),r=document.getElementById('pwh-rc');if(!isNaN(x)&&!isNaN(y)&&y!==0){r.style.display='grid';r.innerHTML=RC('Result',f2(x/y*100)+'%','percentage',true)+RC('Decimal',f2(x/y),'ratio',false);}else r.style.display='none';}
  if(m==='change'){const f=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pch-f').value),t=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pch-t').value),r=document.getElementById('pch-rc');if(!isNaN(f)&&!isNaN(t)&&f!==0){const ch=(t-f)/f*100;r.style.display='grid';r.innerHTML=RC('% Change',`<span style="color:${ch>=0?'var(--green)':'var(--red)'}">${ch>=0?'+':''}${f2(ch)}%</span>`,'',true)+RC('Difference',f2(t-f),'absolute',false);}else r.style.display='none';}
  if(m==='incdec'){const v=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pid-v').value),p=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pid-p').value),r=document.getElementById('pid-rc');if(!isNaN(v)&&!isNaN(p)){r.style.display='grid';r.innerHTML=RC(`+${p}% increase`,f2(v*(1+p/100)),'',true)+RC(`-${p}% decrease`,f2(v*(1-p/100)),'',false);}else r.style.display='none';}
}

// ── BMI ──
let bmiMode='metric';
function setBMIMode(m,btn){bmiMode=m;document.querySelectorAll('#p-calc-bmi .tab').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));btn.classList.add('active');document.getElementById('bmi-metric').style.display=m==='metric'?'block':'none';document.getElementById('bmi-imperial').style.display=m==='imperial'?'block':'none';calcBMI();}
function calcBMI(){
  let h,w;
  if(bmiMode==='metric'){h=parseFloat(document.getElementById('bmi-hcm').value)/100;w=parseFloat(document.getElementById('bmi-wkg').value);}
  else{const ft=parseFloat(document.getElementById('bmi-hft').value)||0,inc=parseFloat(document.getElementById('bmi-hin').value)||0;h=(ft*12+inc)*0.0254;w=parseFloat(document.getElementById('bmi-wlb').value)*0.453592;}
  if(!h||!w)return;
  const bmi=w/(h*h);
  let cat,col,pct;
  if(bmi<18.5){cat='Underweight';col='var(--teal)';pct=bmi/18.5*38;}
  else if(bmi<25){cat='Normal weight';col='var(--green)';pct=38+(bmi-18.5)/6.5*30;}
  else if(bmi<30){cat='Overweight';col='var(--amber)';pct=68+(bmi-25)/5*18;}
  else{cat='Obese';col='var(--red)';pct=Math.min(100,86+(bmi-30)/10*14);}
  document.getElementById('bmi-result').innerHTML=`<div class="rc-grid">${RC('BMI',bmi.toFixed(1),'kg/m²',true)}${RC('Category',`<span style="color:${col}">${cat}</span>`,'',false)}</div><div class="prog"><div class="prog-fill" style="width:${pct}%;background:${col}"></div></div><div style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;color:var(--ink6);margin-top:7px;display:flex;gap:14px;flex-wrap:wrap"><span style="color:var(--teal)">▪ Underweight &lt;18.5</span><span style="color:var(--green)">▪ Normal 18.5–24.9</span><span style="color:var(--amber)">▪ Overweight 25–29.9</span><span style="color:var(--red)">▪ Obese ≥30</span></div>`;
}

// ── AGE ──
function calcAge(){
  const dob=new Date(document.getElementById('age-dob').value);
  const ref=new Date(document.getElementById('age-ref').value);
  if(!dob.getTime()||!ref.getTime())return;
  let y=ref.getFullYear()-dob.getFullYear(),m=ref.getMonth()-dob.getMonth(),d=ref.getDate()-dob.getDate();
  if(d<0){m--;d+=new Date(ref.getFullYear(),ref.getMonth(),0).getDate();}
  if(m<0){y--;m+=12;}
  const td=Math.round((ref-dob)/86400000);
  const nb=new Date(ref.getFullYear(),dob.getMonth(),dob.getDate());if(nb<=ref)nb.setFullYear(ref.getFullYear()+1);
  const dl=Math.round((nb-ref)/86400000);
  document.getElementById('age-result').innerHTML=`<div class="rc-grid">${RC('Age',`${y} years`,`${m}m ${d}d`,true)}${RC('Total Days',td.toLocaleString(),'lived',false)}${RC('Total Weeks',Math.floor(td/7).toLocaleString(),'lived',false)}${RC('Next Birthday','in '+dl+' days','',false)}</div>`;
}

// ── COMPOUND INTEREST ──
function calcCI(){
  const P=parseFloat(document.getElementById('ci-p').value)||0;
  const r=parseFloat(document.getElementById('ci-r').value)/100;
  const t=parseFloat(document.getElementById('ci-t').value)||1;
  const n=parseInt(document.getElementById('ci-n').value)||12;
  const contrib=parseFloat(document.getElementById('ci-c').value)||0;
  const cur=document.getElementById('ci-cur').value;
  const A=P*Math.pow(1+r/n,n*t);
  const cf=r>0?contrib*((Math.pow(1+r/n,n*t)-1)/(r/n)):contrib*n*t;
  const total=A+(isFinite(cf)?cf:0);
  const tc=contrib*12*t, int=total-P-tc;
  document.getElementById('ci-rc').innerHTML=RC('Future Value',f2(total,cur),'after '+t+' years',true)+RC('Interest Earned',f2(int,cur),'',false)+RC('Principal',f2(P,cur),'initial',false)+RC('Contributions',f2(tc,cur),'total added',false);
}

// ── TAX ──
function calcTax(){
  const amt=parseFloat(document.getElementById('tax-amt').value)||0;
  const rate=parseFloat(document.getElementById('tax-rate').value)||0;
  const type=document.getElementById('tax-type').value;
  let pre,tax,post;
  if(type==='excl'){pre=amt;tax=amt*rate/100;post=amt+tax;}
  else{post=amt;pre=amt/(1+rate/100);tax=post-pre;}
  document.getElementById('tax-rc').innerHTML=RC('Pre-tax',f2(pre),'',false)+RC(`Tax (${rate}%)`,f2(tax),'',false)+RC('Total',f2(post),'incl. tax',true)+RC('Effective Rate',((tax/post)*100).toFixed(2)+'%','',false);
}

// ── TIP ──
function calcTip(){
  const bill=parseFloat(document.getElementById('tip-bill').value)||0;
  const pct=parseFloat(document.getElementById('tip-pct').value)||0;
  const split=Math.max(1,parseInt(document.getElementById('tip-split').value)||1);
  const tip=bill*pct/100,tot=bill+tip;
  document.getElementById('tip-rc').innerHTML=RC('Tip Amount',f2(tip),'',false)+RC('Total Bill',f2(tot),'',true)+RC('Per Person',f2(tot/split),'split '+split+'×',false)+RC('Tip Per Person',f2(tip/split),'',false);
}

// UNIT CONVERTERS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════
const UNITS={
  length:{units:['Millimeter','Centimeter','Meter','Kilometer','Inch','Foot','Yard','Mile','Nautical Mile'],base:[1e-3,1e-2,1,1e3,.0254,.3048,.9144,1609.344,1852]},
  weight:{units:['Milligram','Gram','Kilogram','Metric Ton','Ounce','Pound','Stone','US Ton'],base:[1e-6,1e-3,1,1000,.028349,.453592,6.35029,907.185]},
  temp:{units:['Celsius','Fahrenheit','Kelvin'],special:true},
  area:{units:['Sq mm','Sq cm','Sq m','Sq km','Sq inch','Sq foot','Acre','Hectare'],base:[1e-6,1e-4,1,1e6,6.452e-4,.0929,4046.86,10000]},
  volume:{units:['Milliliter','Liter','Cubic m','US Cup','US Pint','US Gallon','UK Gallon','Cubic ft'],base:[.001,1,1000,.2366,.4732,3.7854,4.5461,28.317]},
  speed:{units:['m/s','km/h','mph','Knot','Mach'],base:[1,.27778,.44704,.51444,340.29]},
  data:{units:['Bit','Byte','Kilobyte','Megabyte','Gigabyte','Terabyte','Petabyte'],base:[.125,1,1024,1048576,1073741824,1.0995e12,1.1259e15]},
  currency:{units:['USD','EUR','GBP','INR','JPY','CAD','AUD','CHF','CNY','SGD','AED'],base:[1,1.08,1.27,0.012,0.0067,.73,.65,1.12,.138,.74,.272]},
  time:{units:['Second','Minute','Hour','Day','Week','Month','Year'],base:[1,60,3600,86400,604800,2629800,31557600]},
};

function buildUnitConverter(type){
  const card=document.getElementById('uc-'+type); if(!card||card.innerHTML.trim())return;
  if(type==='number'){buildNBConverter(card);return;}
  const def=UNITS[type]; if(!def)return;
  card.innerHTML=`<div class="ctrl-row" style="align-items:flex-end">
    <div class="ctrl-g"><div class="ctrl-l">Value</div><input class="ctrl-i" type="number" id="ucv-${type}" value="1" oninput="doUC('${type}')" style="width:140px"></div>
    <div class="ctrl-g"><div class="ctrl-l">From</div><select class="ctrl-i" id="ucf-${type}" onchange="doUC('${type}')">${def.units.map(u=>`<option>${u}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
    <span style="align-self:flex-end;padding-bottom:10px;font-size:18px;color:var(--ink5)">⟶</span>
    <div class="ctrl-g"><div class="ctrl-l">To</div><select class="ctrl-i" id="uct-${type}" onchange="doUC('${type}')">${def.units.map((u,i)=>`<option${i===1?' selected':''}>${u}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
  </div>
  <div id="ucd-${type}" class="unit-display"></div>
  <hr class="div">
  <div class="sub-h">All Conversions</div>
  <div id="uca-${type}"></div>`;
  doUC(type);
}

function doUC(type){
  const val=parseFloat(document.getElementById('ucv-'+type).value); if(isNaN(val))return;
  const fromU=document.getElementById('ucf-'+type).value;
  const toU=document.getElementById('uct-'+type).value;
  const def=UNITS[type];
  let result;
  if(type==='temp'){
    const inC=fromU==='Celsius'?val:fromU==='Fahrenheit'?(val-32)*5/9:val-273.15;
    result=toU==='Celsius'?inC:toU==='Fahrenheit'?inC*9/5+32:inC+273.15;
  }else{
    const fi=def.units.indexOf(fromU),ti=def.units.indexOf(toU);
    result=val*def.base[fi]/def.base[ti];
  }
  const fv=n=>Math.abs(n)>=1e9||Math.abs(n)<1e-6&&n!==0?n.toExponential(4):parseFloat(n.toPrecision(7)).toString();
  document.getElementById('ucd-'+type).innerHTML=`<div class="unit-box"><div class="ub-v">${fv(val)}</div><div class="ub-u">${fromU}</div></div><span style="font-size:20px;color:var(--ink5)">⟹</span><div class="unit-box hi"><div class="ub-v">${fv(result)}</div><div class="ub-u">${toU}</div></div>`;
  const all=document.getElementById('uca-'+type); if(!all)return;
  let html='<div class="tbl-wrap"><div class="tbl-scroll"><table class="dtbl"><thead><tr><th>Unit</th><th>Value</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  def.units.forEach((u,i)=>{
    let r;
    if(type==='temp'){const inC=fromU==='Celsius'?val:fromU==='Fahrenheit'?(val-32)*5/9:val-273.15;r=u==='Celsius'?inC:u==='Fahrenheit'?inC*9/5+32:inC+273.15;}
    else{const fi=def.units.indexOf(fromU);r=val*def.base[fi]/def.base[i];}
    html+=`<tr><td style="color:var(--ink5)">${u}</td><td style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;color:${u===toU?'var(--red)':'var(--ink)'}">${fv(r)}</td></tr>`;
  });
  all.innerHTML=html+'</tbody></table></div></div>';
}

function buildNBConverter(card){
  card.innerHTML=`<div class="wcard-desc">Convert between Decimal, Binary, Hexadecimal and Octal.</div>
  <div class="nb-row"><span class="nb-badge">Decimal (10)</span><input class="ctrl-i" id="nb-dec" placeholder="e.g. 255" oninput="nbConv('dec')" style="flex:1"></div>
  <div class="nb-row"><span class="nb-badge">Binary (2)</span><input class="ctrl-i" id="nb-bin" placeholder="e.g. 11111111" oninput="nbConv('bin')" style="flex:1"></div>
  <div class="nb-row"><span class="nb-badge">Hex (16)</span><input class="ctrl-i" id="nb-hex" placeholder="e.g. FF" oninput="nbConv('hex')" style="flex:1"></div>
  <div class="nb-row"><span class="nb-badge">Octal (8)</span><input class="ctrl-i" id="nb-oct" placeholder="e.g. 377" oninput="nbConv('oct')" style="flex:1"></div>`;
}
function nbConv(from){
  try{
    let d;
    if(from==='dec')d=parseInt(document.getElementById('nb-dec').value)||0;
    else if(from==='bin')d=parseInt(document.getElementById('nb-bin').value,2);
    else if(from==='hex')d=parseInt(document.getElementById('nb-hex').value,16);
    else d=parseInt(document.getElementById('nb-oct').value,8);
    if(isNaN(d))return;
    if(from!=='dec')document.getElementById('nb-dec').value=d;
    if(from!=='bin')document.getElementById('nb-bin').value=d.toString(2);
    if(from!=='hex')document.getElementById('nb-hex').value=d.toString(16).toUpperCase();
    if(from!=='oct')document.getElementById('nb-oct').value=d.toString(8);
  }catch(e){}
}

// TEXT TOOLS

function calcWords(){
  const t=document.getElementById('wc-ta').value;
  const w=t.trim()?t.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length:0;
  const c=t.length, cn=t.replace(/\s/g,'').length;
  const s=t.split(/[.!?]+/).filter(x=>x.trim()).length;
  const p=t.split(/\n\s*\n/).filter(x=>x.trim()).length;
  const rt=Math.ceil(w/238);
  document.getElementById('wc-rc').innerHTML=RC('Words',w.toLocaleString(),'',true)+RC('Characters',c.toLocaleString(),'incl. spaces',false)+RC('Chars (no sp)',cn.toLocaleString(),'',false)+RC('Sentences',s.toLocaleString(),'',false)+RC('Paragraphs',Math.max(p,t.trim()?1:0),'',false)+RC('Read Time',rt+' min','at 238 wpm',false);
}

function calcCases(){
  const t=document.getElementById('case-ta').value;
  if(!t){document.getElementById('case-out').innerHTML='';return;}
  const cases={'UPPERCASE':t.toUpperCase(),'lowercase':t.toLowerCase(),'Title Case':t.replace(/\w\S*/g,w=>w[0].toUpperCase()+w.slice(1).toLowerCase()),'Sentence case':t[0].toUpperCase()+t.slice(1).toLowerCase(),'camelCase':t.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]+(.)/g,(_,c)=>c.toUpperCase()),'PascalCase':t.replace(/(^|[^a-zA-Z0-9]+)(\w)/g,(_,s,c)=>c.toUpperCase()),'snake_case':t.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]+/g,'_').replace(/^_|_$/g,''),'kebab-case':t.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]+/g,'-').replace(/^-|-$/g,''),'SCREAMING_SNAKE':t.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g,'_').replace(/^_|_$/g,'')};
  document.getElementById('case-out').innerHTML=Object.entries(cases).map(([name,val])=>`<div class="case-row"><span class="case-lbl">${name}</span><span class="case-val">${val}</span><button class="copy-btn" onclick="navigator.clipboard.writeText('${val.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">Copy</button></div>`).join('');
}

let encType='b64';
function setEncType(t,btn){encType=t;document.querySelectorAll('#p-txt-encode .tab').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));btn.classList.add('active');}
function doEnc(dir){
  const inp=document.getElementById('enc-ta').value;
  try{
    let r;
    if(encType==='b64')r=dir==='encode'?btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(inp))):decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(inp)));
    else if(encType==='url')r=dir==='encode'?encodeURIComponent(inp):decodeURIComponent(inp);
    else r=dir==='encode'?inp.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;'):inp.replace(/&amp;/g,'&').replace(/&lt;/g,'<').replace(/&gt;/g,'>').replace(/&quot;/g,'"');
    document.getElementById('enc-out').textContent=r;
  }catch(e){document.getElementById('enc-out').textContent='Error: '+e.message;}
}

async function doHashes(){
  const inp=document.getElementById('hash-ta').value; if(!inp){document.getElementById('hash-out').innerHTML='';return;}
  const enc=new TextEncoder().encode(inp);
  const h=async algo=>{const buf=await crypto.subtle.digest(algo,enc);return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');};
  const [s1,s256,s512]=await Promise.all([h('SHA-1'),h('SHA-256'),h('SHA-512')]);
  document.getElementById('hash-out').innerHTML=[['SHA-1',s1],['SHA-256',s256],['SHA-512',s512]].map(([algo,val])=>`<div class="hash-row"><div class="hash-algo">${algo}</div><div style="display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:8px"><div class="hash-val" style="flex:1">${val}</div><button class="copy-btn" onclick="navigator.clipboard.writeText('${val}');this.textContent='✓';setTimeout(()=>this.textContent='Copy',1500)">Copy</button></div></div>`).join('');
}

function doDiff(){
  const a=document.getElementById('diff-a').value.split('\n');
  const b=document.getElementById('diff-b').value.split('\n');
  const max=Math.max(a.length,b.length);
  let html='<div style="border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--r8);overflow:hidden">',add=0,rem=0;
  for(let i=0;i<max;i++){
    const la=a[i]??null,lb=b[i]??null;
    if(la===lb)html+=`<div class="diff-line diff-eq">  ${esc(la||'')}</div>`;
    else{if(la!==null){html+=`<div class="diff-line diff-rem">− ${esc(la)}</div>`;rem++;}if(lb!==null){html+=`<div class="diff-line diff-add">+ ${esc(lb)}</div>`;add++;}}
  }
  html+=`</div><div style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:9px;color:var(--ink6);margin-top:8px;display:flex;gap:14px"><span style="color:var(--green)">+ ${add} additions</span><span style="color:var(--red)">− ${rem} removals</span></div>`;
  document.getElementById('diff-out').innerHTML=html;
}

function doDedup(){
  let lines=document.getElementById('ded-ta').value.split('\n');
  if(document.getElementById('ded-trim').checked)lines=lines.map(l=>l.trim());
  if(document.getElementById('ded-blank').checked)lines=lines.filter(l=>l);
  const seen=new Set(),out=[];
  lines.forEach(l=>{if(!seen.has(l)){seen.add(l);out.push(l);}});
  if(document.getElementById('ded-sort').checked)out.sort();
  const el=document.getElementById('ded-out'); el.style.display='block'; el.textContent=out.join('\n');
}

const LW=['lorem','ipsum','dolor','sit','amet','consectetur','adipiscing','elit','sed','do','eiusmod','tempor','incididunt','ut','labore','et','dolore','magna','aliqua','enim','ad','minim','veniam','quis','nostrud','exercitation','ullamco','laboris','nisi','aliquip','ex','ea','commodo','consequat','duis','aute','irure','reprehenderit','voluptate','velit','esse','cillum','fugiat','nulla','pariatur','excepteur','sint','occaecat','cupidatat','non','proident','culpa','qui','officia','deserunt','mollit','anim','id','est','laborum','curabitur','pretium','tincidunt','lacus','nunc','purus','pulvinar','facilisis','a','pellentesque','nisl','fringilla','morbi','porta','vestibulum'];
function genLorem(){
  const n=parseInt(document.getElementById('lor-n').value)||3;
  const type=document.getElementById('lor-type').value;
  const start=document.getElementById('lor-start').value==='yes';
  const rw=()=>LW[Math.floor(Math.random()*LW.length)];
  const sent=()=>{const ws=Array.from({length:8+Math.floor(Math.random()*12)},rw);ws[0]=ws[0][0].toUpperCase()+ws[0].slice(1);return ws.join(' ')+'.';};
  const para=()=>Array.from({length:4+Math.floor(Math.random()*4)},sent).join(' ');
  let out;
  if(type==='paragraphs'){const ps=Array.from({length:n},para);if(start)ps[0]='Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet, consectetur adipiscing elit. '+ps[0];out=ps.join('\n\n');}
  else if(type==='sentences'){const ss=Array.from({length:n},sent);if(start)ss[0]='Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet, consectetur adipiscing elit.';out=ss.join(' ');}
  else{const ws=Array.from({length:n},rw);if(start){ws[0]='lorem';ws[1]='ipsum';}out=ws.join(' ');}
  document.getElementById('lor-out').textContent=out;
}

function doCopy(outId,btnId){
  const el=document.getElementById(outId); const txt=el.value||el.textContent; if(!txt.trim())return;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(()=>{
    const b=document.getElementById(btnId); if(!b)return;
    const orig=b.textContent; b.textContent='✓ Copied'; b.classList.add('done');
    setTimeout(()=>{b.textContent=orig;b.classList.remove('done');},1500);
  });
}

let recentTools = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ts_recent')||'[]');

function addRecent(toolId) {
  if(toolId==='home') return;
  recentTools = [toolId, ...recentTools.filter(t=>t!==toolId)].slice(0,6);
  try { localStorage.setItem('ts_recent', JSON.stringify(recentTools)); } catch(e) {}
  renderRecents();
}

function renderRecents() {
  const el = document.getElementById('dash-recents');
  const chips = document.getElementById('recent-chips');
  if(!el||!chips) return;
  if(!recentTools.length) { el.style.display='none'; return; }
  el.style.display='block';
  chips.innerHTML = recentTools.map(t=>`<div class="recent-chip" onclick="goTo('${t}',document.querySelector('[onclick*=\'${t}\']'))"><span class="recent-chip-icon">${TOOL_ICONS[t]||'⚡'}</span>${TOOL_NAMES[t]||t}</div>`).join('');
}

// ─── SIDEBAR PARTICLE EFFECT ───
(function(){
  const canvas = document.getElementById('sb-particles');
  if(!canvas) return;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  let W, H, dots = [];
  function resize(){ W=canvas.width=canvas.offsetWidth; H=canvas.height=canvas.offsetHeight; }
  resize(); window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
  for(let i=0;i<28;i++) dots.push({x:Math.random()*100,y:Math.random()*100,r:Math.random()*1.4+0.4,dx:(Math.random()-0.5)*0.05,dy:(Math.random()-0.5)*0.05,a:Math.random(),da:(Math.random()-0.5)*0.007});
  function frame(){
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
    dots.forEach(d=>{
      d.x+=d.dx;d.y+=d.dy;d.a+=d.da;
      if(d.x<0||d.x>100)d.dx*=-1;if(d.y<0||d.y>100)d.dy*=-1;if(d.a<0||d.a>1)d.da*=-1;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(d.x/100*W,d.y/100*H,d.r,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(163,255,79,'+d.a*0.55+')';ctx.fill();
    });
  }
  frame();
})();

// ─── CARD STAGGER ENTRANCE ───
function animateCards(){
  const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.panel.active .cat-card, .panel.active .wcard');
  cards.forEach((c,i)=>{
    c.style.opacity='0';c.style.transform='translateY(18px)';c.style.transition='none';
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      c.style.transition='opacity 0.32s ease '+(i*40)+'ms, transform 0.32s cubic-bezier(.22,.68,0,1.2) '+(i*40)+'ms';
      c.style.opacity='1';c.style.transform='translateY(0)';
    }));
  });
}
setTimeout(animateCards, 80);

// Patch goTo to trigger card animation
const _origGoToAnim = window.goTo;
if(typeof _origGoToAnim === 'function'){
  window.goTo = function(id, navEl){
    _origGoToAnim(id, navEl);
    setTimeout(animateCards, 50);
  };
}

// ─── TYPING EFFECT on hero subtitle ───
const heroLine = document.querySelector('.dash-h1-line2');
if(heroLine){
  const text = heroLine.textContent.trim();
  heroLine.textContent='';
  let i=0;
  const type = ()=>{ if(i<=text.length){ heroLine.textContent=text.slice(0,i++)+'|'; setTimeout(type, i<text.length?65:800); } else { heroLine.textContent=text; } };
  setTimeout(type, 700);
}

// ─── COUNTER ANIMATION on stats ───
document.querySelectorAll('.stat-n').forEach(el=>{
  const txt = el.textContent.trim();
  if(txt==='60+'){
    let n=0;
    const iv=setInterval(()=>{ n+=4; if(n>=64){clearInterval(iv);el.textContent='60+';} else el.textContent=n+'+'; }, 28);
    setTimeout(()=>clearInterval(iv), 2000);
  }
});

// ─── RIPPLE EFFECT on primary buttons ───
document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
  const btn = e.target.closest('.btn-primary');
  if(!btn) return;
  const ripple = document.createElement('span');
  const rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
  const size = Math.max(rect.width, rect.height) * 2;
  ripple.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;width:'+size+'px;height:'+size+'px;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.22);transform:scale(0);left:'+(e.clientX-rect.left-size/2)+'px;top:'+(e.clientY-rect.top-size/2)+'px;pointer-events:none;animation:_ripple 0.5s ease-out forwards';
  if(!document.getElementById('_ripple_style')){
    const s=document.createElement('style');s.id='_ripple_style';
    s.textContent='@keyframes _ripple{to{transform:scale(1);opacity:0}}';
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  }
  btn.style.position='relative';btn.style.overflow='hidden';
  btn.appendChild(ripple);
  setTimeout(()=>ripple.remove(), 600);
});

// ─── HOVER GLOW on section cards ───
document.querySelectorAll('.cat-card').forEach(card=>{
  card.addEventListener('mousemove', e=>{
    const r = card.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = ((e.clientX-r.left)/r.width*100).toFixed(1);
    const y = ((e.clientY-r.top)/r.height*100).toFixed(1);
    card.style.background = 'radial-gradient(circle at '+x+'% '+y+'%, rgba(163,255,79,0.06) 0%, var(--surface) 60%)';
  });
  card.addEventListener('mouseleave', ()=>{
    card.style.background = '';
  });
});

// ═══════════════════════════════════════
// SCIENTIFIC CALCULATOR
// ═══════════════════════════════════════
(function(){
  let sciDisplay = '0';
  let sciExpr = '';
  let sciMode = 'deg'; // deg | rad
  let sciMem = null;
  let sciNewNum = true;
  let sciHistory = [];
  let sciPendingOp = null;
  let sciPendingVal = null;
  let sciOpenParens = 0;
  let sciLastResult = null;

  function toRad(v){ return sciMode==='deg' ? v*Math.PI/180 : v; }
  function fromRad(v){ return sciMode==='deg' ? v*180/Math.PI : v; }

  function fact(n){
    n=Math.round(Math.abs(n));
    if(n>170)return Infinity;
    let r=1;for(let i=2;i<=n;i++)r*=i;return r;
  }

  function fmtNum(n){
    if(isNaN(n))return 'Error';
    if(!isFinite(n))return n>0?'∞':'-∞';
    if(Math.abs(n)>=1e12||( Math.abs(n)<1e-7 && n!==0))return n.toExponential(6).replace(/\.?0+e/,'e');
    let s=parseFloat(n.toPrecision(12)).toString();
    return s.length>15?parseFloat(n.toPrecision(10)).toString():s;
  }

  function updateDisplay(){
    const rd=document.getElementById('sci-result');
    const ed=document.getElementById('sci-expr');
    if(rd) rd.textContent = sciDisplay;
    if(ed) ed.textContent = sciExpr||'0';
    const ml=document.getElementById('sci-mem-label');
    if(ml) ml.textContent = sciMem!==null ? 'MEM: '+fmtNum(sciMem) : 'MEM: —';
  }

  function flashResult(){
    const r=document.getElementById('sci-result');
    if(!r)return;
    r.classList.add('flash');
    setTimeout(()=>r.classList.remove('flash'),300);
  }

  function addHistory(expr,result){
    sciHistory.unshift({expr,result});
    if(sciHistory.length>8)sciHistory.pop();
    const el=document.getElementById('sci-history');
    if(!el)return;
    el.innerHTML=sciHistory.map(h=>`<div class="sci-history-item" onclick="sciLoadHistory('${h.result}')"><span>${h.expr}</span><span style="color:rgba(163,255,79,0.6)">${h.result}</span></div>`).join('');
  }

  window.sciLoadHistory=function(val){sciDisplay=val;sciNewNum=true;updateDisplay();}
  window.sciSetMode=function(m){
    sciMode=m;
    document.getElementById('sci-deg').classList.toggle('active',m==='deg');
    document.getElementById('sci-rad').classList.toggle('active',m==='rad');
  }

  window.sciDigit=function(d){
    if(sciNewNum){
      sciDisplay=d==='.'?'0.':d;
      sciNewNum=false;
    } else {
      if(d==='.'&&sciDisplay.includes('.'))return;
      if(sciDisplay==='0'&&d!=='.') sciDisplay=d;
      else sciDisplay+=d;
    }
    updateDisplay();
  }

  window.sciOp=function(op){
    const cur=parseFloat(sciDisplay);
    let result;

    switch(op){
      // Memory
      case 'mc': sciMem=null; updateDisplay(); return;
      case 'mr': if(sciMem!==null){sciDisplay=fmtNum(sciMem);sciNewNum=true;updateDisplay();}return;
      case 'ms': sciMem=(isNaN(cur)?sciLastResult:cur)||0; updateDisplay(); return;

      // Clear
      case 'ac':
        sciDisplay='0';sciExpr='';sciNewNum=true;
        sciPendingOp=null;sciPendingVal=null;sciOpenParens=0;
        updateDisplay();return;
      case 'del':
        if(sciNewNum){sciDisplay='0';}
        else{sciDisplay=sciDisplay.slice(0,-1)||'0';}
        updateDisplay();return;

      // Unary functions
      case 'sin': result=fromRad(Math.sin(toRad(cur))); sciExpr=`sin(${cur})`; break;
      case 'cos': result=fromRad(Math.cos(toRad(cur))); sciExpr=`cos(${cur})`; break;
      case 'tan':
        if(sciMode==='deg'&&Math.abs(cur%180)===90){result=Infinity;} 
        else result=fromRad(Math.tan(toRad(cur)));
        sciExpr=`tan(${cur})`; break;
      case 'asin': result=fromRad(Math.asin(cur)); sciExpr=`asin(${cur})`; break;
      case 'acos': result=fromRad(Math.acos(cur)); sciExpr=`acos(${cur})`; break;
      case 'atan': result=fromRad(Math.atan(cur)); sciExpr=`atan(${cur})`; break;
      case 'log': result=Math.log10(cur); sciExpr=`log(${cur})`; break;
      case 'ln': result=Math.log(cur); sciExpr=`ln(${cur})`; break;
      case 'sqrt': result=Math.sqrt(cur); sciExpr=`√(${cur})`; break;
      case 'cbrt': result=Math.cbrt(cur); sciExpr=`∛(${cur})`; break;
      case 'sq': result=cur*cur; sciExpr=`(${cur})²`; break;
      case 'cube': result=cur*cur*cur; sciExpr=`(${cur})³`; break;
      case 'fact': result=fact(cur); sciExpr=`${cur}!`; break;
      case 'inv': result=1/cur; sciExpr=`1/(${cur})`; break;
      case 'abs': result=Math.abs(cur); sciExpr=`|${cur}|`; break;
      case 'sign': sciDisplay=fmtNum(-cur); sciNewNum=true; updateDisplay(); return;
      case 'percent':
        if(sciPendingVal!==null) result=(sciPendingVal*(cur/100));
        else result=cur/100;
        sciExpr=`${cur}%`; break;

      // Constants
      case 'pi': sciDisplay=fmtNum(Math.PI); sciNewNum=true; updateDisplay(); return;
      case 'e': sciDisplay=fmtNum(Math.E); sciNewNum=true; updateDisplay(); return;

      // Parens (simplified — just tracking visually)
      case '(': sciExpr+='('; sciOpenParens++; updateDisplay(); return;
      case ')': if(sciOpenParens>0){sciExpr+=')';sciOpenParens--;updateDisplay();} return;

      // Binary operators — queue up
      case 'pow':
        if(sciPendingVal!==null&&sciPendingOp) { let t=applyBinary(sciPendingVal,cur,sciPendingOp); sciPendingVal=t; } else { sciPendingVal=cur; }
        sciPendingOp='pow'; sciExpr=`${cur} ^`; sciNewNum=true; updateDisplay(); return;
      case 'mod':
        if(sciPendingVal!==null&&sciPendingOp) { let t=applyBinary(sciPendingVal,cur,sciPendingOp); sciPendingVal=t; } else { sciPendingVal=cur; }
        sciPendingOp='mod'; sciExpr=`${cur} mod`; sciNewNum=true; updateDisplay(); return;
      case 'add': case 'sub': case 'mul': case 'div':
        if(sciPendingVal!==null&&sciPendingOp&&!sciNewNum){
          result=applyBinary(sciPendingVal,cur,sciPendingOp);
          sciPendingVal=result;
          sciDisplay=fmtNum(result);
        } else { sciPendingVal=cur; }
        sciPendingOp=op;
        sciExpr=fmtNum(sciPendingVal)+' '+{add:'+',sub:'−',mul:'×',div:'÷'}[op];
        sciNewNum=true; updateDisplay(); return;

      case 'eq':
        if(sciPendingVal!==null&&sciPendingOp){
          result=applyBinary(sciPendingVal,cur,sciPendingOp);
          const histExpr=`${fmtNum(sciPendingVal)} ${{'pow':'^','mod':'mod','add':'+','sub':'−','mul':'×','div':'÷'}[sciPendingOp]||sciPendingOp} ${fmtNum(cur)}`;
          sciExpr=histExpr+' =';
          sciPendingVal=null; sciPendingOp=null;
        } else {
          if(sciExpr&&!sciExpr.endsWith('=')){
            result=cur; sciExpr+=' =';
          } else { return; }
        }
        break;

      default: return;
    }

    if(result===undefined)return;
    if(isNaN(result)||!isFinite(result)&&result!==Infinity&&result!==-Infinity){result=NaN;}
    sciLastResult=result;
    const rs=fmtNum(result);
    addHistory(sciExpr||sciDisplay, rs);
    sciDisplay=rs;
    sciNewNum=true;
    flashResult();
    updateDisplay();
  }

  function applyBinary(a,b,op){
    switch(op){
      case 'add':return a+b;
      case 'sub':return a-b;
      case 'mul':return a*b;
      case 'div':return b===0?Infinity:a/b;
      case 'pow':return Math.pow(a,b);
      case 'mod':return a%b;
      default:return b;
    }
  }

  // Keyboard support
  document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){

    const panel=document.getElementById('p-calc-sci');
    if(!panel||!panel.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const k=e.key;
    if(k>='0'&&k<='9'||k==='.')window.sciDigit(k);
    else if(k==='+')window.sciOp('add');
    else if(k==='-')window.sciOp('sub');
    else if(k==='*')window.sciOp('mul');
    else if(k==='/')window.sciOp('div');
    else if(k==='Enter'||k==='=')window.sciOp('eq');
    else if(k==='Backspace')window.sciOp('del');
    else if(k==='Escape')window.sciOp('ac');
    else if(k==='%')window.sciOp('percent');
  });

  updateDisplay();
})();


// ═══════════════════════════════════════
// SOCIAL MEDIA EXPORT + MANUAL CROP
// ═══════════════════════════════════════

  ]},
