    {name:'Story',w:1080,h:1920},
    {name:'Ad',w:1080,h:1920},
  ]},
];

let socialBitmap = null;
let socialSelectedPlatforms = new Set();
let socialExportedBlobs = [];
let socialMode = 'auto'; // 'auto' | 'manual'

// Manual crop state
let scCropState = {}; // keyed by "platformId:sizeName" → {x,y,w,h} in bitmap coords
let scCropPhase = 'idle'; // 'idle' | 'dragging' | 'done'
let scCropStart = {x:0,y:0};
let scCropScaleX = 1, scCropScaleY = 1;
let scCurrentSizeKey = null;
let scCurrentTargetW = 0, scCurrentTargetH = 0;

function setSocialMode(mode, btn) {
  socialMode = mode;
  document.querySelectorAll('#social-platforms-wrap .tab').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
  if(btn) btn.classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('social-mode-auto-note').style.display = mode==='auto' ? 'block' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('social-mode-manual-note').style.display = mode==='manual' ? 'block' : 'none';
  const cropSec = document.getElementById('social-crop-section');
  if(mode==='manual') {
    cropSec.style.display = 'block';
    buildSocialCropSizeSelector();
  } else {
    cropSec.style.display = 'none';
  }
}

function buildSocialCropSizeSelector() {
  const sel = document.getElementById('social-crop-size-sel');
  sel.innerHTML = '';
  // Add a placeholder
  const ph = document.createElement('option');
  ph.value=''; ph.textContent='— Pick a size to crop —';
  sel.appendChild(ph);
  SOCIAL_PLATFORMS.forEach(p => {
    if(!socialSelectedPlatforms.size || socialSelectedPlatforms.has(p.id)) {
      const grp = document.createElement('optgroup');
      grp.label = p.icon + ' ' + p.name;
      p.sizes.forEach(s => {
        const opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = p.id + ':' + s.name;
        opt.textContent = s.name + ' (' + s.w + '×' + s.h + ')';
        opt.dataset.w = s.w;
        opt.dataset.h = s.h;
        grp.appendChild(opt);
      });
      sel.appendChild(grp);
    }
  });
}

function onSocialCropSizeChange() {
  if(!socialBitmap) return;
  const sel = document.getElementById('social-crop-size-sel');
  const key = sel.value;
  if(!key) { document.getElementById('social-crop-dims-label').textContent = 'Pick a size above'; return; }
  const opt = sel.options[sel.selectedIndex];
  scCurrentSizeKey = key;
  scCurrentTargetW = parseInt(opt.dataset.w);
  scCurrentTargetH = parseInt(opt.dataset.h);
  document.getElementById('social-crop-target-label').textContent = 'Target: ' + scCurrentTargetW + '×' + scCurrentTargetH + 'px';
  // Draw canvas
  initSocialCropCanvas();
  // If we have a saved crop for this key, show it
  if(scCropState[key]) {
    renderSocialCropOverlay();
    renderSocialCropPreview();
  } else {
    // Default: center crop at correct aspect ratio
    socialDefaultCrop();
  }
}

function initSocialCropCanvas() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('social-crop-canvas');
  const maxW = Math.min(640, socialBitmap.width);
  const scale = maxW / socialBitmap.width;
  scCropScaleX = scale;
  scCropScaleY = scale;
  canvas.width = Math.round(socialBitmap.width * scale);
  canvas.height = Math.round(socialBitmap.height * scale);
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.drawImage(socialBitmap, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  setupSocialCropEvents(canvas);
}

function socialDefaultCrop() {
  if(!socialBitmap || !scCurrentTargetW) return;
  const bw = socialBitmap.width, bh = socialBitmap.height;
  const tw = scCurrentTargetW, th = scCurrentTargetH;
  const scale = Math.min(bw/tw, bh/th);
  const cw = Math.round(tw * scale), ch = Math.round(th * scale);
  const cx = Math.round((bw - cw) / 2), cy = Math.round((bh - ch) / 2);
  scCropState[scCurrentSizeKey] = {x:cx, y:cy, w:cw, h:ch};
  scCropPhase = 'done';
  renderSocialCropOverlay();
  renderSocialCropPreview();
}

function socialResetCrop() {
  if(scCurrentSizeKey) delete scCropState[scCurrentSizeKey];
  scCropPhase = 'idle';
  document.getElementById('social-crop-preview-wrap').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('social-crop-dims-label').textContent = 'Drag to select crop area';
  if(socialBitmap && scCurrentTargetW) initSocialCropCanvas();
}

function setupSocialCropEvents(canvas) {
  // Remove old listeners by replacing clone
  const nc = canvas.cloneNode(true);
  canvas.parentNode.replaceChild(nc, canvas);
  const c = document.getElementById('social-crop-canvas');

  function getPos(e) {
    const r = c.getBoundingClientRect();
    const cx = (e.clientX - r.left);
    const cy = (e.clientY - r.top);
    return { x: Math.round(cx / scCropScaleX), y: Math.round(cy / scCropScaleY) };
  }

  c.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
    if(!scCurrentTargetW) return;
    const p = getPos(e);
    scCropStart = p;
    scCropPhase = 'dragging';
  });

  c.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
    if(scCropPhase !== 'dragging') return;
    const p = getPos(e);
    applyAspectCrop(scCropStart, p);
    renderSocialCropOverlay();
  });

  c.addEventListener('mouseup', e => {
    if(scCropPhase !== 'dragging') return;
    const p = getPos(e);
    applyAspectCrop(scCropStart, p);
    if(!scCropState[scCurrentSizeKey] || scCropState[scCurrentSizeKey].w < 5) {
      scCropPhase = 'idle';
      return;
    }
    scCropPhase = 'done';
    renderSocialCropOverlay();
    renderSocialCropPreview();
  });

  // Touch
  c.addEventListener('touchstart', e => { e.preventDefault(); const t=e.touches[0]; c.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousedown',{clientX:t.clientX,clientY:t.clientY})); }, {passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('touchmove',  e => { e.preventDefault(); const t=e.touches[0]; c.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousemove',{clientX:t.clientX,clientY:t.clientY})); }, {passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('touchend',   e => { e.preventDefault(); c.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mouseup',{})); }, {passive:false});
}

function applyAspectCrop(start, end) {
  if(!scCurrentTargetW || !scCurrentTargetH) return;
  const bw = socialBitmap.width, bh = socialBitmap.height;
  let dx = end.x - start.x, dy = end.y - start.y;
  // Determine which dimension to lead with, lock aspect ratio
  const ratio = scCurrentTargetW / scCurrentTargetH;
  if(Math.abs(dx) / ratio > Math.abs(dy)) {
    dy = Math.round(Math.abs(dx) / ratio) * Math.sign(dy || 1);
  } else {
    dx = Math.round(Math.abs(dy) * ratio) * Math.sign(dx || 1);
  }
  let x = dx < 0 ? start.x + dx : start.x;
  let y = dy < 0 ? start.y + dy : start.y;
  let w = Math.abs(dx), h = Math.abs(dy);
  // Clamp
  x = Math.max(0, Math.min(x, bw - 1));
  y = Math.max(0, Math.min(y, bh - 1));
  w = Math.min(w, bw - x);
  h = Math.min(h, bh - y);
  if(w < 2 || h < 2) return;
  scCropState[scCurrentSizeKey] = {x, y, w, h};
}

function renderSocialCropOverlay() {
  const c = document.getElementById('social-crop-canvas');
  if(!c || !socialBitmap) return;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
  ctx.drawImage(socialBitmap, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);

  const crop = scCropState[scCurrentSizeKey];
  if(!crop) return;

  const cx = Math.round(crop.x * scCropScaleX);
  const cy = Math.round(crop.y * scCropScaleY);
  const cw = Math.round(crop.w * scCropScaleX);
  const ch = Math.round(crop.h * scCropScaleY);

  // Dim outside
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
  // Clear crop region
  ctx.clearRect(cx, cy, cw, ch);
  ctx.drawImage(socialBitmap, crop.x, crop.y, crop.w, crop.h, cx, cy, cw, ch);
  // Border
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#a3ff4f';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.strokeRect(cx+1, cy+1, cw-2, ch-2);
  // Rule-of-thirds grid
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(163,255,79,0.35)';
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  for(let i=1; i<3; i++) {
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx + cw*i/3, cy); ctx.lineTo(cx + cw*i/3, cy+ch); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx, cy + ch*i/3); ctx.lineTo(cx+cw, cy + ch*i/3); ctx.stroke();
  }
  // Corner handles
  ctx.fillStyle = '#a3ff4f';
  [[cx,cy],[cx+cw,cy],[cx,cy+ch],[cx+cw,cy+ch]].forEach(([hx,hy])=>{
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(hx, hy, 5, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
  });

  document.getElementById('social-crop-dims-label').textContent =
    `Crop: ${crop.w}×${crop.h}px  →  exports as ${scCurrentTargetW}×${scCurrentTargetH}px`;
}

function renderSocialCropPreview() {
  const crop = scCropState[scCurrentSizeKey];
  if(!crop || !socialBitmap) return;
  const prev = document.getElementById('social-crop-preview-canvas');
  // Display at a reasonable preview size
  const maxPrevW = 220, maxPrevH = 160;
  const scl = Math.min(maxPrevW/crop.w, maxPrevH/crop.h, 1);
  prev.width = Math.round(crop.w * scl);
  prev.height = Math.round(crop.h * scl);
  prev.getContext('2d').drawImage(socialBitmap, crop.x, crop.y, crop.w, crop.h, 0, 0, prev.width, prev.height);
  document.getElementById('social-crop-preview-dims').textContent = scCurrentTargetW + '×' + scCurrentTargetH + 'px';
  document.getElementById('social-crop-preview-wrap').style.display = 'block';
}

async function imgLoadSocial(files){
  if(!files||!files[0])return;
  socialBitmap = await createImageBitmap(files[0]);
  const previewWrap = document.getElementById('social-preview-img-wrap');
  const srcImg = document.getElementById('social-source-img');
  const srcInfo = document.getElementById('social-source-info');
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(files[0]);
  srcImg.src = url;
  srcImg.onload = () => URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  srcInfo.textContent = `${files[0].name} · ${socialBitmap.width}×${socialBitmap.height}px · ${fz(files[0].size)}`;
  previewWrap.style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('social-platforms-wrap').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('social-results-wrap').style.display = 'none';
  socialExportedBlobs = [];
  scCropState = {};
  renderSocialPlatforms();
  // If in manual mode, refresh crop canvas
  if(socialMode === 'manual') {
    buildSocialCropSizeSelector();
    document.getElementById('social-crop-section').style.display = 'block';
  }
}

function renderSocialPlatforms(){
  const grid = document.getElementById('social-platform-grid');
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  SOCIAL_PLATFORMS.forEach(p=>{
    const card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'social-platform-card' + (socialSelectedPlatforms.has(p.id)?' selected':'');
    card.dataset.id = p.id;
    const sizeList = p.sizes.map(s=>`${s.name}: ${s.w}×${s.h}`).join('<br>');
    card.innerHTML=`
      <div class="social-platform-card-check">${socialSelectedPlatforms.has(p.id)?'✓':''}</div>
      <div class="social-platform-icon">${p.icon}</div>
      <div class="social-platform-name">${p.name}</div>
      <div class="social-platform-sizes">${sizeList}</div>`;
    card.onclick=()=>{
      if(socialSelectedPlatforms.has(p.id)) socialSelectedPlatforms.delete(p.id);
      else socialSelectedPlatforms.add(p.id);
      renderSocialPlatforms();
      if(socialMode==='manual') buildSocialCropSizeSelector();
    };
    grid.appendChild(card);
  });
}

function socialSelectAll(){ SOCIAL_PLATFORMS.forEach(p=>socialSelectedPlatforms.add(p.id)); renderSocialPlatforms(); if(socialMode==='manual') buildSocialCropSizeSelector(); }
function socialClearAll(){ socialSelectedPlatforms.clear(); renderSocialPlatforms(); document.getElementById('social-results-wrap').style.display='none'; if(socialMode==='manual') buildSocialCropSizeSelector(); }

// Smart center-crop canvas (auto-fit mode)
function smartCropCanvas(bitmap, tw, th, manualCrop){
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = tw; canvas.height = th;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.fillStyle='#000'; ctx.fillRect(0,0,tw,th);
  if(manualCrop) {
    // Use the manual crop region, scale to target
    ctx.drawImage(bitmap, manualCrop.x, manualCrop.y, manualCrop.w, manualCrop.h, 0, 0, tw, th);
  } else {
    const bw=bitmap.width, bh=bitmap.height;
    const scale = Math.max(tw/bw, th/bh);
    const sw=bw*scale, sh=bh*scale;
    const dx=(tw-sw)/2, dy=(th-sh)/2;
    ctx.drawImage(bitmap, dx, dy, sw, sh);
  }
  return canvas;
}

async function doSocialExport(){
  if(!socialBitmap){ setStatus('social','err','Please upload an image first.'); return; }
  if(socialSelectedPlatforms.size===0){ setStatus('social','err','Please select at least one platform.'); return; }
  setStatus('social','loading','Exporting…');
  const resultWrap = document.getElementById('social-export-result');
  resultWrap.innerHTML='';
  socialExportedBlobs=[];
  const dlAllBtn = document.getElementById('btn-social-dl-all');

  const selectedPlatforms = SOCIAL_PLATFORMS.filter(p=>socialSelectedPlatforms.has(p.id));
  let count=0;
  for(const platform of selectedPlatforms){
    for(const size of platform.sizes){
      const sizeKey = platform.id + ':' + size.name;
      const manualCrop = (socialMode==='manual' && scCropState[sizeKey]) ? scCropState[sizeKey] : null;
      const canvas = smartCropCanvas(socialBitmap, size.w, size.h, manualCrop);
      const blob = await new Promise(res=>canvas.toBlob(res,'image/jpeg',0.92));
      const u8 = await blobU8(blob);
      const fname = `${platform.id}_${size.name.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi,'_').toLowerCase()}_${size.w}x${size.h}.jpg`;
      socialExportedBlobs.push({blob,fname,platform,size});

      const item = document.createElement('div');
      item.className='social-export-item';
      const thumbWrap = document.createElement('div');
      thumbWrap.className='social-export-thumb';
      const thumbCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      thumbCanvas.width=48; thumbCanvas.height=48;
      thumbCanvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(canvas,0,0,size.w,size.h,0,0,48,48);
      thumbCanvas.style.cssText='width:48px;height:48px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:var(--r6);';
      thumbWrap.appendChild(thumbCanvas);
      const info = document.createElement('div');
      info.className='social-export-info';
      const cropTag = (socialMode==='manual' && manualCrop) ? ' <span style="font-size:8px;color:var(--accent);background:rgba(163,255,79,0.1);border:1px solid rgba(163,255,79,0.2);border-radius:3px;padding:1px 5px;">✂ custom crop</span>' : '';
      info.innerHTML=`<div class="social-export-platform">${platform.icon} ${platform.name}</div><div class="social-export-size-name">${size.name}${cropTag}</div><div class="social-export-dims">${size.w}×${size.h}px</div>`;
      const dlBtn = document.createElement('a');
      dlBtn.className='social-export-dl';
      dlBtn.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      dlBtn.download=fname;
      dlBtn.innerHTML='⬇ Save';
      item.appendChild(thumbWrap); item.appendChild(info); item.appendChild(dlBtn);
      resultWrap.appendChild(item);
      count++;
    }
  }
  document.getElementById('social-results-wrap').style.display='block';
  dlAllBtn.style.display = count>1 ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
  setStatus('social','ok',`✓ ${count} image(s) ready for download`);
}

async function doSocialDownloadAll(){
  if(!socialExportedBlobs.length)return;
  setStatus('social','loading','Creating ZIP…');
  try{
    const zip=new JSZip();
    for(const {blob,fname} of socialExportedBlobs){
      zip.file(fname, await blob.arrayBuffer());
    }
    const zipBlob=await zip.generateAsync({type:'blob'});
    const a=document.createElement('a');
    a.href=URL.createObjectURL(zipBlob);
    a.download='social_media_exports.zip';
    a.click();
    setStatus('social','ok','✓ ZIP downloaded');
  }catch(e){setStatus('social','err','Error: '+e.message);}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════
// AUTH SYSTEM (localStorage-based demo)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════
let currentUser = null;

function authInit() {
  const stored = localStorage.getItem('ts_user');
  if(stored) {
    try { currentUser = JSON.parse(stored); updateAuthUI(); } catch(e) {}
  }
  renderReviews();
}

function openLoginModal() {
  closeModals();
  document.getElementById('login-modal').style.display='flex';
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('login-email').focus(),100);
}
function openSignupModal() {
  closeModals();
  document.getElementById('signup-modal').style.display='flex';
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('signup-name').focus(),100);
}
function closeModals() {
  document.getElementById('login-modal').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('signup-modal').style.display='none';
}

function doLogin() {
  const email = document.getElementById('login-email').value.trim();
  const pass  = document.getElementById('login-pass').value;
  const errEl = document.getElementById('login-error');
  errEl.style.display='none';
  if(!email || !pass) { errEl.textContent='Please fill in all fields.'; errEl.style.display='block'; return; }
  // Load accounts from localStorage
  const accounts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ts_accounts')||'{}');
  const user = accounts[email.toLowerCase()];
  if(!user || user.pass !== btoa(pass)) {
    errEl.textContent='Invalid email or password. Try signing up.'; errEl.style.display='block'; return;
  }
  currentUser = {name: user.name, email: email.toLowerCase(), joined: user.joined};
  localStorage.setItem('ts_user', JSON.stringify(currentUser));
  closeModals();
  updateAuthUI();
}

function doSignup() {
  const name  = document.getElementById('signup-name').value.trim();
  const email = document.getElementById('signup-email').value.trim();
  const pass  = document.getElementById('signup-pass').value;
  const errEl = document.getElementById('signup-error');
  const sucEl = document.getElementById('signup-success');
  errEl.style.display='none'; sucEl.style.display='none';
  if(!name||!email||!pass) { errEl.textContent='Please fill in all fields.'; errEl.style.display='block'; return; }
  if(pass.length < 8) { errEl.textContent='Password must be at least 8 characters.'; errEl.style.display='block'; return; }
  if(!email.includes('@')) { errEl.textContent='Please enter a valid email.'; errEl.style.display='block'; return; }
  const accounts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ts_accounts')||'{}');
  if(accounts[email.toLowerCase()]) { errEl.textContent='This email is already registered.'; errEl.style.display='block'; return; }
  accounts[email.toLowerCase()] = {name, pass: btoa(pass), joined: new Date().toISOString()};
  localStorage.setItem('ts_accounts', JSON.stringify(accounts));
  currentUser = {name, email: email.toLowerCase(), joined: new Date().toISOString()};
  localStorage.setItem('ts_user', JSON.stringify(currentUser));
  sucEl.textContent='Account created! Welcome to ToolSuite 🎉'; sucEl.style.display='block';
  setTimeout(()=>{ closeModals(); updateAuthUI(); }, 1400);
}

function signOut() {
  currentUser = null;
  localStorage.removeItem('ts_user');
  updateAuthUI();
  goTo('home', document.querySelector('[onclick*="goTo(\'home\'"]'));
}

function toggleUserMenu() {
  const m = document.getElementById('user-menu');
  m.style.display = m.style.display==='none' ? 'block' : 'none';
}

document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('user-avatar-wrap');
  if(wrap && !wrap.contains(e.target)) {
    const m = document.getElementById('user-menu');
    if(m) m.style.display='none';
  }
});

function updateAuthUI() {
  const authArea = document.getElementById('auth-area');
  const avatarWrap = document.getElementById('user-avatar-wrap');
  const sideAcct = document.getElementById('sidebar-account-item');
  const sideDl   = document.getElementById('sidebar-downloads-item');
  if(currentUser) {
    authArea.style.display='none';
    avatarWrap.style.display='flex';
    const initials = currentUser.name.split(' ').map(w=>w[0]).join('').toUpperCase().slice(0,2);
    document.getElementById('user-initials').textContent = initials;
    document.getElementById('user-menu-name').textContent = currentUser.name;
    document.getElementById('user-menu-email').textContent = currentUser.email;
    if(sideAcct) sideAcct.style.display='flex';
    if(sideDl)   sideDl.style.display='flex';
    // Update account panel
    const big = document.getElementById('acct-avatar-big');
    if(big) big.textContent = initials;
    const dn = document.getElementById('acct-display-name');
    if(dn) dn.textContent = currentUser.name;
    const de = document.getElementById('acct-display-email');
    if(de) de.textContent = currentUser.email;
    const ni = document.getElementById('acct-name-input');
    if(ni) ni.value = currentUser.name;
    const ei = document.getElementById('acct-email-input');
    if(ei) ei.value = currentUser.email;
    // Stats
    const toolsUsed = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('ts_stat_tools')||'0');
    const filesProc = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('ts_stat_files')||'0');
    const joined = new Date(currentUser.joined);
    const days = Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now()-joined)/86400000));
    const sn = document.getElementById('acct-stat-tools'); if(sn) sn.textContent = toolsUsed;
    const sf = document.getElementById('acct-stat-files'); if(sf) sf.textContent = filesProc;
    const sd = document.getElementById('acct-stat-days'); if(sd) sd.textContent = days;
    // Show/hide account content vs guest msg
    const ac = document.getElementById('account-content'); if(ac) ac.style.display='block';
    const ag = document.getElementById('account-guest-msg'); if(ag) ag.style.display='none';
    const dc = document.getElementById('downloads-content'); if(dc) dc.style.display='block';
    const dg = document.getElementById('downloads-guest-msg'); if(dg) dg.style.display='none';
    renderDownloadsList();
  } else {
    authArea.style.display='flex';
    avatarWrap.style.display='none';
    if(sideAcct) sideAcct.style.display='none';
    if(sideDl)   sideDl.style.display='none';
    const ac = document.getElementById('account-content'); if(ac) ac.style.display='none';
    const ag = document.getElementById('account-guest-msg'); if(ag) ag.style.display='block';
    const dc = document.getElementById('downloads-content'); if(dc) dc.style.display='none';
    const dg = document.getElementById('downloads-guest-msg'); if(dg) dg.style.display='block';
  }
}

function saveAccountSettings() {
  if(!currentUser) return;
  const newName = document.getElementById('acct-name-input').value.trim();
  if(!newName) return;
  currentUser.name = newName;
  localStorage.setItem('ts_user', JSON.stringify(currentUser));
  // Update accounts store
  const accounts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ts_accounts')||'{}');
  if(accounts[currentUser.email]) { accounts[currentUser.email].name = newName; localStorage.setItem('ts_accounts', JSON.stringify(accounts)); }
  updateAuthUI();
  setStatus('home','ok','✓ Settings saved');
}

let reviewRating = 0;

function handleStarPick(e) {
  const s = e.target.closest('span');
  if(!s) return;
  reviewRating = parseInt(s.dataset.val);
  document.querySelectorAll('#star-picker span').forEach(sp => {
    sp.classList.toggle('active', parseInt(sp.dataset.val) <= reviewRating);
  });
}

function submitReview() {
  const name = document.getElementById('rv-name').value.trim();
  const text = document.getElementById('rv-text').value.trim();
  const tool = document.getElementById('rv-tool').value;
  const errEl = document.getElementById('rv-error');
  const sucEl = document.getElementById('rv-success');
  errEl.style.display='none'; sucEl.style.display='none';
  if(!name) { errEl.textContent='Please enter your name.'; errEl.style.display='block'; return; }
  if(reviewRating === 0) { errEl.textContent='Please select a star rating.'; errEl.style.display='block'; return; }
  if(!text || text.length < 15) { errEl.textContent='Please write at least 15 characters.'; errEl.style.display='block'; return; }
  // Save review
  const stored = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ts_reviews')||'[]');
  const initials = name.split(' ').map(w=>w[0]).join('').toUpperCase().slice(0,2);
  const colors = ['linear-gradient(135deg,#8b5cf6,#a3ff4f)','linear-gradient(135deg,#f472b6,#22d3ee)','linear-gradient(135deg,#22d3ee,#fbbf24)','linear-gradient(135deg,#4ade80,#a78bfa)'];
  stored.unshift({name,tool,stars:reviewRating,text,date:'Now',avatar:initials,color:colors[Math.floor(Math.random()*colors.length)]});
  localStorage.setItem('ts_reviews', JSON.stringify(stored.slice(0,50)));
  sucEl.textContent='Thank you for your review! 🎉'; sucEl.style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('rv-name').value='';
  document.getElementById('rv-text').value='';
  reviewRating=0;
  document.querySelectorAll('#star-picker span').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('active'));
  renderReviews();
}

function renderReviews() {
  const grid = document.getElementById('reviews-grid');
  if(!grid) return;
  const userReviews = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ts_reviews')||'[]');
  const all = [...userReviews, ...SEED_REVIEWS].slice(0,9);
  const total = 1247 + userReviews.length;
  const countEl = document.getElementById('reviews-total-count');
  if(countEl) countEl.textContent = `Based on ${total.toLocaleString()} reviews`;
  grid.innerHTML = all.map(r => `
    <div class="review-card">
      <div class="review-card-top">
        <div class="review-avatar" style="background:${r.color}">${r.avatar}</div>
        <div class="review-meta">
          <div class="review-name">${r.name}</div>
          <div class="review-date">${r.date}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="review-stars">${'★'.repeat(r.stars)}${'☆'.repeat(5-r.stars)}</div>
      <div class="review-text">${r.text}</div>
      <span class="review-tool-tag">${r.tool}</span>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════
// FIXED SOCIAL CROP — pan only (no resize)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════
// Override setupSocialCropEvents to implement "pan only" behavior
// The crop box is pre-sized to exact aspect ratio; user only moves it
const _origInitSocialCropCanvas = initSocialCropCanvas;
function initSocialCropCanvas() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('social-crop-canvas');
  const maxW = Math.min(640, socialBitmap.width);
  const scale = maxW / socialBitmap.width;
  scCropScaleX = scale;
  scCropScaleY = scale;
  canvas.width  = Math.round(socialBitmap.width  * scale);
  canvas.height = Math.round(socialBitmap.height * scale);
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.drawImage(socialBitmap, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  setupSocialCropPanOnly(canvas);
  // Auto-set the default crop box
  socialDefaultCrop();
}

function setupSocialCropPanOnly(canvas) {
  const nc = canvas.cloneNode(true);
  canvas.parentNode.replaceChild(nc, canvas);
  const c = document.getElementById('social-crop-canvas');
  c.style.cursor = 'grab';

  // Show hint
  let hint = document.getElementById('social-pan-hint');
  if(!hint) {
    hint = document.createElement('div');
    hint.id = 'social-pan-hint';
    hint.className = 'social-crop-move-hint';
    hint.textContent = '↔ Drag to reposition — frame is pre-sized to exact platform dimensions';
    c.parentNode.insertBefore(hint, c);
  }

  let dragging = false, startX = 0, startY = 0, startCropX = 0, startCropY = 0;

  function getPos(e) {
    const r = c.getBoundingClientRect();
    return { x: (e.clientX - r.left) / scCropScaleX, y: (e.clientY - r.top) / scCropScaleY };
  }

  function onDown(e) {
    if(!scCurrentTargetW) return;
    const p = getPos(e);
    dragging = true;
    startX = p.x; startY = p.y;
    const crop = scCropState[scCurrentSizeKey];
    startCropX = crop ? crop.x : 0;
    startCropY = crop ? crop.y : 0;
    c.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
  }
  function onMove(e) {
    if(!dragging) return;
    const p = getPos(e);
    const dx = p.x - startX, dy = p.y - startY;
    const crop = scCropState[scCurrentSizeKey];
    if(!crop) return;
    const bw = socialBitmap.width, bh = socialBitmap.height;
    crop.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(startCropX + dx, bw - crop.w));
    crop.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(startCropY + dy, bh - crop.h));
    renderSocialCropOverlay();
    renderSocialCropPreview();
  }
  function onUp() {
    dragging = false;
    c.style.cursor = 'grab';
  }

  c.addEventListener('mousedown', onDown);
  c.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
  c.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
  c.addEventListener('mouseleave', onUp);
  c.addEventListener('touchstart', e=>{e.preventDefault();onDown(e.touches[0]);},{passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('touchmove',  e=>{e.preventDefault();onMove(e.touches[0]);},{passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('touchend',   e=>{e.preventDefault();onUp();},{passive:false});
}

// Initialize on page load
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', authInit);
// Also call immediately in case DOM is ready
if(document.readyState!=='loading') authInit();

</script>
